## [M] src/app/core/models/wwell-planning/integrated-requirement.model.ts

```ts
import { IrAttachment } from './ir-attachment.model';
import { ApprovalChainStep } from './wwell-planning.model';

export type IntegratedRequirementStatus = 'draft' | 'in-progress' | 'submit-for-review' | 'in-review' | 'approved';
export type IntegratedRequirementTestType = 'flow-test' | 'pump-test';

export interface IntegratedRequirementRequestReference {
  readonly linkedRequestId: string | null;
  /** Correspondence/letter reference tied to this request — optional since older records predate the field. */
  readonly letterNumber?: string | null;
  readonly client: string;
  readonly supportingBusiness: string;
  readonly wellName: string;
  readonly targetAquifer: string;
  readonly targetDepthFt: number;
  readonly surfaceElevationFtAsl: number | null;
  readonly coordinates: string;
}

export interface IntegratedRequirementFormationTop {
  readonly name: string;
  readonly depthFt: number;
  /** Optional since older records predate this field. */
  readonly subSeaFtAsl?: number | null;
}

export interface IntegratedRequirementCasingRow {
  readonly type: string;
  readonly sizeIn: string;
  readonly depthFtBgl: number;
  /** Top-of-liner depth — only meaningful when `type === 'Liner'`. */
  readonly tolFtBgl: number | null;
  readonly remarks: string | null;
}

export interface IntegratedRequirementDevelopmentTesting {
  readonly logging: string;
  readonly mudLogging: string;
  readonly sampling: string;
  readonly flowTest: string;
  /** General Development/Testing remarks (exad_gwd_ir_header.dev_pump_test) — a catch-all note for
   *  the whole Development/Testing group, distinct from logging/mudLogging/sampling/flowTest's own
   *  per-topic remarks. */
  readonly remarks: string;
}

/**
 * exad_gwd_well_design's own completion-element columns (OH_FLG/OH_REMARKS, GP_FLG/GP_REMARKS,
 * PERFORATED_FLG/PERFORATED_REMARKS, LS_FLG/LS_REMARKS) — derived one-way from `completion`/
 * `openHoleSizeIn` (see deriveCompletionElementFlags in wwell-ir-wellbore-diagram.mapper.ts) and
 * persisted through update_ir_data_prc's exad_gwd_well_design MERGE on every save, so the real
 * backend table — not just this app's own `completion` text — carries the flags. `completion`
 * stays the one field the UI actually edits/displays; this is the round-tripped mirror of what
 * the save wrote, read back from a GET's own exad_gwd_well_design block.
 */
export interface IntegratedRequirementWellDesignFlags {
  readonly ohFlg: 'Y' | 'N';
  readonly ohRemarks: string | null;
  readonly gpFlg: 'Y' | 'N';
  readonly gpRemarks: string | null;
  readonly perforatedFlg: 'Y' | 'N';
  readonly perforatedRemarks: string | null;
  readonly lsFlg: 'Y' | 'N';
  readonly lsRemarks: string | null;
}

export interface IntegratedRequirementProgramDetails {
  readonly completion: string;
  /** Bit size below the last casing shoe (e.g. `8 1/2"`) — only meaningful when completion is 'Open Hole'. */
  readonly openHoleSizeIn: string | null;
  readonly acidization: string;
  /** exad_gwd_ir_header.dt_rmks — not trns_type (VARCHAR2(10), too short for these labels). */
  readonly transmission: string;
  readonly developmentTesting: IntegratedRequirementDevelopmentTesting;
  /** `undefined` until a detail fetch resolves one (a freshly-built local draft has none yet),
   *  `null` when that fetch found no exad_gwd_well_design row at all — see
   *  IntegratedRequirementWellDesignFlags' own doc comment. */
  readonly wellDesignFlags?: IntegratedRequirementWellDesignFlags | null;
}

export interface IntegratedRequirementContacts {
  readonly geologistPrimary: string;
  readonly geologistSecondary: string | null;
}

export interface IntegratedRequirementOffsetWell {
  /** exad_gwd_ir_water.ep_a_num — the offset well's numeric id, and (with ir_seq) the natural key
   *  the save proc matches rows on. That table has no well-name column: production identifies the
   *  well numerically and resolves its display name elsewhere. See docs/ir/p2-ir.md. */
  readonly epANum: number;
  /** Everything below is historical test-result data — usually enriched after creation, not captured at intake. */
  readonly distanceKm?: number | null;
  readonly location?: string | null;
  readonly aquifer?: string | null;
  readonly totalDepthFt?: number | null;
  readonly testDurationMin?: number | null;
  readonly rateGpm?: number | null;
  readonly rpm?: number | null;
  readonly swlFt?: number | null;
  readonly pwlFt?: number | null;
  readonly drawdownFt?: number | null;
  readonly specificCapacity?: number | null;
  readonly tdsPpm?: number | null;
  readonly h2sPpm?: number | null;
}

export interface IntegratedRequirementHydrogeology {
  readonly aquifer: string;
  readonly estimatedTdsPpm: number | null;
  readonly h2sPpm: number | null;
  readonly estimatedPumpDepthFt: number | null;
}

export interface IntegratedRequirementTestProgram {
  readonly testType: IntegratedRequirementTestType;
  readonly estimatedRateGpm: number | null;
  readonly estimatedWaterLevelFt: number | null;
  /** Shut-in wellhead pressure — only meaningful for a flow test. */
  readonly siwhpPsi?: number | null;
}

export interface IntegratedRequirement {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly code: string;
  /** Backend-assigned exad_gwd_ir_header.ir_seq — absent until the first successful save. */
  readonly irSeq?: number | null;
  /** Backend-assigned exad_gwd_well_design.wd_seq for this well's design record — that table is
   *  keyed by wd_seq/prewap_seq, not ir_seq (see docs/ir/p2-ir.md), so it can't be derived from
   *  irSeq. Known only once a detail fetch has resolved one; threaded through purely so a later
   *  save of Estimated Pump Depth UPDATEs that same row instead of INSERTing a duplicate. */
  readonly wellDesignSeq?: number | null;
  readonly updatedDate: string;
  readonly status: IntegratedRequirementStatus;
  readonly requestReference: IntegratedRequirementRequestReference;
  readonly formationTops: readonly IntegratedRequirementFormationTop[];
  /** Single free-text note applying to all Formation Tops rows together (exad_gwd_ir_header.tops_remarks
   *  — a single column, not one per row). Mirrors `offsetWellsRemarks` below. */
  readonly topsRemarks: string | null;
  readonly casing: readonly IntegratedRequirementCasingRow[];
  readonly programDetails: IntegratedRequirementProgramDetails;
  readonly contacts: IntegratedRequirementContacts;
  readonly offsetWells: readonly IntegratedRequirementOffsetWell[];
  readonly offsetWellsRemarks: string | null;
  readonly hydrogeology: IntegratedRequirementHydrogeology;
  readonly testProgram: IntegratedRequirementTestProgram;
  /** Real stored files (Location Map / Initiation Request), loaded per ir_seq by its own endpoint
   *  — like `chain` below, not part of the detail blocks. Empty until loadIrAttachments resolves;
   *  a slot the user hasn't filled is simply absent from the list. */
  readonly attachments: readonly IrAttachment[];
  readonly chain: readonly ApprovalChainStep[];
  /** exad_gwd_ir_header.sap_wf_id — the raw workflow id minted by Submit for Review (see
   *  wwell-ir-sap.config.ts) and the `wf_obj_id` the SAP display GET is looked up by. `null` until
   *  a detail fetch resolves one (a never-submitted IR has no SAP workflow to look up). */
  readonly sapWfId: number | null;
  /** SAP `/display` header.wf_status_text (see SapWorkflowHeader) for this record's sapWfId —
   *  the actual current workflow status text, refreshed alongside `chain` by
   *  WwellIrStore.loadIrApprovalTimeline. `null` until that first resolves (never-submitted IR,
   *  or a submit whose follow-up display fetch hasn't landed yet), in which case the list falls
   *  back to the local status label instead of showing nothing. */
  readonly sapWfStatusText: string | null;
  /** True while the stored header remarks (completion, acidization, …) still carry legacy HTML. The
   *  fields above already hold the cleaned text, so a Save has to write it back even when the user
   *  edited nothing — otherwise the markup stays in the database. Recomputed by every detail fetch,
   *  so it clears itself once a save has stored the plain text. */
  readonly hasLegacyMarkup?: boolean;
}

export interface CreateIntegratedRequirementPayload
  extends Pick<
    IntegratedRequirement,
    | 'requestReference'
    | 'formationTops'
    | 'topsRemarks'
    | 'casing'
    | 'programDetails'
    | 'contacts'
    | 'offsetWells'
    | 'offsetWellsRemarks'
    | 'hydrogeology'
    | 'testProgram'
  > {
  readonly status: Extract<IntegratedRequirementStatus, 'draft' | 'in-progress'>;
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/morning-report/morning-report.component.scss

```scss

:host {
  /* ── mr.html color tokens ─────────────────────────────────── */
  --mr-navy:          #1f3864;
  --mr-navy-dark:     #003366;
  --mr-label-bg:      #ddeaf7;
  --mr-label-text:    #003d99;
  --mr-well-bg:       #dde9f5;
  --mr-well-text:     #874920;
  --mr-tbl-hdr-bg:    #d0cece;
  --mr-tbl-hdr-text:  #8b0000;
  --mr-value-text:    #111827;
  --mr-border:        #c0c0c0;
  --mr-missing:       #cc0000;

  display: block;
  padding: 2rem 1rem 3rem;
  background: var(--bg-app);
}

/* ── Outer shell ─────────────────────────────────────────────── */
.report-shell {
  max-width: 1080px;
  margin: 0 auto;
  padding: 0 0 2rem;
  border: 1px solid var(--mr-border);
  border-radius: 12px;
  background: #fff;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 24px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.1), 0 1px 4px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.06);
  overflow: hidden;
  color: var(--mr-value-text);
}

/* ── Title block — exact mr.html banner ─────────────────────── */
.report-title-block {
  padding: 18px 24px 20px;
  background: #fff;
  border-left: 5px solid var(--mr-navy-dark);
  margin-bottom: 2px;
  text-align: center;
}

.report-title-main {
  font-size: 20px;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: var(--mr-navy-dark);
  margin: 0 0 2px;
  line-height: 1.2;
}

.report-title-sub {
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: #1a2e4a;
  margin: 0 0 3px;
  line-height: 1.3;
}

.report-title-date {
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 800;
  color: #6b7280;
  margin: 0;
}

/* ── Toolbar ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
.report-toolbar {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 1rem;
  justify-content: space-between;
  padding: 0.75rem 1.5rem;
  background: #f9fafb;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--mr-border);
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--mr-border);
}

.date-picker-section {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.date-picker-label {
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: var(--mr-navy);
  white-space: nowrap;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}

.date-input-wrapper {
  position: relative;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
}

.date-picker-input {
  padding: 6px 34px 6px 12px;
  border: 1.5px solid var(--mr-border);
  border-radius: 6px;
  background: #fff;
  color: var(--mr-value-text);
  font-family: inherit;
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 600;
  cursor: text;
  outline: none;
  min-width: 122px;
  width: 122px;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  transition: border-color 0.2s ease, box-shadow 0.2s ease;
  appearance: none;

  &:hover {
    border-color: var(--mr-navy);
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(31, 56, 100, 0.08);
  }

  &:focus {
    border-color: var(--mr-navy);
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(31, 56, 100, 0.12);
  }
}

.date-cal-btn {
  position: absolute;
  right: 6px;
  top: 50%;
  transform: translateY(-50%);
  width: 22px;
  height: 22px;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  background: transparent;
  border: none;
  border-radius: 4px;
  cursor: pointer;
  color: var(--mr-navy);
  padding: 0;
  transition: color 0.15s ease, background 0.15s ease;

  svg { width: 13px; height: 13px; }

  &:hover {
    color: var(--mr-navy-dark);
    background: var(--mr-label-bg);
  }
}

.date-native-hidden {
  position: absolute;
  right: 0;
  top: 0;
  width: 28px;
  height: 100%;
  opacity: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
  border: none;
  background: transparent;
  padding: 0;
  margin: 0;
  appearance: none;
  -webkit-appearance: none;
}

/* ── Divider bar — mr.html .divider ─────────────────────────── */
.report-divider {
  width: 100%;
  height: 6px;
  background-color: var(--mr-navy);
  margin: 0;
}

/* ── Loading bar ─────────────────────────────────────────────── */
.report-loading-bar {
  position: fixed;
  top: var(--shell-bar-h, 62px); // sits directly under the top bar, whichever shell is showing
  left: 0;
  right: 0;
  height: 3px;
  z-index: 99;
  --mdc-linear-progress-track-height: 3px;
  --mdc-linear-progress-active-indicator-height: 3px;
  --mdc-linear-progress-track-color: transparent;
  --mdc-linear-progress-active-indicator-color: transparent;
}

:host ::ng-deep .report-loading-bar .mdc-linear-progress__bar-inner {
  border-top: none !important;
  height: 4px;
  background: linear-gradient(to right, var(--mr-navy), var(--mr-label-text));
}

/* ── Email button ────────────────────────────────────────────── */
.email-btn {
  position: relative;
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.55rem;
  padding: 0.55rem 1.1rem 0.55rem 0.85rem;
  border: 1.5px solid rgba(31, 56, 100, 0.35);
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: #fff;
  color: var(--mr-navy);
  font-size: 0.82rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  cursor: pointer;
  overflow: hidden;
  box-shadow: 0 1px 4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
  transition: background 180ms ease, border-color 180ms ease, color 180ms ease, box-shadow 180ms ease;

  &:hover {
    background: var(--mr-label-bg);
    border-color: var(--mr-navy);
    color: var(--mr-navy-dark);
    box-shadow: 0 2px 8px rgba(31, 56, 100, 0.18);

    .email-btn__arrow { transform: translateX(3px); }
  }

  &:active { transform: scale(0.97); }
}

.email-btn__icon,
.email-btn__arrow {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.email-btn__icon svg { width: 15px; height: 15px; }

.email-btn__label { white-space: nowrap; }

.email-btn__arrow {
  opacity: 0.7;
  transition: transform 180ms ease;
  svg { width: 13px; height: 13px; }
}

.email-btn--sending {
  opacity: 0.75;
  cursor: not-allowed;
  pointer-events: none;
}

.email-btn__spinner {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  flex-shrink: 0;

  svg {
    width: 15px;
    height: 15px;
    animation: spin 0.8s linear infinite;
  }
}

@keyframes spin {
  to { transform: rotate(360deg); }
}

/* ── Content area ────────────────────────────────────────────── */
.report-content {
  padding: 1.25rem 1.25rem 0.5rem;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0;
  transition: opacity 200ms ease;

  &--loading {
    opacity: 0.4;
    pointer-events: none;
  }
}

.report-error {
  padding: 0.95rem 1.1rem;
  margin: 1rem 0;
  border: 1px solid rgba(204, 0, 0, 0.35);
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: rgba(204, 0, 0, 0.06);
  color: var(--mr-missing);
  font-weight: 600;
}

.report-empty {
  padding: 1.1rem 1.2rem;
  margin: 1rem 0;
  border: 1px solid rgba(31, 56, 100, 0.2);
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: var(--mr-label-bg);
  color: var(--mr-navy);
  text-align: center;
  font-size: 0.98rem;
  font-weight: 600;
}

/* ── Data table ──────────────────────────────────────────────── */
.data-table {
  border-collapse: collapse;
  width: 100%;
  margin: 0;
  table-layout: fixed;

  td {
    border: 1px solid var(--mr-border);
    padding: 9px 14px;
    font-size: 14px;
    line-height: 1.5;
    word-wrap: break-word;
    width: 25%;
  }
}

.tbl-header {
  background-color: var(--mr-tbl-hdr-bg);
  color: var(--mr-tbl-hdr-text);
  font-weight: 700;
  font-size: 15px;
  text-align: center;
  padding: 10px 14px;
  letter-spacing: 0.3px;
}

.lbl {
  background-color: var(--mr-label-bg);
  color: var(--mr-label-text);
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: 13px;
  text-align: left;
}

.val {
  color: var(--mr-value-text);
  font-size: 14px;
  font-weight: 500;
  text-align: left;

  &.missing {
    color: var(--mr-missing);
    font-weight: 700;
  }
}

.val-span {
  color: var(--mr-value-text);
  font-size: 14px;
  font-weight: 500;
  text-align: left;
}

.well-name-cell {
  background-color: var(--mr-well-bg);
  color: var(--mr-well-text);
  font-weight: 700;
  font-size: 16px;
  text-align: center;
  line-height: 1.5;
  padding: 10px 14px;
}

.status-cell {
  background-color: var(--mr-well-bg);
  color: var(--mr-well-text);
  font-weight: 700;
  font-size: 16px;
  text-align: center;
  padding: 10px 14px;
}

.opt-cell {
  word-wrap: break-word;
  white-space: normal;
  text-align: left;
  font-size: 13px;
  line-height: 1.6;
}

/* ── Map section ─────────────────────────────────────────────── */
.report-map-section {
  margin-top: 0;
  border-top: 4px solid var(--mr-navy);
  background: #fff;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.map-container {
  width: 100%;
  min-height: 450px;
  background: #fff;
}

:host ::ng-deep app-wwellmap {
  display: block;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  min-height: 450px;
}

/* ── Responsive ─────────────────────────────────────────────── */
@media (max-width: 640px) {
  :host { padding: 1rem 0.5rem 2rem; }

  .report-title-main { font-size: 16px; }
  .report-title-sub  { font-size: 12px; }

  .report-toolbar { flex-wrap: wrap; }

  .map-container { min-height: 300px; }

  :host ::ng-deep app-wwellmap { min-height: 300px; }
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/presentation/presentation.component.scss

```scss
:host {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  height: 100%;
  overflow: hidden;
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #eef2f5 0%, #e7edf2 100%);
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 15px;
  line-height: 1.5;
}

.presentation {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  height: 100%;
  overflow: hidden;
  gap: 2px;
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(248, 250, 252, 0.72) 0%, rgba(230, 236, 241, 0.86) 100%);

  // In-app focus mode — covers the entire viewport, hiding the app's
  // sidebar/header. Combined with the native Fullscreen API on :host.
  &--fullscreen {
    position: fixed;
    inset: 0;
    z-index: 1000;
    height: 100vh;
    padding: 10px;
    background: linear-gradient(180deg, #eef2f5 0%, #e7edf2 100%);
  }
}

// ── Toolbar row ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
.presentation__toolbar {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  flex-shrink: 0;

  .toolbar__chips {
    flex: 1;
    min-width: 0;
  }
}

::ng-deep .pres-modal-dialog .mat-mdc-dialog-surface {
  background: var(--bg-app);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: 18px;
  box-shadow: 0 24px 80px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4);
}

::ng-deep .pres-modal-dialog .mat-mdc-dialog-content {
  max-height: none;
}

// ── Modal title ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
.pres-modal__title {
  font-size: 18px;
  font-weight: 800;
  color: var(--text-primary);
  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
}

.pres-modal__header {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 1rem;
  background: var(--bg-surface);
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);
  padding: 12px 20px;
}

.pres-modal__close {
  width: 28px;
  height: 28px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  background: var(--bg-card);
  color: var(--text-primary);

  svg {
    width: 16px;
    height: 16px;
  }
}

// ── Modal body — vertical stack: diagram on top, logs below ──────────────────
.pres-modal__body {
  flex: 1;
  overflow-y: auto;
  overflow-x: hidden;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  min-height: 0;
  height: calc(90vh - 60px);
  gap: 0;

  &::-webkit-scrollbar { width: 4px; }
  &::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb { background: var(--border); border-radius: 4px; }
}

// Diagram layout inside modal — depth scale + wellbore view side by side
.modal-diagram {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  height: calc(90vh - 160px);
  display: flex;
  align-items: stretch;
  overflow: hidden;
  position: relative;

  &::after {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    background: linear-gradient(90deg, transparent, rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.08), transparent);
    transform: translateX(-100%);
    opacity: 0;
    pointer-events: none;
  }

  &--loading::after {
    opacity: 1;
    animation: card-sheen 1.15s linear infinite;
  }
}

.pres-error-banner {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  margin: 0 12px 4px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 0.8rem 1rem;
  border: 1px solid rgba(220, 38, 38, 0.22);
  background: rgba(220, 38, 38, 0.08);
  color: var(--danger);
  font-size: 14px;
  font-weight: 700;
  line-height: 1.5;
}

// ── Layout toggle — absolute corner button inside diagram area ────────────────
.diagram-layout-toggle {
  position: absolute;
  top: 12px;
  right: 12px;
  z-index: 10;
  width: 32px;
  height: 32px;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  border-radius: 8px;
  border: 1.5px solid var(--border);
  background: var(--bg-surface);
  color: var(--text-primary);
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: border-color 0.18s, background 0.18s, color 0.18s, box-shadow 0.18s, transform 0.18s;

  svg {
    width: 15px;
    height: 15px;
    flex-shrink: 0;
  }

  &:hover {
    border-color: var(--accent);
    color: var(--accent);
    background: var(--accent-muted);
    transform: scale(1.05);
  }

  // Active / Free-mode state
  &--on {
    border-color: var(--accent);
    background: var(--accent-muted);
    color: var(--accent);
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.15);
  }
}

// ── Error banner ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
.error-banner {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  margin: 0 12px 4px;
  padding: 7px 14px;
  background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.1);
  border: 1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.3);
  border-radius: 8px;
  color: var(--danger);
  font-size: 14px;
  font-weight: 700;
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// MODE A — FIXED three-column layout (default)
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
.pres-fixed {
  flex: 1;
  display: flex;
  align-items: stretch;
  overflow: hidden;
  min-height: 0;
  gap: 0;

  &--loading {
    .pres-fixed__left > *,
    .pres-fixed__right > * {
      opacity: 0.9;
      filter: saturate(0.95);
    }

    .diagram-card {
      border-color: rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.34);
      box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.12);
    }
  }

  // Left panel — width driven by signal
  &__left {
    flex-shrink: 0;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 10px;
    overflow-y: auto;
    overflow-x: hidden;
    padding: 10px 0 10px 10px;
    min-width: 0;
    position: relative;

    > * {
      transition: opacity 0.22s ease, filter 0.22s ease;
    }

    // Make draggable-card children fill available height
    app-draggable-card {
      flex: 1;
      min-height: 0;
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
    }

    &::-webkit-scrollbar {
      width: 3px;
    }

    &::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
      background: var(--border);
      border-radius: 3px;
    }
  }

  // Center panel — fills remaining space
  &__center {
    flex: 1;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    overflow: hidden;
    min-width: 0;
    padding: 10px 0;
    gap: 10px;
    position: relative; // needed for canvas-controls absolute positioning
    transition: opacity 0.22s ease, filter 0.22s ease;
  }

  // Right panel — width driven by signal
  &__right {
    flex-shrink: 0;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 10px;
    overflow-y: auto;
    overflow-x: hidden;
    padding: 10px 10px 10px 0;
    min-width: 0;
    position: relative;

    > * {
      transition: opacity 0.22s ease, filter 0.22s ease;
    }

    app-draggable-card {
      flex-shrink: 0;
    }

    &::-webkit-scrollbar {
      width: 3px;
    }

    &::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
      background: var(--border);
      border-radius: 3px;
    }
  }
}

// Wellbore diagram + indicators unified card

.diagram-card {
  flex: 1;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #fcfdff 0%, #f4f8ff 100%);
  border-radius: 16px;
  border: 1px solid rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.22);
  box-shadow: 0 18px 40px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.12);
  overflow: hidden;
  min-height: 0;
  position: relative;
  transition: border-color 0.22s ease, box-shadow 0.22s ease;

  &::after {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    background: linear-gradient(90deg, transparent, rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.09), transparent);
    transform: translateX(-100%);
    opacity: 0;
    pointer-events: none;
  }

  &--loading::after {
    opacity: 1;
    animation: card-sheen 1.15s linear infinite;
  }
}

.diagram-scroll-area {
  flex: 1;
  display: flex;
  align-items: stretch;
  overflow: hidden;
}

// ── Diagram fullscreen overlay ────────────────────────────────────────────────
.diagram-card--diagram-fs {
  position: fixed;
  inset: 0;
  z-index: 900;
  border-radius: 0;
  border: none;

  .diagram-scroll-area {
    overflow: hidden;
  }
}

// ── Diagram fullscreen toggle button ─────────────────────────────────────────
.diagram-fs-toggle {
  position: absolute;
  top: 10px;
  right: 10px;
  z-index: 10;
  width: 34px;
  height: 34px;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  border-radius: 8px;
  border: 1.5px solid var(--border);
  background: var(--bg-surface);
  color: var(--text-secondary);
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: border-color 0.18s, background 0.18s, color 0.18s, box-shadow 0.18s, transform 0.18s;

  svg {
    width: 16px;
    height: 16px;
    flex-shrink: 0;
  }

  &:hover {
    border-color: var(--accent);
    color: var(--accent);
    background: var(--accent-muted);
    transform: scale(1.06);
  }

  &--active {
    border-color: var(--accent);
    background: var(--accent-muted);
    color: var(--accent);
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.15);
  }
}

.pres-section-card {
  display: block;
  border-radius: 16px;
  overflow: visible;

  > * {
    display: block;
    width: 100%;
  }

  &--left,
  &--right {
    flex-shrink: 0;
  }
}

:host ::ng-deep .pres-section-card .misc-card,
:host ::ng-deep .pres-section-card .formation-card,
:host ::ng-deep .pres-section-card .map-card,
:host ::ng-deep .pres-section-card .ow-container,
:host ::ng-deep .pres-section-card .test-results-container,
:host ::ng-deep .pres-section-card .docs-container {
  border-radius: 16px;
  border: 1px solid rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.18);
  box-shadow: 0 10px 24px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.09);
  overflow: hidden;
}

:host ::ng-deep .pres-section-card .misc-card {
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #ffffff 0%, #f8fafc 100%);
  padding: 12px;
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// MODE B — FREE canvas layout (Restructured to Vertical)
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
.pres-canvas {
  flex: 1;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  overflow-y: auto;
  overflow-x: hidden;
  padding: 12px 12px 24px;
  gap: 12px;
  min-height: 0;

  // Subtle dot grid backdrop
  background-image: radial-gradient(circle, var(--border) 1px, transparent 1px);
  background-size: 28px 28px;
  background-position: 14px 14px;
  background-color: var(--bg-content);

  &::-webkit-scrollbar {
    width: 4px;
  }

  &::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
    background: var(--border);
    border-radius: 4px;
  }
}

.canvas__section {
  width: 100%;
  position: relative;

  &--diagram {
    flex-shrink: 0;
    height: calc(100vh - var(--shell-bar-h, 62px) - 6px); // viewport minus the top bar (56/62px) and the page's 6px gap
    background: var(--bg-card);
    border-radius: 18px;
    padding: 50px 16px 16px;
    border: 1px solid var(--border);
    box-shadow: 0 10px 40px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 12px;
    overflow: hidden;

    // Logs indicator must not be squeezed by diagram-inner (flex:1)
    app-wwells-logs-indicators {
      flex-shrink: 0;
    }
  }
}

.canvas__row {
  display: flex;
  gap: 20px;
  width: 100%;

  @media (max-width: 1200px) {
    flex-direction: column;
  }

  .flex-1 {
    flex: 1;
    min-width: 0;
  }
}

// Diagram in free mode
.diagram-inner {
  flex: 1;
  display: flex;
  min-height: 0;
  background: var(--bg-surface);
  border-radius: 12px;
  overflow: hidden;
}

// ── Shared empty state ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
.empty-state {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  height: 100%;
  gap: 14px;
  color: var(--text-primary);
  padding: 24px;

  &__icon {
    font-size: 40px;
    opacity: 0.4;
  }

  &__text {
    font-size: 15px;
    font-weight: 700;
    line-height: 1.45;
    max-width: 28rem;
    text-align: center;
  }
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// UNIFIED INFO PANEL — tabbed card in Free Mode
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
.info-panel {
  width: 100%;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  min-height: 480px;
  background: var(--bg-card);
  border-radius: 18px;
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  box-shadow: 0 8px 32px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.12);
  overflow: hidden;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;

  // ── Tab navigation bar ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
  &__tabs {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 2px;
    padding: 10px 10px 0;
    background: var(--bg-surface);
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);
    overflow-x: auto;
    flex-shrink: 0;

    &::-webkit-scrollbar { height: 0; }
  }

  // ── Tab content area ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  &__content {
    flex: 1;
    overflow-y: auto;
    min-height: 400px;

    &::-webkit-scrollbar { width: 4px; }
    &::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb { background: var(--border); border-radius: 4px; }

    // Stretch child components to fill
    > * {
      display: block;
      width: 100%;
    }
  }
}

// ── Individual tab button ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
.tab-btn {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  padding: 8px 16px;
  border: none;
  border-radius: 10px 10px 0 0;
  background: transparent;
  color: var(--text-secondary);
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
  cursor: pointer;
  white-space: nowrap;
  position: relative;
  transition: color 0.18s, background 0.18s;
  flex-shrink: 0;

  svg {
    width: 13px;
    height: 13px;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    opacity: 0.7;
    transition: opacity 0.18s;
  }

  &::after {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    bottom: 0;
    left: 50%;
    width: 0;
    height: 2px;
    background: var(--accent);
    border-radius: 2px 2px 0 0;
    transform: translateX(-50%);
    transition: width 0.22s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);
  }

  &:hover {
    color: var(--text-primary);
    background: rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.06);

    svg { opacity: 1; }
  }

  &--active {
    color: var(--accent);
    background: rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.08);

    svg { opacity: 1; color: var(--accent); }

    &::after {
      width: calc(100% - 16px);
    }
  }
}

@keyframes float {

  0%,
  100% {
    transform: translateY(0);
  }

  50% {
    transform: translateY(-5px);
  }
}

@keyframes card-sheen {
  0% { transform: translateX(-100%); }
  100% { transform: translateX(100%); }
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/shared/wwell-theme-toggle/wwell-theme-toggle.component.html

```html
<div class="wtt-group" cdkOverlayOrigin #origin="cdkOverlayOrigin">
  <!-- The label names the action (not the state), so a screen reader hears what a press will do. -->
  <button
    type="button"
    mat-icon-button
    class="wtt wtt--quick"
    [class.wtt--dark]="theme.isDark()"
    [attr.aria-label]="theme.isDark() ? 'Switch to light theme' : 'Switch to dark theme'"
    [matTooltip]="theme.isDark() ? 'Light theme' : 'Dark theme'"
    matTooltipClass="wp-tooltip"
    (click)="theme.toggle()"
  >
    <mat-icon class="wtt__icon wtt__icon--moon">dark_mode</mat-icon>
    <mat-icon class="wtt__icon wtt__icon--sun">light_mode</mat-icon>
  </button>

  <button
    #tuneTrigger
    type="button"
    mat-icon-button
    class="wtt wtt--tune"
    [class.wtt--open]="isOpen()"
    aria-label="Appearance settings"
    aria-haspopup="dialog"
    [attr.aria-expanded]="isOpen()"
    matTooltip="Appearance"
    matTooltipClass="wp-tooltip"
    [matTooltipDisabled]="isOpen()"
    (click)="toggleOpen()"
  >
    <mat-icon>tune</mat-icon>
  </button>
</div>

<ng-template
  cdkConnectedOverlay
  [cdkConnectedOverlayOrigin]="origin"
  [cdkConnectedOverlayOpen]="isOpen()"
  [cdkConnectedOverlayPositions]="popoverPositions"
  [cdkConnectedOverlayPush]="true"
  [cdkConnectedOverlayViewportMargin]="8"
  [cdkConnectedOverlayHasBackdrop]="true"
  cdkConnectedOverlayBackdropClass="cdk-overlay-transparent-backdrop"
  (backdropClick)="close()"
  (overlayKeydown)="onOverlayKeydown($event)"
  (detach)="isOpen.set(false)"
>
  <section class="wtt-pop" role="dialog" aria-label="Appearance" cdkTrapFocus [cdkTrapFocusAutoCapture]="true">
    <h2 class="wtt-pop__title">Appearance</h2>

    <fieldset class="wtt-field">
      <legend class="wtt-field__label">Theme</legend>
      <div class="wtt-seg">
        @for (option of modeOptions; track option.value) {
          <label class="wtt-seg__opt">
            <input
              class="wtt-seg__input"
              type="radio"
              name="wtt-mode"
              [value]="option.value"
              [checked]="theme.mode() === option.value"
              (change)="theme.setMode(option.value)"
            />
            <span class="wtt-seg__face">
              <mat-icon aria-hidden="true">{{ option.icon }}</mat-icon>
              {{ option.label }}
            </span>
          </label>
        }
      </div>
    </fieldset>

    <div class="wtt-field">
      <label class="wtt-field__head">
        <span class="wtt-field__label">{{ theme.isDark() ? 'Dark' : 'Light' }} contrast</span>
        <span class="wtt-field__value" aria-hidden="true">{{ theme.contrast() }}%</span>
        <input
          class="wtt-range"
          type="range"
          step="1"
          [attr.aria-label]="(theme.isDark() ? 'Dark' : 'Light') + ' theme contrast'"
          [min]="minContrast"
          [max]="maxContrast"
          [value]="theme.contrast()"
          [attr.aria-valuetext]="theme.contrast() + ' percent'"
          [style.--wtt-p]="theme.contrast() / maxContrast"
          (input)="onContrastInput($event)"
        />
      </label>
      <div class="wtt-range__ends" aria-hidden="true">
        <span>Soft</span>
        <span>High</span>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="wtt-foot">
      <p class="wtt-foot__hint">
        {{ theme.isDark() ? 'Lower it if dark mode feels harsh.' : 'Lower it to cut glare in bright rooms.' }}
      </p>
      <button type="button" class="wtt-reset" [disabled]="theme.isContrastDefault()" (click)="theme.resetContrast()">
        Reset
      </button>
    </div>

    <div class="wtt-legacy">
      <div class="wtt-legacy__text">
        <span class="wtt-legacy__title" id="wtt-legacy-label">Legacy layout</span>
        <span class="wtt-legacy__hint">Classic navy top bar and left nav. Theme still applies to the page.</span>
      </div>
      <button
        type="button"
        class="wtt-switch"
        role="switch"
        aria-labelledby="wtt-legacy-label"
        [attr.aria-checked]="theme.isLegacyShell()"
        (click)="theme.setLegacyShell(!theme.isLegacyShell())"
      >
        <span class="wtt-switch__thumb"></span>
      </button>
    </div>
  </section>
</ng-template>
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/shared/wwell-theme-toggle/wwell-theme-toggle.component.scss

```scss
@use '../wwell-design-tokens' as tokens;

// The control lives in the shell's top bar, so its two buttons are drawn from the shell tokens (one
// look per shell, scoped like the bar itself: `data-shell` on <html>). The popover further down is a
// planning surface and keeps the `--wp-*` tokens.
:host {
  display: inline-flex;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  --mat-icon-button-icon-size: 18px;
}

.wtt-group {
  display: inline-flex;
}

.wtt {
  position: relative;
  transition:
    background-color tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out,
    border-color tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out,
    color tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out;

  &:focus-visible {
    z-index: 2;
  }
}

// ── Mono shell: a joined pair of square buttons under one hairline ───────────
:host-context([data-shell='mono']) {
  --mat-icon-button-state-layer-size: 34px;

  .wtt-group {
    overflow: hidden;
    border: 1px solid var(--shell-hair-strong);
    border-radius: 5px;
  }

  .wtt {
    --mat-icon-button-icon-color: var(--shell-muted);
    border-radius: 0;

    &:hover,
    &.wtt--open {
      background: var(--shell-press);
      --mat-icon-button-icon-color: var(--shell-text);
    }

    &:focus-visible {
      outline: 2px solid var(--shell-text);
      outline-offset: -2px;
    }
  }

  .wtt--tune {
    border-left: 1px solid var(--shell-hair-strong);
  }
}

// ── Legacy shell: the same two buttons as the old navy bar's bell ────────────
:host-context([data-shell='legacy']) {
  --mat-icon-button-state-layer-size: 40px;

  .wtt-group {
    gap: 8px;
  }

  .wtt {
    --mat-icon-button-icon-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.55);
    border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
    border-radius: 10px;
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);

    &:hover,
    &.wtt--open {
      border-color: rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.4);
      background: rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.14);
      box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.2);
      --mat-icon-button-icon-color: #38bdf8;
    }

    &:focus-visible {
      outline: 2px solid #38bdf8;
      outline-offset: 2px;
    }
  }
}

// Both glyphs are stacked and cross-rotate with transform + opacity only (compositor-friendly).
// Light theme shows the moon (pressing goes dark); dark theme shows the sun.
// `.wtt .wtt__icon` (not `.wtt__icon`): Material sets its own position/size on `.mat-icon`, which would
// otherwise win and let the second glyph wrap out of the button.
.wtt .wtt__icon {
  position: absolute;
  top: 50%;
  left: 50%;
  width: 18px;
  height: 18px;
  margin: -9px 0 0 -9px;
  font-size: 18px;
  line-height: 18px;
  transition:
    transform tokens.$duration-base tokens.$ease-out,
    opacity tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out;
}

.wtt .wtt__icon--sun {
  opacity: 0;
  transform: rotate(-90deg) scale(0.5);
}

.wtt--dark {
  .wtt__icon--sun {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: none;
  }

  .wtt__icon--moon {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: rotate(90deg) scale(0.5);
  }
}

// ── Appearance popover ───────────────────────────────────────────
// Rendered in the CDK overlay on <body>, but it is this component's own template, so these scoped
// rules still apply; the `--wp-*` tokens arrive by inheritance from <html>.
.wtt-pop {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 16px;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  width: 272px;
  padding: 16px;
  border: 1px solid tokens.$border-strong;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-lg;
  background: tokens.$surface-raised;
  box-shadow: tokens.$shadow-3;
  color: tokens.$navy;
  font-family: tokens.$font-family;
  transform-origin: top right;
  animation: wtt-pop-in tokens.$duration-base tokens.$ease-out;
}

@keyframes wtt-pop-in {
  from {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(-4px) scale(0.98);
  }
}

.wtt-pop__title {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: tokens.$text-sm;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
  color: tokens.$navy-deep;
}

.wtt-field {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 8px;
  min-width: 0;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  border: 0;
}

.wtt-field__label {
  padding: 0;
  @include tokens.type-eyebrow;
}

// A fieldset's legend is not a flex item, so the column's `gap` doesn't reach it.
legend.wtt-field__label {
  margin-bottom: 8px;
}

// The label wraps the slider so the whole row is one click target; the head text and the range
// share a two-column grid: name left, value right, slider full width beneath.
.wtt-field__head {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr auto;
  align-items: center;
  row-gap: 8px;

  .wtt-range {
    grid-column: 1 / -1;
  }
}

.wtt-field__value {
  @include tokens.type-mono;
  font-size: tokens.$text-xs;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: tokens.$navy-deep;
}

// Segmented System/Light/Dark control. The native radios stay (arrow-key roving, form semantics)
// and are stretched invisibly over their face, so the whole segment is the hit target.
.wtt-seg {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
  gap: 2px;
  padding: 2px;
  border: 1px solid tokens.$border;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-md;
  background: tokens.$surface-sunken;
}

.wtt-seg__opt {
  position: relative;
  display: block;
}

.wtt-seg__input {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  margin: 0;
  opacity: 0;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.wtt-seg__face {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  gap: 5px;
  height: 30px;
  border: 1px solid transparent;
  border-radius: calc(#{tokens.$radius-md} - 2px);
  font-size: tokens.$text-xs;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: tokens.$muted;
  transition:
    background-color tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out,
    border-color tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out,
    color tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out;

  mat-icon {
    width: 15px;
    height: 15px;
    font-size: 15px;
    line-height: 15px;
  }
}

.wtt-seg__opt:hover .wtt-seg__face {
  color: tokens.$navy-deep;
}

.wtt-seg__input:checked + .wtt-seg__face {
  border-color: tokens.$border-strong;
  background: tokens.$surface;
  box-shadow: tokens.$shadow-1;
  color: tokens.$navy-deep;
}

.wtt-seg__input:focus-visible + .wtt-seg__face {
  @include tokens.focus-ring;
}

// Native range, restyled. The filled stretch stops at the thumb's centre, which travels from half a
// thumb in to half a thumb short of the end — hence the calc rather than a bare percentage.
.wtt-range {
  --wtt-thumb: 16px;
  --wtt-stop: calc(var(--wtt-thumb) / 2 + (100% - var(--wtt-thumb)) * var(--wtt-p, 0.5));

  appearance: none;
  -webkit-appearance: none;
  width: 100%;
  height: 20px;
  margin: 0;
  background: transparent;
  cursor: pointer;

  &::-webkit-slider-runnable-track {
    height: 4px;
    border-radius: tokens.$radius-sm;
    background: linear-gradient(to right, tokens.$accent var(--wtt-stop), tokens.$border-strong var(--wtt-stop));
  }

  &::-moz-range-track {
    height: 4px;
    border-radius: tokens.$radius-sm;
    background: linear-gradient(to right, tokens.$accent var(--wtt-stop), tokens.$border-strong var(--wtt-stop));
  }

  &::-webkit-slider-thumb {
    -webkit-appearance: none;
    box-sizing: border-box;
    width: var(--wtt-thumb);
    height: var(--wtt-thumb);
    margin-top: -6px; // (track 4px − thumb 16px) / 2
    border: 2px solid tokens.$surface-raised;
    border-radius: 50%;
    background: tokens.$navy-deep;
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px tokens.$border-control;
    transition: transform tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out;
  }

  &::-moz-range-thumb {
    box-sizing: border-box;
    width: var(--wtt-thumb);
    height: var(--wtt-thumb);
    border: 2px solid tokens.$surface-raised;
    border-radius: 50%;
    background: tokens.$navy-deep;
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px tokens.$border-control;
    transition: transform tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out;
  }

  &:hover::-webkit-slider-thumb,
  &:active::-webkit-slider-thumb {
    transform: scale(1.12);
  }

  &:hover::-moz-range-thumb,
  &:active::-moz-range-thumb {
    transform: scale(1.12);
  }

  &:focus-visible {
    outline: none;
  }

  &:focus-visible::-webkit-slider-thumb {
    box-shadow:
      0 0 0 1px tokens.$border-control,
      0 0 0 3px tokens.$surface-raised,
      0 0 0 5px tokens.$accent;
  }

  &:focus-visible::-moz-range-thumb {
    box-shadow:
      0 0 0 1px tokens.$border-control,
      0 0 0 3px tokens.$surface-raised,
      0 0 0 5px tokens.$accent;
  }
}

.wtt-range__ends {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  @include tokens.type-label;
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
}

.wtt-foot {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 12px;
  padding-top: 12px;
  border-top: 1px solid tokens.$border; // $border-soft vanishes against the raised surface
}

.wtt-foot__hint {
  margin: 0;
  @include tokens.type-label;
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
  line-height: 1.4;
}

.wtt-reset {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  padding: 4px 8px;
  border: 1px solid tokens.$border;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-sm;
  background: transparent;
  font: inherit;
  font-size: tokens.$text-xs;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: tokens.$navy-deep;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition:
    background-color tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out,
    border-color tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out;

  &:hover:not(:disabled) {
    border-color: tokens.$border-strong;
    background: tokens.$surface-hover;
  }

  &:focus-visible {
    @include tokens.focus-ring;
  }

  &:disabled {
    cursor: default;
    opacity: 0.45;
  }
}

// ── Legacy layout switch ─────────────────────────────────────────
// Swaps the redesigned top bar and left nav for the old navy ones; light/dark is untouched.
.wtt-legacy {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
  padding-top: 12px;
  border-top: 1px solid tokens.$border;
}

.wtt-legacy__text {
  display: flex;
  flex: 1;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 2px;
  min-width: 0;
}

.wtt-legacy__title {
  font-size: tokens.$text-sm;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: tokens.$navy-deep;
}

.wtt-legacy__hint {
  @include tokens.type-label;
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
  line-height: 1.4;
}

.wtt-switch {
  position: relative;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  box-sizing: border-box;
  width: 38px;
  height: 22px;
  padding: 0;
  border: 1px solid tokens.$border-control;
  border-radius: tokens.$radius-pill;
  background: tokens.$surface-sunken;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition:
    background-color tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out,
    border-color tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out;

  &:focus-visible {
    @include tokens.focus-ring;
  }

  &[aria-checked='true'] {
    border-color: tokens.$cta;
    background: tokens.$cta;

    .wtt-switch__thumb {
      background: tokens.$on-cta;
      transform: translateX(16px);
    }
  }
}

.wtt-switch__thumb {
  position: absolute;
  top: 3px;
  left: 3px;
  width: 14px;
  height: 14px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: tokens.$muted;
  transition:
    transform tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out,
    background-color tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out;
}

// Vendor thumb pseudo-elements get their own rules: one unknown selector in a list drops the whole rule.
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .wtt .wtt__icon,
  .wtt-pop,
  .wtt-switch__thumb {
    transition: none;
    animation: none;
  }

  .wtt-range::-webkit-slider-thumb {
    transition: none;
  }

  .wtt-range::-moz-range-thumb {
    transition: none;
  }
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/shared/wwell-theme-toggle/wwell-theme-toggle.component.ts

```ts
import { CdkTrapFocus } from '@angular/cdk/a11y';
import { CdkConnectedOverlay, CdkOverlayOrigin, ConnectedPosition } from '@angular/cdk/overlay';
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, ElementRef, inject, signal, viewChild } from '@angular/core';
import { MatButtonModule } from '@angular/material/button';
import { MatIconModule } from '@angular/material/icon';
import { MatTooltipModule } from '@angular/material/tooltip';
import { MAX_CONTRAST, MIN_CONTRAST, WwellThemeMode, WwellThemeService } from '../wwell-theme.service';

interface ModeOption {
  readonly value: WwellThemeMode;
  readonly label: string;
  readonly icon: string;
}

const MODE_OPTIONS: readonly ModeOption[] = [
  { value: 'light', label: 'Light', icon: 'light_mode' },
  { value: 'dark', label: 'Dark', icon: 'dark_mode' },
  { value: 'system', label: 'System', icon: 'brightness_auto' },
];

/** Below the control, right-aligned to it; flips above when there is no room underneath. */
const POPOVER_POSITIONS: ConnectedPosition[] = [
  { originX: 'end', originY: 'bottom', overlayX: 'end', overlayY: 'top', offsetY: 6 },
  { originX: 'end', originY: 'top', overlayX: 'end', overlayY: 'bottom', offsetY: -6 },
];

/**
 * Appearance control for the shell's top bar: a one-press light/dark switch, plus an Appearance popover
 * for the System/Light/Dark choice, a contrast dial for whichever theme is showing, and the Legacy
 * layout switch that swaps the redesigned top bar and left nav for the old navy ones.
 */
@Component({
  selector: 'app-wwell-theme-toggle',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CdkConnectedOverlay, CdkOverlayOrigin, CdkTrapFocus, MatButtonModule, MatIconModule, MatTooltipModule],
  templateUrl: './wwell-theme-toggle.component.html',
  styleUrl: './wwell-theme-toggle.component.scss',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class WwellThemeToggleComponent {
  protected readonly theme = inject(WwellThemeService);
  protected readonly modeOptions = MODE_OPTIONS;
  protected readonly popoverPositions = POPOVER_POSITIONS;
  protected readonly minContrast = MIN_CONTRAST;
  protected readonly maxContrast = MAX_CONTRAST;
  protected readonly isOpen = signal(false);

  private readonly tuneTrigger = viewChild.required('tuneTrigger', { read: ElementRef<HTMLElement> });

  protected toggleOpen(): void {
    this.isOpen.update((open) => !open);
  }

  /** Closes the popover and puts focus back on its trigger — Safari doesn't focus a button on click,
   *  so the focus trap's own restore would land on <body>. */
  protected close(): void {
    this.isOpen.set(false);
    this.tuneTrigger().nativeElement.focus();
  }

  protected onOverlayKeydown(event: KeyboardEvent): void {
    if (event.key !== 'Escape') return;
    event.preventDefault();
    this.close();
  }

  protected onContrastInput(event: Event): void {
    this.theme.setContrast((event.target as HTMLInputElement).valueAsNumber);
  }
}
```

## [A] src/app/features/wwell-planning/shared/wwell-theme.service.spec.ts

```ts
import { TestBed } from '@angular/core/testing';

import { WwellThemeService } from './wwell-theme.service';

const SHELL_STORAGE_KEY = 'wwell-shell';
const SHELL_ATTRIBUTE = 'data-shell';

describe('WwellThemeService shell layout', () => {
  const root = document.documentElement;

  beforeEach(() => {
    localStorage.clear();
    root.removeAttribute(SHELL_ATTRIBUTE);
    TestBed.resetTestingModule();
  });

  it('defaults to the mono shell and stamps it on <html>', () => {
    const service = TestBed.inject(WwellThemeService);

    expect(service.shell()).toBe('mono');
    expect(service.isLegacyShell()).toBe(false);
    expect(root.getAttribute(SHELL_ATTRIBUTE)).toBe('mono');
  });

  it('switches to the legacy shell, updates <html> and remembers the choice', () => {
    const service = TestBed.inject(WwellThemeService);

    service.setLegacyShell(true);
    TestBed.tick();

    expect(service.shell()).toBe('legacy');
    expect(service.isLegacyShell()).toBe(true);
    expect(root.getAttribute(SHELL_ATTRIBUTE)).toBe('legacy');
    expect(localStorage.getItem(SHELL_STORAGE_KEY)).toBe('legacy');
  });

  it('restores a remembered legacy choice on the next visit', () => {
    localStorage.setItem(SHELL_STORAGE_KEY, 'legacy');

    const service = TestBed.inject(WwellThemeService);

    expect(service.isLegacyShell()).toBe(true);
    expect(root.getAttribute(SHELL_ATTRIBUTE)).toBe('legacy');
  });

  it('forgets the stored choice once the user returns to mono', () => {
    localStorage.setItem(SHELL_STORAGE_KEY, 'legacy');
    const service = TestBed.inject(WwellThemeService);

    service.setLegacyShell(false);
    TestBed.tick();

    expect(service.shell()).toBe('mono');
    expect(root.getAttribute(SHELL_ATTRIBUTE)).toBe('mono');
    expect(localStorage.getItem(SHELL_STORAGE_KEY)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('falls back to mono when the stored value is not recognised', () => {
    localStorage.setItem(SHELL_STORAGE_KEY, 'sepia');

    const service = TestBed.inject(WwellThemeService);

    expect(service.shell()).toBe('mono');
  });

  it('leaves the light/dark theme alone when the shell changes', () => {
    const service = TestBed.inject(WwellThemeService);
    const before = service.theme();

    service.setLegacyShell(true);
    TestBed.tick();

    expect(service.theme()).toBe(before);
  });
});
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/shared/wwell-theme.service.ts

```ts
import { DOCUMENT } from '@angular/common';
import { Injectable, computed, effect, inject, signal } from '@angular/core';

export type WwellTheme = 'light' | 'dark';
/** What the user picked: a fixed theme, or `system` to follow the OS. */
export type WwellThemeMode = WwellTheme | 'system';
/** Which top bar + left nav is showing: the redesigned `mono` shell, or the navy `legacy` one it replaced. */
export type WwellShell = 'mono' | 'legacy';

const THEME_STORAGE_KEY = 'wwell-theme';
const SHELL_STORAGE_KEY = 'wwell-shell';
const SHELL_ATTRIBUTE = 'data-shell';
/** Dark keeps the key it shipped with, so a value saved before light gained a dial still applies. */
const CONTRAST_STORAGE_KEYS: Readonly<Record<WwellTheme, string>> = {
  dark: 'wwell-contrast',
  light: 'wwell-contrast-light',
};
const CONTRAST_PROPERTIES: Readonly<Record<WwellTheme, string>> = {
  dark: '--wp-contrast-dark',
  light: '--wp-contrast-light',
};
const THEMES: readonly WwellTheme[] = ['dark', 'light'];
const THEME_ATTRIBUTE = 'data-wp-theme';
const SWITCHING_CLASS = 'wp-theme-switching';
const DARK_QUERY = '(prefers-color-scheme: dark)';
/** Slightly longer than the 200ms cross-fade in _wwell-theme.scss, so it can finish before the class drops. */
const SWITCHING_MS = 260;

export const MIN_CONTRAST = 0;
export const MAX_CONTRAST = 100;
/** Where each theme rests until the user moves its dial. Keep in step with the `var(--wp-contrast-*, N%)`
 *  fallbacks in _wwell-theme.scss. Light's standard look is its High end; dark opens softer than its own. */
export const DEFAULT_CONTRASTS: Readonly<Record<WwellTheme, number>> = { dark: 50, light: 100 };

/**
 * Light/dark theme for the wwell-planning module.
 *
 * Writes `data-wp-theme` and both contrast variables on <html> (not on a module element) because
 * dialogs, select panels and tooltips render on <body>, outside the module's DOM — they only pick up
 * the `--wp-*` tokens from _wwell-theme.scss by inheriting from the root. The tokens are `--wp-`
 * prefixed, so no other feature reacts to any of them.
 *
 * Follows the OS `prefers-color-scheme` until the user picks a theme; from then on the explicit
 * choice wins and is remembered in localStorage. Each theme has its own contrast dial (0 soft – 100
 * high, see `dark-tone` / `light-tone` in _wwell-theme.scss); both are always written, so switching
 * theme never waits on a second update to repaint at the right contrast.
 *
 * The same attribute mechanism carries the shell layout: `data-shell="mono" | "legacy"` on <html>
 * (see layout/_shell-theme.scss). Legacy is opt-in and remembered; mono is what everyone gets by default.
 */
@Injectable({ providedIn: 'root' })
export class WwellThemeService {
  private readonly document = inject(DOCUMENT);
  private readonly systemQuery = this.document.defaultView?.matchMedia?.(DARK_QUERY) ?? null;

  /** The user's explicit pick; null means "follow the OS". */
  private readonly chosen = signal<WwellTheme | null>(this.readStoredTheme());
  private readonly system = signal<WwellTheme>(this.systemQuery?.matches ? 'dark' : 'light');
  private readonly contrasts = signal<Readonly<Record<WwellTheme, number>>>({
    dark: this.readStoredContrast('dark'),
    light: this.readStoredContrast('light'),
  });
  private readonly legacyChosen = signal<boolean>(this.readStored(SHELL_STORAGE_KEY) === 'legacy');
  private switchingTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;

  readonly shell = computed<WwellShell>(() => (this.legacyChosen() ? 'legacy' : 'mono'));
  readonly isLegacyShell = computed(() => this.legacyChosen());
  readonly theme = computed<WwellTheme>(() => this.chosen() ?? this.system());
  readonly isDark = computed(() => this.theme() === 'dark');
  readonly mode = computed<WwellThemeMode>(() => this.chosen() ?? 'system');
  /** Contrast of the theme that is showing, 0 (soft) – 100 (high). */
  readonly contrast = computed(() => this.contrasts()[this.theme()]);
  readonly isContrastDefault = computed(() => this.contrast() === DEFAULT_CONTRASTS[this.theme()]);

  constructor() {
    this.systemQuery?.addEventListener('change', (event) => {
      this.system.set(event.matches ? 'dark' : 'light');
    });

    // Applied synchronously so the first paint of a wwell-planning page is already in the right
    // theme; the effects keep the root in sync with later changes (toggle, OS switch, dial).
    this.apply(this.theme());
    this.applyContrasts(this.contrasts());
    this.applyShell(this.shell());
    effect(() => {
      this.apply(this.theme());
    });
    effect(() => {
      this.applyContrasts(this.contrasts());
    });
    effect(() => {
      this.applyShell(this.shell());
    });
  }

  toggle(): void {
    this.set(this.isDark() ? 'light' : 'dark');
  }

  set(theme: WwellTheme): void {
    this.setMode(theme);
  }

  setMode(mode: WwellThemeMode): void {
    if (mode === this.mode()) return;
    this.flagSwitching();
    this.chosen.set(mode === 'system' ? null : mode);
    this.writeStored(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, mode === 'system' ? null : mode);
  }

  /** Sets the contrast of the theme that is showing. */
  setContrast(value: number): void {
    const theme = this.theme();
    const next = clampContrast(value);
    if (next === this.contrasts()[theme]) return;
    this.contrasts.update((all) => ({ ...all, [theme]: next }));
    this.writeStored(CONTRAST_STORAGE_KEYS[theme], next === DEFAULT_CONTRASTS[theme] ? null : String(next));
  }

  resetContrast(): void {
    this.setContrast(DEFAULT_CONTRASTS[this.theme()]);
  }

  /** `true` shows the legacy navy shell; `false` is the default mono one. Independent of light/dark. */
  setLegacyShell(legacy: boolean): void {
    if (legacy === this.legacyChosen()) return;
    this.flagSwitching();
    this.legacyChosen.set(legacy);
    this.writeStored(SHELL_STORAGE_KEY, legacy ? 'legacy' : null);
  }

  private apply(theme: WwellTheme): void {
    this.document.documentElement.setAttribute(THEME_ATTRIBUTE, theme);
  }

  private applyShell(shell: WwellShell): void {
    this.document.documentElement.setAttribute(SHELL_ATTRIBUTE, shell);
  }

  private applyContrasts(all: Readonly<Record<WwellTheme, number>>): void {
    const style = this.document.documentElement.style;
    for (const theme of THEMES) {
      style.setProperty(CONTRAST_PROPERTIES[theme], `${all[theme]}%`);
    }
  }

  /** Adds the cross-fade class for the duration of the switch only, so it never animates on load. */
  private flagSwitching(): void {
    const root = this.document.documentElement;
    root.classList.add(SWITCHING_CLASS);
    clearTimeout(this.switchingTimer);
    this.switchingTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      root.classList.remove(SWITCHING_CLASS);
    }, SWITCHING_MS);
  }

  private readStoredTheme(): WwellTheme | null {
    const value = this.readStored(THEME_STORAGE_KEY);
    return value === 'light' || value === 'dark' ? value : null;
  }

  private readStoredContrast(theme: WwellTheme): number {
    const value = this.readStored(CONTRAST_STORAGE_KEYS[theme]);
    if (value === null) return DEFAULT_CONTRASTS[theme];
    const parsed = Number(value);
    return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? clampContrast(parsed) : DEFAULT_CONTRASTS[theme];
  }

  // Storage can throw (private mode, blocked site data). The appearance still works for the
  // session; it just isn't remembered — nothing the user needs to be told about.
  private readStored(key: string): string | null {
    try {
      return this.document.defaultView?.localStorage.getItem(key) ?? null;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  /** `null` clears the key, so a choice equal to the default doesn't linger in storage. */
  private writeStored(key: string, value: string | null): void {
    try {
      const storage = this.document.defaultView?.localStorage;
      if (value === null) storage?.removeItem(key);
      else storage?.setItem(key, value);
    } catch {
      // See readStored: an unwritable store only costs persistence.
    }
  }
}

function clampContrast(value: number): number {
  return Math.min(MAX_CONTRAST, Math.max(MIN_CONTRAST, Math.round(value)));
}
```

```ts
import { TestBed } from '@angular/core/testing';
import { HttpErrorResponse } from '@angular/common/http';

describe('WwellIrStore - createIr', () => {
  let store: InstanceType<typeof WwellIrStore>;

  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
    jest.useFakeTimers();
    // createIr no longer trusts a client-side echo (see its own doc comment in WwellIrStore) — an
    // empty detail response here means "nothing extra to merge," so `irs()` ends up exactly what
    // buildIntegratedRequirementFromPayload alone would build, matching most of these assertions.
    serviceMock.getIrDetailBlocks.mockReturnValue(of([]));

      ],
    });

  afterEach(() => {
    jest.useRealTimers();
  });

    serviceMock.createIr.mockReturnValue(of(9001));

    store.createIr(buildPayload(), buildIrBulkPayload());
    // createIr's request is wrapped in delay(WWELL_SAVE_DELAY_MS) — see WwellIrStore — so its own
    // "creatingIr" flag stays true for at least that long even against this instant mock response.
    jest.advanceTimersByTime(WWELL_SAVE_DELAY_MS);

    expect(serviceMock.createIr).toHaveBeenCalledWith(buildIrBulkPayload());
    expect(serviceMock.getIrDetailBlocks).toHaveBeenCalledWith(9001);
    // The follow-up GET's merge also stamps whether the stored remarks still carry legacy HTML (they don't here).
    expect(store.irs()).toEqual([
      {
        ...buildIntegratedRequirementFromPayload(buildPayload(), { id: 'IR-9001', code: 'IR-9001', irSeq: 9001 }),
        hasLegacyMarkup: false,
      },
    ]);
    expect(store.creatingIr()).toBe(false);
    expect(store.createIrError()).toBeNull();
  });

  /** Attachments are real files fetched per ir_seq (loadIrAttachments), never part of a save — so a
   *  freshly created record starts with none until that separate fetch resolves. */
  it('starts a newly created record with no attachments', () => {
    serviceMock.createIr.mockReturnValue(of(9001));

    store.createIr(buildPayload(), buildIrBulkPayload());
    jest.advanceTimersByTime(WWELL_SAVE_DELAY_MS);

    expect(store.irs()[0].attachments).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('sets createIrError on failure and clears the creating flag', () => {
    serviceMock.createIr.mockReturnValue(throwError(() => new Error('Network down')));

    store.createIr(buildPayload(), buildIrBulkPayload());

    // delay() only defers next/complete notifications, not errors — see WwellIrStore's own note
    // on createIr — so the error path settles synchronously, with no timer to advance.
    expect(store.creatingIr()).toBe(false);
    expect(store.createIrError()).toBe('Network down');
    expect(serviceMock.getIrDetailBlocks).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('surfaces a distinct error and leaves irs untouched when the save succeeds but the follow-up GET fails', () => {
    serviceMock.createIr.mockReturnValue(of(9001));
    serviceMock.getIrDetailBlocks.mockReturnValue(throwError(() => new Error('timeout')));

    store.createIr(buildPayload(), buildIrBulkPayload());
    jest.advanceTimersByTime(WWELL_SAVE_DELAY_MS);

    expect(store.irs()).toEqual([]);
    expect(store.creatingIr()).toBe(false);
    // The backend did confirm this ir_seq, so it's still remembered — a resave must UPDATE, not
    // re-INSERT under a new row, even though `irs` couldn't be refreshed this time.
    expect(store.draftIrSeq()).toBe(9001);
    expect(store.createIrError()).toContain('refresh');
  });

  it('remembers the returned ir_seq — WwellIrComponent.createNewIr() reads this to select the just-created draft', () => {
    serviceMock.createIr.mockReturnValue(of(9001));

    store.createIr(buildPayload(), buildIrBulkPayload());
    jest.advanceTimersByTime(WWELL_SAVE_DELAY_MS);

    expect(store.draftIrSeq()).toBe(9001);
  });
});

function buildIntegratedRequirement(overrides: Partial<IntegratedRequirement> = {}): IntegratedRequirement {
  return {
    id: 'IR-9001',
    code: 'IR-9001',
    irSeq: 9001,
    updatedDate: '2026-01-01',
    status: 'in-progress',
    requestReference: {
      linkedRequestId: null,
      client: 'BI-12',
      supportingBusiness: 'Agriculture',
      wellName: 'HAIL-500',
      targetAquifer: 'Saq',
      targetDepthFt: 510,
      surfaceElevationFtAsl: 980,
      coordinates: '27.5114°N, 41.7208°E',
    },
    formationTops: [],
    topsRemarks: null,
    casing: [],
    programDetails: {
      completion: 'Screened',
      openHoleSizeIn: null,
      acidization: '',
      transmission: '',
      developmentTesting: { logging: '', mudLogging: '', sampling: '', flowTest: '', remarks: '' },
    },
    contacts: { geologistPrimary: 'Eng. A. Al-Mutairi', geologistSecondary: null },
    offsetWells: [],
    offsetWellsRemarks: null,
    hydrogeology: { aquifer: 'Saq', estimatedTdsPpm: 1200, h2sPpm: 5, estimatedPumpDepthFt: 150 },
    testProgram: { testType: 'pump-test', estimatedRateGpm: 350, estimatedWaterLevelFt: 95 },
    attachments: [],
    chain: [],
    sapWfId: null,
    sapWfStatusText: null,
    ...overrides,
  };
}

describe('WwellIrStore - submitIrForReview', () => {
  let store: InstanceType<typeof WwellIrStore>;

  const emptyWorkflowStatus = {
    header: {
      process_name: 'Geological Operations',
      initiator_id: 'ALANMX10',
      requester_id: 'ALANMX10',
      date_initiated: '2026-09-15',
      time_initiated: '11:30:35',
      wf_status: null,
      wf_status_text: 'In Process',
    },
    remarks: [],
    reject_remarks: [],
    approval_chain: [],
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
    jest.useFakeTimers();
    authStoreMock.networkId.mockReturnValue('junedalam');
    sapServiceMock.getWorkflowStatus.mockReturnValue(of(emptyWorkflowStatus));
    TestBed.configureTestingModule({
      providers: [
        WwellIrStore,
        { provide: WwellIrService, useValue: serviceMock },
        { provide: SapService, useValue: sapServiceMock },
        { provide: AuthStore, useValue: authStoreMock },
      ],
    });
    store = TestBed.inject(WwellIrStore);
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    jest.useRealTimers();
  });

  /** Seeds `irs` through the store's own public read path, so the status assertions below run
   *  against a record the store actually holds rather than a detached object. */
  function seedIrs(ir: IntegratedRequirement): void {
    serviceMock.getIrs.mockReturnValue(of([ir]));
    store.loadIrs();
  }

  it('runs the three steps in order — mint id, trigger SAP, record a local "in progress" placeholder — without ever asking SAP for a real status', () => {
    const ir = buildIntegratedRequirement({ status: 'in-progress', irSeq: 9001 });
    seedIrs(ir);
    serviceMock.getNextSapWorkflowSeq.mockReturnValue(of(6));
    sapServiceMock.trigger.mockReturnValue(of({ message: 'Agents were successfully resolved' }));
    serviceMock.updateIrSapWorkflow.mockReturnValue(of(undefined));

    store.submitIrForReview(ir);

    const sentRequest = sapServiceMock.trigger.mock.calls[0][0];
    expect(sentRequest).toMatchObject({
      process_id: WWELL_IR_SAP_PROCESS_ID,
      scenario_id: WWELL_IR_SAP_SCENARIO_ID,
      initiator_network_id: 'junedalam',
      requester_network_id: 'junedalam',
      workflow_id: '6',
      body: [],
    });
    expect(sentRequest).not.toHaveProperty('attachment_files');
    // Submitting must stay a fast, local-only write — no round-trip to SAP's own GET /display.
    expect(sapServiceMock.getWorkflowStatus).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(serviceMock.updateIrSapWorkflow).toHaveBeenCalledWith(9001, 6, WWELL_IR_SAP_WF_STATUS_IN_PROGRESS);
    expect(store.irs()[0].status).toBe('submit-for-review');
    expect(store.irs()[0].sapWfId).toBe(6);
    expect(store.irs()[0].sapWfStatusText).toBe(WWELL_IR_SAP_WF_STATUS_IN_PROGRESS);
    expect(store.updatingIrStatus()).toBe(false);
    expect(store.sapTriggerError()).toBeNull();
  });

  /** The header is stamped only for a workflow SAP actually accepted — a failed trigger must leave
   *  sap_wf_id/sap_wf_status untouched rather than recording a workflow that does not exist in SAP. */
  it('does not touch the IR header at all when the SAP trigger fails', () => {
    const ir = buildIntegratedRequirement({ status: 'in-progress', irSeq: 9001 });
    seedIrs(ir);
    serviceMock.getNextSapWorkflowSeq.mockReturnValue(of(6));
    sapServiceMock.trigger.mockReturnValue(throwError(() => new Error('SAP unreachable')));

    store.submitIrForReview(ir);

    expect(serviceMock.updateIrSapWorkflow).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(store.irs()[0].status).toBe('in-progress');
    expect(store.sapTriggerError()).toBe('SAP unreachable');
    expect(store.updatingIrStatus()).toBe(false);
  });

  it('surfaces the SAP 422 validation detail message instead of a generic HTTP failure text', () => {
    const ir = buildIntegratedRequirement({ status: 'in-progress', irSeq: 9001 });
    serviceMock.getNextSapWorkflowSeq.mockReturnValue(of(6));
    sapServiceMock.trigger.mockReturnValue(
      throwError(
        () =>
          new HttpErrorResponse({
            status: 422,
            error: { detail: [{ loc: ['body', 0], msg: 'field required', type: 'missing', input: '', ctx: {} }] },
          }),
      ),
    );

    store.submitIrForReview(ir);

    expect(store.sapTriggerError()).toBe('field required');
  });

  /** A refused or offline connection still arrives as an HttpErrorResponse, but with status 0 and
   *  `error` holding the transport's own TypeError rather than the backend's ApiResponse envelope.
   *  Reproduced live by pointing sapServiceUrl at a dead port: the toast read "Failed to fetch",
   *  which tells the user nothing about what failed or what to do. */
  it('uses its own copy when the SAP call never reached a server', () => {
    const ir = buildIntegratedRequirement({ status: 'in-progress', irSeq: 9001 });
    serviceMock.getNextSapWorkflowSeq.mockReturnValue(of(6));
    sapServiceMock.trigger.mockReturnValue(
      throwError(
        () =>
          new HttpErrorResponse({
            status: 0,
            statusText: 'Unknown Error',
            error: new TypeError('Failed to fetch'),
          }),
      ),
    );

    store.submitIrForReview(ir);

    expect(store.sapTriggerError()).toBe('Failed to trigger the SAP review workflow');
  });

  /** An unsaved draft has no ir_seq, so there would be nothing to record the SAP outcome against
   *  — the guard must refuse before SAP is called, not after. */
  it('refuses to submit an IR that has never been saved, without calling SAP', () => {
    const ir = buildIntegratedRequirement({ status: 'draft', irSeq: null });

    store.submitIrForReview(ir);

    expect(serviceMock.getNextSapWorkflowSeq).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(sapServiceMock.trigger).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(serviceMock.updateIrSapWorkflow).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(store.sapTriggerError()).toContain('Save the integrated requirement');
    expect(store.updatingIrStatus()).toBe(false);
  });

  it('never calls the SAP trigger when minting a workflow id fails', () => {
    const ir = buildIntegratedRequirement({ status: 'in-progress', irSeq: 9001 });
    serviceMock.getNextSapWorkflowSeq.mockReturnValue(throwError(() => new Error('sequence exhausted')));

    store.submitIrForReview(ir);

    expect(sapServiceMock.trigger).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(serviceMock.updateIrSapWorkflow).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(store.sapTriggerError()).toBe('sequence exhausted');
    expect(store.updatingIrStatus()).toBe(false);
  });

  it('reports a distinct error, and does not mark the record submitted, when recording the outcome fails', () => {
    const ir = buildIntegratedRequirement({ status: 'in-progress', irSeq: 9001 });
    seedIrs(ir);
    serviceMock.getNextSapWorkflowSeq.mockReturnValue(of(6));
    sapServiceMock.trigger.mockReturnValue(of({ message: 'Agents were successfully resolved' }));
    serviceMock.updateIrSapWorkflow.mockReturnValue(throwError(() => new Error('header locked')));

    store.submitIrForReview(ir);

    expect(store.irs()[0].status).toBe('in-progress');
    expect(store.sapTriggerError()).toBe('header locked');
    expect(store.updatingIrStatus()).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('WwellIrStore - loadIrDetail (approval timeline)', () => {
  let store: InstanceType<typeof WwellIrStore>;

  const workflowStatusWithOneApprovedStep = {
    header: {
      process_name: 'Geological Operations',
      initiator_id: 'ALANMX10',
      requester_id: 'ALANMX10',
      date_initiated: '2026-09-15',
      time_initiated: '11:30:35',
      wf_status: 'COMPLETE',
      wf_status_text: 'Approved',
    },
    remarks: [],
    reject_remarks: [],
    approval_chain: [
      {
        level: '01',
        received_date: '2026-09-15',
        received_time: '11:30:39',
        approver_network_id: null,
        approver_name: 'Mohammad Juned Alam',
        approver_title: 'Initiator',
        approver_category: 'Reviewer',
        response_date: '2026-09-15',
        response_time: '11:45:00',
        response: 'APPROVED',
      },
    ],
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
    TestBed.configureTestingModule({
      providers: [
        WwellIrStore,
        { provide: WwellIrService, useValue: serviceMock },
        { provide: SapService, useValue: sapServiceMock },
        { provide: AuthStore, useValue: authStoreMock },
      ],
    });
    store = TestBed.inject(WwellIrStore);
  });

  function seedIrs(ir: IntegratedRequirement): void {
    serviceMock.getIrs.mockReturnValue(of([ir]));
    store.loadIrs();
  }

  /** Only the detail fetch's exad_gwd_ir_header block carries sap_wf_id (see
   *  wwell-ir-summary.mapper.ts) — this is what lets loadIrDetail resolve a real one to look SAP
   *  up by. */
  it('looks up the real SAP workflow once a detail fetch resolves a sap_wf_id', () => {
    const ir = buildIntegratedRequirement({ irSeq: 9001, sapWfId: null });
    seedIrs(ir);
    serviceMock.getIrDetailBlocks.mockReturnValue(
      of([{ table: 'exad_gwd_ir_header', data: [{ sap_wf_id: 11033 }] }]),
    );
    sapServiceMock.getWorkflowStatus.mockReturnValue(of(workflowStatusWithOneApprovedStep));

    store.loadIrDetail(9001);

    expect(sapServiceMock.getWorkflowStatus).toHaveBeenCalledWith(WWELL_IR_SAP_PROCESS_ID, 11033);
    expect(store.irs()[0].sapWfId).toBe(11033);
    expect(store.irs()[0].chain).toEqual([{ role: 'Initiator', name: 'Mohammad Juned Alam', status: 'approved', date: '2026-09-15' }]);
    expect(store.irApprovalTimelineLoading()).toBe(false);
  });

  /** An IR that has never been through Submit for Review has no SAP workflow to look up — calling
   *  SAP with nothing to ask about would be meaningless (and, per the mock's own validation, a
   *  422). */
  it('never calls SAP display for an IR with no sap_wf_id', () => {
    const ir = buildIntegratedRequirement({ irSeq: 9001, sapWfId: null });
    seedIrs(ir);
    serviceMock.getIrDetailBlocks.mockReturnValue(of([{ table: 'exad_gwd_ir_header', data: [{}] }]));

    store.loadIrDetail(9001);

    expect(sapServiceMock.getWorkflowStatus).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(store.irs()[0].sapWfId).toBeNull();
    expect(store.irApprovalTimelineLoading()).toBe(false);
  });

  it('surfaces a SAP display failure as irApprovalTimelineError without touching the chain', () => {
    const ir = buildIntegratedRequirement({ irSeq: 9001, sapWfId: null, chain: [{ role: 'Existing', name: 'Someone', status: 'pending', date: null }] });
    seedIrs(ir);
    serviceMock.getIrDetailBlocks.mockReturnValue(
      of([{ table: 'exad_gwd_ir_header', data: [{ sap_wf_id: 11033 }] }]),
    );
    sapServiceMock.getWorkflowStatus.mockReturnValue(throwError(() => new Error('SAP unreachable')));

    store.loadIrDetail(9001);

    expect(store.irApprovalTimelineError()).toBe('SAP unreachable');
    expect(store.irs()[0].chain).toEqual([{ role: 'Existing', name: 'Someone', status: 'pending', date: null }]);
  });

  /** The manual refresh action (Approval Workflow sidebar / detail header) — lets the UI pull
   *  SAP's real current status/chain for an already-selected record on demand, replacing whatever
   *  submitIrForReview's local "in progress" placeholder (or a stale earlier fetch) last left. */
  it('refreshIrApprovalStatus re-fetches SAP display and updates status/chain for a known sapWfId', () => {
    const ir = buildIntegratedRequirement({ irSeq: 9001, sapWfId: 11033, status: 'submit-for-review', sapWfStatusText: 'In Progress' });
    seedIrs(ir);
    sapServiceMock.getWorkflowStatus.mockReturnValue(of(workflowStatusWithOneApprovedStep));

    store.refreshIrApprovalStatus(9001, 11033);

    expect(sapServiceMock.getWorkflowStatus).toHaveBeenCalledWith(WWELL_IR_SAP_PROCESS_ID, 11033);
    expect(store.irs()[0].sapWfStatusText).toBe('Approved');
    expect(store.irs()[0].chain).toEqual([{ role: 'Initiator', name: 'Mohammad Juned Alam', status: 'approved', date: '2026-09-15' }]);
  });

  it('refreshIrApprovalStatus is a no-op for a record with no sapWfId — nothing in SAP to ask about', () => {
    const ir = buildIntegratedRequirement({ irSeq: 9001, sapWfId: null });
    seedIrs(ir);

    store.refreshIrApprovalStatus(9001, null);

    expect(sapServiceMock.getWorkflowStatus).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```
