```html
                  <mat-icon>edit</mat-icon>
                </button>
                <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn ire__edit-btn--danger" aria-label="Reset Program Details" (click)="resetProgramSection()">
                  <mat-icon>restart_alt</mat-icon>
                </button>
              </div>
            }
          </div>

          @if (programEdit.control(); as ctrl) {
            <div class="ire__edit-block" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              <app-dynamic-section [section]="programSection" [control]="ctrl" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
              <div class="ire__edit-actions">
                <button type="button" mat-button (click)="programEdit.close()">Cancel</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          } @else {
            <dl class="ire__view-grid" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              @for (row of programRows(); track row.label) {
                <div class="ire__view-field" [attr.data-tone]="row.tone">
                  <dt>{{ row.label }}</dt>
                  <dd><app-wwell-ir-kv-value [row]="row" /></dd>
                </div>
              }
            </dl>
          }
        </section>

        <!-- 2.2 Geologist Contact -->
        <section #sectionEl id="ire-sec-geologist" class="ire__section ire__section--sub">
          <div class="ire__section-head">
            <h3 class="ire__subsection-title">2.2 Geologist Contact</h3>
            @if (!geologistContactEdit.isEditing()) {
              <div class="ire__section-actions">
                <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn" aria-label="Edit Geologist Contact" (click)="geologistContactEdit.open()">
                  <mat-icon>edit</mat-icon>
                </button>
                <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn ire__edit-btn--danger" aria-label="Reset Geologist Contact" (click)="resetGeologistSection()">
                  <mat-icon>restart_alt</mat-icon>
                </button>
              </div>
            }
          </div>

          @if (geologistContactEdit.control(); as ctrl) {
            <div class="ire__edit-block" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              <app-dynamic-section [section]="geologistSection" [control]="ctrl" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
              <div class="ire__edit-actions">
                <button type="button" mat-button (click)="geologistContactEdit.close()">Cancel</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          } @else {
            <dl class="ire__view-grid" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              @for (row of geologistContactRows(); track row.label) {
                <div class="ire__view-field" [attr.data-tone]="row.tone">
                  <dt>{{ row.label }}</dt>
                  <dd><app-wwell-ir-kv-value [row]="row" /></dd>
                </div>
              }
            </dl>
          }
        </section>
        </div>
        }
      </div>
      </div>
      </div>

      <!-- 3.0 Horizons -->
      <div class="ire__tl-row">
      <div class="ire__tl-rail">
        <span class="ire__tl-dot" [class.ire__tl-dot--done]="timelineStepStatus('horizons') === 'completed'" [class.ire__tl-dot--active]="viewMode() === 'timeline' && isTimelineStepExpanded('horizons')">
          @if (timelineStepStatus('horizons') === 'completed') {
            <mat-icon>check</mat-icon>
          }
        </span>
      </div>
      <div class="ire__tl-content">
      <div class="ire__group">
        <div
          class="ire__section-head"
          role="button"
          tabindex="0"
          (click)="toggleTimelineStep('horizons')"
          (keydown.enter)="toggleTimelineStep('horizons')"
          (keydown.space)="$event.preventDefault(); toggleTimelineStep('horizons')"
        >
          <h2 class="ire__section-title">3.0 Horizons</h2>
          @if (viewMode() === 'timeline') {
            <span class="ire__tl-status" [class.ire__tl-status--done]="timelineStepStatus('horizons') === 'completed'">
              {{ timelineStepStatus("horizons") === "completed" ? "Completed" : "Pending" }}
            </span>
          }
        </div>

        @if (isTimelineStepExpanded('horizons')) {
        <div class="ire__step-body" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
        <!-- 3.1 Horizons and 3.2 Remarks are independent edit units — opening one's pencil must not
             flip the other into its edit form (see formationTopsEdit's own doc comment in the .ts). -->
        <section #sectionEl id="ire-sec-horizons" class="ire__section ire__section--sub">
          <div class="ire__section-head">
            <h3 class="ire__subsection-title">3.1 Horizons</h3>
            @if (!formationTopsEdit.visible()) {
              <div class="ire__section-actions">
                <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn" aria-label="Add horizon" matTooltip="Add horizon" (click)="formationTopAdd.open()">
                  <mat-icon>add</mat-icon>
                </button>
                <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn" aria-label="Edit Horizons" (click)="formationTopAdd.close(); formationTopsEdit.open()">
                  <mat-icon>edit</mat-icon>
                </button>
              </div>
            }
          </div>

          @if (formationTopAdd.control(); as addCtrl) {
            <div class="ire__edit-block ire__add-block" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              <app-dynamic-section [section]="formationTopAddSection" [control]="addCtrl" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
              <div class="ire__edit-actions">
                <button type="button" mat-button (click)="formationTopAdd.close()">Cancel</button>
                <button type="button" mat-flat-button color="primary" [disabled]="addCtrl.invalid" (click)="addFormationTopRow()">Add horizon</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          }

          @if (formationTopsEdit.visible() && formationTopsEdit.control(); as ctrl) {
            <div class="ire__edit-block" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              <app-dynamic-section
                [section]="formationTopsSection"
                [control]="ctrl"
                mode="edit"
                [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass"
              />
              <div class="ire__edit-actions">
                <button type="button" mat-button (click)="formationTopsEdit.close()">Cancel</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          } @else {
            <div class="ire__view-table-wrap" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              <table class="ire__view-table">
                <thead>
                  <tr>
                    <th>Formation</th>
                    <th>Depth (ft bgl)</th>
                    <th>Sub-sea (ft asl)</th>
                    <th class="ire__view-table-actions-col">Actions</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  @for (top of formationTopsRows(); track top.name; let i = $index) {
                    <tr appWwellCollapseAnimate>
                      @if (formationTopsInlineEdit.isEditing(i) && formationTopsRowGroup(i); as rowGroup) {
                        <td>
                          <app-dynamic-field [config]="formationTopsFieldConfig('name')" [control]="rowFieldControl(rowGroup, 'name')" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
                        </td>
                        <td>
                          <app-dynamic-field [config]="formationTopsFieldConfig('depthFt')" [control]="rowFieldControl(rowGroup, 'depthFt')" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
                        </td>
                        <td>{{ subSeaFt(rowFieldControl(rowGroup, 'depthFt').value) ?? "—" }}</td>
                        <td class="ire__row-actions">
                          <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__row-action-btn" aria-label="Save formation top" [disabled]="rowGroup.invalid" (click)="saveFormationTopRowInline()">
                            <mat-icon>check</mat-icon>
                          </button>
                          <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__row-action-btn" aria-label="Cancel formation top edit" (click)="cancelFormationTopRowInline()">
                            <mat-icon>close</mat-icon>
                          </button>
                        </td>
                      } @else {
                        <td>{{ top.name }}</td>
                        <td>{{ top.depthFt }}</td>
                        <td>{{ subSeaFt(top.depthFt) ?? "—" }}</td>
                        <td class="ire__row-actions">
                          <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__row-action-btn" [attr.aria-label]="'Edit formation top ' + top.name" (click)="editFormationTopRowInline(i)">
                            <mat-icon>edit</mat-icon>
                          </button>
                          <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__row-action-btn ire__row-action-btn--danger" [attr.aria-label]="'Delete formation top ' + top.name" (click)="deleteFormationTopRow(i)">
                            <mat-icon>delete</mat-icon>
                          </button>
                        </td>
                      }
                    </tr>
                  } @empty {
                    <tr>
                      <td colspan="4" class="ire__view-table-empty">No formation tops recorded.</td>
                    </tr>
                  }
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          }
        </section>

        <section #sectionEl id="ire-sec-horizons-remarks" class="ire__section ire__section--sub">
          <div class="ire__section-head">
            <h3 class="ire__subsection-title">3.2 Remarks</h3>
            @if (!topsRemarksEdit.visible()) {
              <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn" aria-label="Edit Formation Tops Remarks" (click)="topsRemarksEdit.open()">
                <mat-icon>edit</mat-icon>
              </button>
            }
          </div>

          @if (topsRemarksEdit.visible() && topsRemarksEdit.control(); as ctrl) {
            <div class="ire__edit-block" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              <app-dynamic-section
                [section]="topsRemarksSection"
                [control]="ctrl"
                mode="edit"
                [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass"
              />
              <div class="ire__edit-actions">
                <button type="button" mat-button (click)="topsRemarksEdit.close()">Cancel</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          } @else if (ir().topsRemarks) {
            <p class="ire__remarks" appWwellCollapseAnimate>{{ ir().topsRemarks }}</p>
          } @else {
            <p class="ire__empty-text" appWwellCollapseAnimate>No remarks.</p>
          }
        </section>
        </div>
        }
      </div>
      </div>
      </div>

      <!-- 4.0 Casing -->
      <div class="ire__tl-row">
      <div class="ire__tl-rail">
        <span class="ire__tl-dot" [class.ire__tl-dot--done]="timelineStepStatus('casing') === 'completed'" [class.ire__tl-dot--active]="viewMode() === 'timeline' && isTimelineStepExpanded('casing')">
          @if (timelineStepStatus('casing') === 'completed') {
            <mat-icon>check</mat-icon>
          }
        </span>
      </div>
      <div class="ire__tl-content">
      <section #sectionEl id="ire-sec-casing" class="ire__section">
        <div
          class="ire__section-head"
          role="button"
          tabindex="0"
          (click)="toggleTimelineStep('casing')"
          (keydown.enter)="toggleTimelineStep('casing')"
          (keydown.space)="$event.preventDefault(); toggleTimelineStep('casing')"
        >
          <h2 class="ire__section-title">4.0 Casing</h2>
          @if (viewMode() === 'timeline') {
            <span class="ire__tl-status" [class.ire__tl-status--done]="timelineStepStatus('casing') === 'completed'">
              {{ timelineStepStatus("casing") === "completed" ? "Completed" : "Pending" }}
            </span>
          }
          @if (!casingEdit.visible() && isTimelineStepExpanded('casing')) {
            <div class="ire__section-actions">
              <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn" aria-label="Add casing" matTooltip="Add casing" (click)="$event.stopPropagation(); casingAdd.open()">
                <mat-icon>add</mat-icon>
              </button>
              <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn" aria-label="Edit Casing" (click)="$event.stopPropagation(); casingAdd.close(); casingEdit.open()">
                <mat-icon>edit</mat-icon>
              </button>
            </div>
          }
        </div>

        @if (isTimelineStepExpanded('casing')) {
        <div class="ire__step-body" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
        @if (casingAdd.control(); as addCtrl) {
          <div class="ire__edit-block ire__add-block" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
            <app-dynamic-section [section]="casingAddSection" [control]="addCtrl" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
            <div class="ire__edit-actions">
              <button type="button" mat-button (click)="casingAdd.close()">Cancel</button>
              <button type="button" mat-flat-button color="primary" [disabled]="addCtrl.invalid" (click)="addCasingRow()">Add casing</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        }
        @if (casingEdit.visible() && casingEdit.control(); as ctrl) {
          <div class="ire__edit-block" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
            <app-dynamic-section [section]="casingSection" [control]="ctrl" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
            <div class="ire__edit-actions">
              <button type="button" mat-button (click)="casingEdit.close()">Cancel</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        } @else {
          <div class="ire__view-table-wrap" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
            <table class="ire__view-table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Type</th>
                  <th>Size</th>
                  <th>Depth (ft bgl)</th>
                  <th>Remarks</th>
                  <th class="ire__view-table-actions-col">Actions</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                @for (row of casingRows(); track row.type + row.depthFtBgl; let i = $index) {
                  <tr appWwellCollapseAnimate>
                    @if (casingInlineEdit.isEditing(i) && casingRowGroup(i); as rowGroup) {
                      <td>
                        <app-dynamic-field [config]="casingFieldConfig('type')" [control]="rowFieldControl(rowGroup, 'type')" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
                      </td>
                      <td>
                        <app-dynamic-field [config]="casingFieldConfig('sizeIn')" [control]="rowFieldControl(rowGroup, 'sizeIn')" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
                      </td>
                      <td>
                        <app-dynamic-field [config]="casingFieldConfig('depthFtBgl')" [control]="rowFieldControl(rowGroup, 'depthFtBgl')" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
                      </td>
                      <td>
                        <app-dynamic-field [config]="casingFieldConfig('remarks')" [control]="rowFieldControl(rowGroup, 'remarks')" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
                      </td>
                      <td class="ire__row-actions">
                        <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__row-action-btn" aria-label="Save casing row" [disabled]="rowGroup.invalid" (click)="saveCasingRowInline()">
                          <mat-icon>check</mat-icon>
                        </button>
                        <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__row-action-btn" aria-label="Cancel casing row edit" (click)="cancelCasingRowInline()">
                          <mat-icon>close</mat-icon>
                        </button>
                      </td>
                    } @else {
                      <td>{{ row.type }}</td>
                      <td>{{ row.sizeIn }}</td>
                      <td>{{ row.depthFtBgl }}</td>
                      <td>{{ row.remarks || "—" }}</td>
                      <td class="ire__row-actions">
                        <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__row-action-btn" [attr.aria-label]="'Edit casing row ' + (i + 1)" (click)="editCasingRowInline(i)">
                          <mat-icon>edit</mat-icon>
                        </button>
                        <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__row-action-btn ire__row-action-btn--danger" [attr.aria-label]="'Delete casing row ' + (i + 1)" (click)="deleteCasingRow(i)">
                          <mat-icon>delete</mat-icon>
                        </button>
                      </td>
                    }
                  </tr>
                } @empty {
                  <tr>
                    <td colspan="5" class="ire__view-table-empty">No casing recorded.</td>
                  </tr>
                }
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>

          @if (casingSizeWarnings().length) {
            <ul class="ire__casing-warnings">
              @for (warning of casingSizeWarnings(); track warning.rowIndex) {
                <li class="ire__casing-warning">
                  <mat-icon>warning</mat-icon>
                  <span>
                    Casing #{{ warning.rowIndex + 1 }} ({{ warning.size }}) is deeper and wider than
                    Casing #{{ warning.conflictsWithRowIndex + 1 }} ({{ warning.conflictsWithSize }}) — the
                    diagram will draw it over that shallower casing, which may hide it.
                  </span>
                </li>
              }
            </ul>
          }
        }
        </div>
        }
      </section>
      </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Wellbore preview — read-only, always derived from the persisted record. Sections view
           keeps it inline right here between Casing and Hydrogeology, unchanged. Timeline view
           doesn't render it inline at all — it isn't tied to any one lifecycle step, so it's
           pinned in a sticky sidebar instead (see `wellborePreviewTpl` usage further down),
           staying visible no matter which step is open or how far the steps are scrolled. -->
      @if (viewMode() === 'sections') {
        <ng-container [ngTemplateOutlet]="wellborePreviewTpl" />
      }

      <ng-template #wellborePreviewTpl let-compact="compact">
        <app-wwell-ir-wellbore-panel
          [diagramData]="wellboreDiagramData()"
          [hasData]="hasWellboreData()"
          [hasTargetDepth]="hasTargetDepth()"
          [compact]="!!compact"
          [layout]="compact ? sidebarDiagramLayout : previewDiagramLayout"
        />
      </ng-template>

      <!-- 5.0 Hydrogeology -->
      <div class="ire__tl-row">
      <div class="ire__tl-rail">
        <span class="ire__tl-dot" [class.ire__tl-dot--done]="timelineStepStatus('hydrogeology') === 'completed'" [class.ire__tl-dot--active]="viewMode() === 'timeline' && isTimelineStepExpanded('hydrogeology')">
          @if (timelineStepStatus('hydrogeology') === 'completed') {
            <mat-icon>check</mat-icon>
          }
        </span>
      </div>
      <div class="ire__tl-content">
      <section #sectionEl id="ire-sec-hydrogeology" class="ire__section">
        <div
          class="ire__section-head"
          role="button"
          tabindex="0"
          (click)="toggleTimelineStep('hydrogeology')"
          (keydown.enter)="toggleTimelineStep('hydrogeology')"
          (keydown.space)="$event.preventDefault(); toggleTimelineStep('hydrogeology')"
        >
          <h2 class="ire__section-title">5.0 Hydrogeology</h2>
          @if (viewMode() === 'timeline') {
            <span class="ire__tl-status" [class.ire__tl-status--done]="timelineStepStatus('hydrogeology') === 'completed'">
              {{ timelineStepStatus("hydrogeology") === "completed" ? "Completed" : "Pending" }}
            </span>
          }
          @if (!hydrogeologyEdit.isEditing() && isTimelineStepExpanded('hydrogeology')) {
            <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn" aria-label="Edit Hydrogeology" (click)="$event.stopPropagation(); hydrogeologyEdit.open()">
              <mat-icon>edit</mat-icon>
            </button>
          }
        </div>

        @if (isTimelineStepExpanded('hydrogeology')) {
        <div class="ire__step-body" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
        @if (hydrogeologyEdit.control(); as ctrl) {
          <div class="ire__edit-block" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
            <app-dynamic-section [section]="hydrogeologySection" [control]="ctrl" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
            <div class="ire__edit-actions">
              <button type="button" mat-button (click)="hydrogeologyEdit.close()">Cancel</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        } @else {
          <dl class="ire__view-grid" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
            @for (row of hydrogeologyRows(); track row.label) {
              <div class="ire__view-field" [attr.data-tone]="row.tone">
                <dt>{{ row.label }}</dt>
                <dd><app-wwell-ir-kv-value [row]="row" /></dd>
              </div>
            }
          </dl>
        }
        </div>
        }
      </section>
      </div>
      </div>

      <!-- 6.0 Area Water Wells -->
      <div class="ire__tl-row">
      <div class="ire__tl-rail">
        <span class="ire__tl-dot" [class.ire__tl-dot--done]="timelineStepStatus('areaWaterWells') === 'completed'" [class.ire__tl-dot--active]="viewMode() === 'timeline' && isTimelineStepExpanded('areaWaterWells')">
          @if (timelineStepStatus('areaWaterWells') === 'completed') {
            <mat-icon>check</mat-icon>
          }
        </span>
      </div>
      <div class="ire__tl-content">
      <div class="ire__group">
        <div
          class="ire__section-head"
          role="button"
          tabindex="0"
          (click)="toggleTimelineStep('areaWaterWells')"
          (keydown.enter)="toggleTimelineStep('areaWaterWells')"
          (keydown.space)="$event.preventDefault(); toggleTimelineStep('areaWaterWells')"
        >
          <h2 class="ire__section-title">6.0 Area Water Wells</h2>
          @if (viewMode() === 'timeline') {
            <span class="ire__tl-status" [class.ire__tl-status--done]="timelineStepStatus('areaWaterWells') === 'completed'">
              {{ timelineStepStatus("areaWaterWells") === "completed" ? "Completed" : "Pending" }}
            </span>
          }
        </div>

        @if (isTimelineStepExpanded('areaWaterWells')) {
        <div class="ire__step-body" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
        <!-- 6.1 / 6.2 Area Water Wells + Remarks — one shared edit unit, matching the domain model (offsetWellsRemarks lives beside offsetWells on the same record). -->
        <section #sectionEl id="ire-sec-offset-wells" class="ire__section ire__section--sub">
          <div class="ire__section-head">
            <h3 class="ire__subsection-title">6.1 Area Water Wells</h3>
            @if (!offsetWellsEdit.visible()) {
              <div class="ire__section-actions">
                <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn" aria-label="Add area water well" matTooltip="Add area water well" (click)="offsetWellAdd.open()">
                  <mat-icon>add</mat-icon>
                </button>
                <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__edit-btn" aria-label="Edit Area Water Wells" (click)="offsetWellAdd.close(); offsetWellsEdit.open()">
                  <mat-icon>edit</mat-icon>
                </button>
              </div>
            }
          </div>

          @if (offsetWellAdd.control(); as addCtrl) {
            <div class="ire__edit-block ire__add-block" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              <app-dynamic-section [section]="offsetWellAddSection" [control]="addCtrl" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
              <div class="ire__edit-actions">
                <button type="button" mat-button (click)="offsetWellAdd.close()">Cancel</button>
                <button type="button" mat-flat-button color="primary" [disabled]="addCtrl.invalid" (click)="addOffsetWellRow()">Add well</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          }

          @if (offsetWellsEdit.visible() && offsetWellsEdit.control(); as ctrl) {
            <div class="ire__edit-block" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              <app-dynamic-section
                [section]="offsetWellsSection"
                [control]="offsetWellsArrayControl(ctrl)"
                mode="edit"
                [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass"
              />
            </div>
          } @else {
            <div class="ire__view-table-wrap" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              <table class="ire__view-table">
                <thead>
                  <tr>
                    <th>EP A Num</th>
                    <th>Aquifer</th>
                    <th>Distance (km)</th>
                    <th>Rate (GPM)</th>
                    <th class="ire__view-table-actions-col">Actions</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  @for (row of offsetWellsRows(); track row.epANum; let i = $index) {
                    <tr appWwellCollapseAnimate>
                      @if (offsetWellsInlineEdit.isEditing(i) && offsetWellsRowGroup(i); as rowGroup) {
                        <td>
                          <app-dynamic-field [config]="offsetWellsFieldConfig('epANum')" [control]="rowFieldControl(rowGroup, 'epANum')" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
                        </td>
                        <td>
                          <app-dynamic-field [config]="offsetWellsFieldConfig('aquifer')" [control]="rowFieldControl(rowGroup, 'aquifer')" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
                        </td>
                        <td>
                          <app-dynamic-field [config]="offsetWellsFieldConfig('distanceKm')" [control]="rowFieldControl(rowGroup, 'distanceKm')" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
                        </td>
                        <td>
                          <app-dynamic-field [config]="offsetWellsFieldConfig('rateGpm')" [control]="rowFieldControl(rowGroup, 'rateGpm')" mode="edit" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass" />
                        </td>
                        <td class="ire__row-actions">
                          <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__row-action-btn" aria-label="Save offset well" [disabled]="rowGroup.invalid" (click)="saveOffsetWellRowInline()">
                            <mat-icon>check</mat-icon>
                          </button>
                          <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__row-action-btn" aria-label="Cancel offset well edit" (click)="cancelOffsetWellRowInline()">
                            <mat-icon>close</mat-icon>
                          </button>
                        </td>
                      } @else {
                        <td>{{ row.epANum }}</td>
                        <td>{{ row.aquifer || "—" }}</td>
                        <td>{{ row.distanceKm ?? "—" }}</td>
                        <td>{{ row.rateGpm ?? "—" }}</td>
                        <td class="ire__row-actions">
                          <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__row-action-btn" [attr.aria-label]="'Edit offset well ' + row.epANum" (click)="editOffsetWellRowInline(i)">
                            <mat-icon>edit</mat-icon>
                          </button>
                          <button type="button" mat-icon-button class="ire__row-action-btn ire__row-action-btn--danger" [attr.aria-label]="'Delete offset well ' + row.epANum" (click)="deleteOffsetWellRow(i)">
                            <mat-icon>delete</mat-icon>
                          </button>
                        </td>
                      }
                    </tr>
                  } @empty {
                    <tr>
                      <td colspan="5" class="ire__view-table-empty">No offset wells recorded.</td>
                    </tr>
                  }
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          }
        </section>

        <section #sectionEl id="ire-sec-offset-remarks" class="ire__section ire__section--sub">
          <h3 class="ire__subsection-title">6.2 Remarks</h3>

          @if (offsetWellsEdit.visible() && offsetWellsEdit.control(); as ctrl) {
            <div class="ire__edit-block" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
              <app-dynamic-section
                [section]="offsetRemarksSection"
                [control]="offsetWellsRemarksControl(ctrl)"
                mode="edit"
                [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass"
              />
              <div class="ire__edit-actions">
                <button type="button" mat-button (click)="offsetWellsEdit.close()">Cancel</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          } @else if (ir().offsetWellsRemarks) {
            <p class="ire__remarks" appWwellCollapseAnimate>{{ ir().offsetWellsRemarks }}</p>
          } @else {
            <p class="ire__empty-text" appWwellCollapseAnimate>No remarks.</p>
          }
        </section>
        </div>
        }
      </div>
      </div>
      </div>

      <!-- 7.0 Attachments -->
      <div class="ire__tl-row">
      <div class="ire__tl-rail">
        <span class="ire__tl-dot" [class.ire__tl-dot--done]="timelineStepStatus('attachments') === 'completed'" [class.ire__tl-dot--active]="viewMode() === 'timeline' && isTimelineStepExpanded('attachments')">
          @if (timelineStepStatus('attachments') === 'completed') {
            <mat-icon>check</mat-icon>
          }
        </span>
      </div>
      <div class="ire__tl-content">
      <section class="ire__section">
        <div
          class="ire__section-head"
          role="button"
          tabindex="0"
          (click)="toggleTimelineStep('attachments')"
          (keydown.enter)="toggleTimelineStep('attachments')"
          (keydown.space)="$event.preventDefault(); toggleTimelineStep('attachments')"
        >
          <h2 class="ire__section-title">7.0 Attachments</h2>
          @if (viewMode() === 'timeline') {
            <span class="ire__tl-status" [class.ire__tl-status--done]="timelineStepStatus('attachments') === 'completed'">
              {{ timelineStepStatus("attachments") === "completed" ? "Completed" : "Pending" }}
            </span>
          }
        </div>

        @if (isTimelineStepExpanded('attachments')) {
        <div class="ire__step-body" appWwellCollapseAnimate>

        @if (store.attachmentError(); as error) {
          <p class="ire__attachment-error" role="alert" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
            <mat-icon>error_outline</mat-icon>
            {{ error }}
          </p>
        }

        <!-- One #sectionEl per slot, index-aligned with the 7.1/7.2 rail leaves (see
             wwell-ir-section-rail.config.ts) exactly as 6.1/6.2 above are. -->
        @for (slot of attachmentSlots; track slot.slot) {
          <section #sectionEl [id]="'ire-sec-' + slot.slot" class="ire__section ire__section--sub">
            <div class="ire__section-head">
              <h3 class="ire__subsection-title">
                <mat-icon class="ire__attachment-slot-icon">{{ slot.icon }}</mat-icon>
                {{ slot.number }} {{ slot.label }}
                @if (slot.required) {
                  <span class="ire__attachment-required" aria-hidden="true">*</span>
                }
              </h3>
            </div>

            @if (hasAttachment(slot.slot)) {
              <!-- One row for both cases: a file already stored server-side, or one picked on a New
                   IR and waiting for the first save. The actions are identical either way, so the
                   only difference is where the name/size come from and the "pending" note below.
                   A staged file wins over the stored one — on Replace both exist until the upload
                   lands, and the row must describe what View/Upload will act on (the new file). -->
              @let stored = attachmentFor(slot.slot);
              @let pending = pendingAttachmentFor(slot.slot);

              <div class="ire__attachment-file" [class.ire__attachment-file--pending]="pending" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
                <mat-icon class="ire__attachment-file-icon">
                  {{ attachmentIcon(pending ? pending.type : stored!.contentType) }}
                </mat-icon>
                <span class="ire__attachment-file-meta">
                  <span class="ire__attachment-file-name">{{ pending ? pending.name : stored!.fileName }}</span>
                  <span class="ire__attachment-file-size">
                    {{ attachmentSizeLabel(pending ? pending.size : stored!.sizeBytes) }}
                    @if (pending) {
                      · {{ canAttach() ? "not uploaded yet" : "uploads when you save" }}
                    }
                  </span>
                </span>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  mat-icon-button
                  class="ire__attachment-action"
                  [attr.aria-label]="'View ' + slot.label"
                  matTooltip="View"

                  matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip"

                  matTooltipPosition="above"
                  (click)="viewAttachment(slot.slot)"
                >
                  <mat-icon>visibility</mat-icon>
                </button>

                <!-- Only while the file is still staged: once it's on the server there is nothing
                     left to send, so the icon disappears rather than sitting there inert. -->
                @if (pending) {
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    mat-icon-button
                    class="ire__attachment-action ire__attachment-action--upload"
                    [attr.aria-label]="'Upload ' + slot.label"
                    matTooltip="Upload"

                    matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip"

                    matTooltipPosition="above"
                    [disabled]="isAttachmentBusy(slot.slot)"
                    (click)="uploadStagedAttachment(slot.slot)"
                  >
                    <mat-icon>cloud_upload</mat-icon>
                  </button>
                }

                <label
                  class="ire__attachment-action ire__attachment-action--replace"
                  [attr.aria-label]="'Replace ' + slot.label"
                  matTooltip="Replace"

                  matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip"

                  matTooltipPosition="above"
                >
                  <mat-icon>swap_horiz</mat-icon>
                  <input
                    type="file"
                    hidden
                    [accept]="acceptedAttachmentTypes"
                    [disabled]="isAttachmentBusy(slot.slot)"
                    (change)="onAttachmentPicked(slot.slot, $event)"
                  />
                </label>

                <!-- Delete only ever renders inside this block, i.e. only when a doc is attached. -->
                <button
                  type="button"
                  mat-icon-button
                  class="ire__attachment-action ire__attachment-action--danger"
                  [attr.aria-label]="'Remove ' + slot.label"
                  matTooltip="Delete"

                  matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip"

                  matTooltipPosition="above"
                  [disabled]="isAttachmentBusy(slot.slot)"
                  (click)="removeAttachment(slot.slot)"
                >
                  <mat-icon>delete</mat-icon>
                </button>
              </div>
            } @else {
              <div class="ire__attachment-drop" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
                <span class="ire__attachment-drop-label">{{ slot.hint }}</span>
                <span class="ire__attachment-drop-hint">PDF, JPG or PNG · max 25 MB</span>

                <!-- A <label> wrapping a hidden input, not a <button>: this opens the OS file
                     picker natively, so there's no click-forwarding JS to keep working. -->
                <!-- "Attach", not "Upload": picking a file only stages it, and the row that appears
                     then carries the separate upload action that actually sends it. -->
                <label
                  class="ire__attachment-upload-icon"
                  [class.ire__attachment-upload-icon--busy]="isAttachmentBusy(slot.slot)"
                  [attr.aria-label]="'Attach ' + slot.label"
                  matTooltip="Attach"

                  matTooltipClass="ir-attachment-tooltip"

                  matTooltipPosition="above"
                >
                  <mat-icon>attach_file</mat-icon>
                  <input
                    type="file"
                    hidden
                    [accept]="acceptedAttachmentTypes"
                    [disabled]="isAttachmentBusy(slot.slot)"
                    (change)="onAttachmentPicked(slot.slot, $event)"
                  />
                </label>
              </div>
            }

            <!-- Skinny progress bar, shared by the empty and occupied states so a Replace shows the
                 same feedback as a first upload. Determinate off real bytes sent; indeterminate
                 only when the browser couldn't report a total (see WwellIrService.uploadIrAttachment). -->
            @if (isAttachmentBusy(slot.slot)) {
              <mat-progress-bar
                class="ire__attachment-progress"
                [mode]="attachmentUploadPercent() === null ? 'indeterminate' : 'determinate'"
                [value]="attachmentUploadPercent() ?? 0"
              />
            }
          </section>
        }
        </div>
        }
      </section>
      </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Stays in the DOM for all of Timeline view (not gated on previewSidebarOpen) so hiding it
         is a pure CSS transition (see --hidden below) instead of an instant destroy/recreate. -->
    @if (viewMode() === 'timeline') {
      <aside class="ire__tl-preview-sidebar" [class.ire__tl-preview-sidebar--hidden]="!previewSidebarOpen()">
        <div class="ire__tl-preview-anim">
          <ng-container [ngTemplateOutlet]="wellborePreviewTpl" [ngTemplateOutletContext]="{ compact: true }" />
        </div>
      </aside>
    }
  </div>

  <footer class="ire__footer">
    @if (hasSavedIrSeq()) {
      <button
        type="button"
        mat-stroked-button
        color="warn"
        class="ire__delete-btn"
        [disabled]="anyFooterActionPending()"
        (click)="deleteIr()"
      >
        @if (isDeleting()) {
          <mat-spinner diameter="16" class="ire__save-spinner" />
        } @else {
          <mat-icon>delete_forever</mat-icon>
        }
        Delete IR
      </button>
    }
    <button
      type="button"
      mat-stroked-button
      color="primary"
      class="ire__save-btn"
      [disabled]="anyFooterActionPending()"
      (click)="saveAll()"
    >
      @if (isSaving()) {
        <mat-spinner diameter="16" class="ire__save-spinner" />
      } @else {
        <mat-icon>save</mat-icon>
      }
      Save
    </button>
    @if (canSubmitForReview()) {
      <button
        type="button"
        mat-flat-button
        color="primary"
        [disabled]="anyFooterActionPending()"
        (click)="submitForReview()"
      >
        @if (isSubmittingForReview()) {
          <mat-spinner diameter="16" class="ire__save-spinner" />
        } @else {
          <mat-icon>send</mat-icon>
        }
        Submit for Review
      </button>
    }
  </footer>
</div>
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-editor/wwell-ir-editor.component.scss

```scss
@use '@angular/material' as mat;
@use '../../shared/wwell-select-panel-mixin' as wsel;
@use '../../shared/wwell-design-tokens' as tokens;
@use '../../shared/wwell-material' as wmat;

$navy: tokens.$navy-deep;
$muted: tokens.$muted;
$border: tokens.$border;
$accent: tokens.$accent;
$warning: tokens.$warning;

// `:host` is a no-op under ViewEncapsulation.None (see the component's own comment) — the tag
// selector is what actually reaches this element. Bounds the component's own height end to end
// so only `.ire__body` scrolls internally; header/rail-label/footer stay fixed chrome, same
// pattern as app-wwell-ir-detail (wwell-ir-detail.component.scss).
app-wwell-ir-editor {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  height: 100%;
  min-height: 0;
  font-family: tokens.$font-family;
}

// Same rationale as wwell-ir-new-page/wwell-ir-detail's identical block — the app only calls
// mat.core() globally with no full M3 theme, so every Material control here (selects, inputs,
// toggle groups) needs its own scoped theme, including a select's portaled overlay panel (CDK
// renders it into <body>, hence the separate `.ire-select-panel` target).
.ire,
.ire-select-panel {
  color-scheme: var(--wp-scheme);

  @include mat.theme(
    (
      color: (
        theme-type: color-scheme,
        primary: mat.$blue-palette,
        tertiary: mat.$azure-palette,
      ),
      typography: (
        plain-family: tokens.$font-family,
        brand-family: tokens.$font-family,
      ),
      density: -4,
    )
  );
}

.ire {
  @include wmat.buttons;
  @include wmat.form-fields;
}

.ire-select-panel {
  border-radius: 10px !important;
  box-shadow:
    var(--wp-shadow-3) !important;
  @include wsel.panel($accent, $navy);
}

.ire,
.ire *,
.ire *::before,
.ire *::after {
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

.ire {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  flex: 1;
  min-height: 0;
  gap: var(--wwell-gutter, 0.6rem);
  padding: 0;
  @include wsel.trigger($accent);
}

/* ── Header: fixed chrome, never scrolls ─────────────────────────────────── */
.ire__header {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 1rem;
  background: tokens.$surface;
  border: 1px solid $border;
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 0.55rem 0.9rem;
  box-shadow:
    var(--wp-shadow-1);
}

.ire__eyebrow {
  margin: 0 0 1px;
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: $accent;
  font-family: tokens.$font-mono;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}

.ire__title {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 1.1rem;
  font-weight: 650;
  color: $navy;
  letter-spacing: -0.018em;
}

.ire__badge {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  padding: 4px 12px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
  font-weight: 600;
  white-space: nowrap;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.ire__badge-dot {
  width: 6px;
  height: 6px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.ire__status-refresh-btn {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  width: 28px;
  height: 28px;
  padding: 0;
  line-height: 28px;

  ::ng-deep .mat-icon {
    font-size: 18px;
    width: 18px;
    height: 18px;
    line-height: 18px;
  }
}

.ire__header-actions {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.6rem;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

/* ── Sections/Timeline view toggle ────────────────────────────────
 * A plain segmented control — no @angular/material/button-toggle import needed for two states. */
.ire__view-toggle {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 2px;
  padding: 2px;
  background: tokens.$surface-alt;
  border: 1px solid $border;
  border-radius: 999px;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.ire__view-toggle-btn {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  width: 28px;
  height: 28px;
  border: none;
  background: none;
  border-radius: 999px;
  padding: 0;
  color: $muted;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition:
    background-color 150ms ease,
    color 150ms ease;

  mat-icon {
    width: 16px;
    height: 16px;
    font-size: 16px;
  }

  &:hover {
    color: $navy;
  }

  &:focus-visible {
    outline: 2px solid $accent;
    outline-offset: 1px;
  }
}

.ire__view-toggle-btn--active {
  background: tokens.$surface;
  color: $accent;
  box-shadow: var(--wp-shadow-1);

  &:hover {
    color: $accent;
  }
}

.ire__tl-toggle-all-btn {
  width: 32px;
  height: 32px;
  padding: 0;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  color: $muted;

  &:hover {
    color: $accent;
  }

  mat-icon {
    width: 18px;
    height: 18px;
    font-size: 18px;
  }
}

.ire__tl-toggle-all-btn--active {
  color: $accent;
  background: tokens.$accent-soft;
  border-radius: 50%;
}

/* ── Body: IR SECTIONS rail + the always-rendered form ───────────────────── */
.ire__body {
  flex: 1;
  min-height: 0;
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  gap: var(--wwell-gutter, 0.7rem);
  overflow-y: auto;
  overscroll-behavior-y: contain;
}

/* ── Rail ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 * A plain nested nav list, not the old wizard's absolutely-positioned chip timeline — nothing
 * here needs to track a panel's live Y-position (see SectionRailController's doc comment: every
 * section is always rendered, so there's no accordion resize to chase). `position: sticky`
 * against `.ire__body` (its scrolling ancestor) is enough to keep it in view.
 */
.ire__rail {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  position: sticky;
  top: 0;
  align-self: flex-start;
  width: 196px;
  max-height: 100%;
  overflow-y: auto;
  padding: 0.65rem 0.5rem;
  background: tokens.$surface-alt;
  border: 1px solid $border;
  border-radius: 10px;
  box-shadow:
    var(--wp-shadow-1);
}

.ire__rail-title {
  margin: 0 0 0.4rem 0.35rem;
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
  font-weight: 650;
  letter-spacing: 0.08em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: $muted;
}

.ire__rail-list,
.ire__rail-children {
  list-style: none;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
}

.ire__rail-list {
  gap: 0.15rem;
}

.ire__rail-group-btn,
.ire__rail-child-btn {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  width: 100%;
  border: none;
  border-left: 3px solid transparent;
  background: none;
  border-radius: 7px;
  padding: 0.32rem 0.4rem 0.32rem calc(0.4rem - 3px);
  font: inherit;
  text-align: left;
  cursor: pointer;
  color: $navy;
  transition:
    background-color 150ms ease,
    border-color 150ms ease,
    color 150ms ease;

  &:hover {
    background: tokens.alpha($accent, 8%);
  }

  &:focus-visible {
    outline: 2px solid $accent;
    outline-offset: -2px;
  }
}

.ire__rail-group-dot {
  width: 6px;
  height: 6px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: $muted;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.ire__rail-number {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  font-weight: 650;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
  color: $muted;
}

.ire__rail-label {
  font-size: 0.84rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.ire__rail-group-btn--active,
.ire__rail-child-btn--active {
  background: tokens.alpha($accent, 12%);
  border-left-color: $accent;

  .ire__rail-number,
  .ire__rail-label {
    color: $accent;
  }

  .ire__rail-group-dot {
    background: $accent;
  }
}

.ire__rail-children {
  margin: 0.05rem 0 0.2rem 1.3rem;
  padding-left: 0.4rem;
  border-left: 1px solid $border;
}

.ire__rail-child-btn {
  padding: 0.24rem 0.4rem;

  .ire__rail-label {
    font-weight: 500;
  }
}

/* ── Sections column ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
.ire__sections {
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.65rem;
  padding-bottom: 0.5rem;
}

.ire__section,
.ire__group {
  background: tokens.$surface;
  border: 1px solid $border;
  border-radius: 12px;
  padding: 0.7rem 0.9rem;
  box-shadow:
    var(--wp-shadow-1);
  // Keeps a scrolled-to section's heading clear of the sticky app chrome above `.ire__body`.
  scroll-margin-top: 0.5rem;
}

.ire__group {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0;
}

.ire__group .ire__section--sub {
  border: none;
  border-radius: 0;
  // Inherited from `.ire__section` above; a sub-section is only a divided region of its group,
  // so its own shadow drew a faint box edge around each one.
  box-shadow: none;
  background: transparent;
  border-top: 1px solid $border;
  margin: 0.65rem 0 0;
  padding: 0.65rem 0 0;

  &:first-of-type {
    margin-top: 0.5rem;
    border-top: none;
    padding-top: 0;
  }
}

.ire__section-title {
  position: relative;
  margin: 0 0 0.5rem;
  padding-left: 0.65rem;
  font-size: 0.95rem;
  font-weight: 650;
  color: $navy;

  &::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    left: 0;
    top: 0.1em;
    bottom: 0.1em;
    width: 3px;
    border-radius: 999px;
    background: $accent;
  }
}

.ire__group > .ire__section-head {
  margin-bottom: 0;
}

/* ── Timeline view ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
 * `.ire__tl-row`/`.ire__tl-rail`/`.ire__tl-content` wrap every top-level section unconditionally
 * in the template (see wwell-ir-editor.component.html) so switching view modes never re-creates
 * a section's SectionEditState or its scroll-spy anchor. In "sections" mode (the default) they
 * collapse to `display: contents` — invisible to layout — so today's plain `.ire__sections` flex
 * list renders exactly as before. Only `.ire__sections--timeline` turns them into an actual
 * dot + connecting-line stepper, using the same light-surface tokens as the rest of the page. */
.ire__tl-row,
.ire__tl-content {
  display: contents;
}

.ire__tl-rail {
  display: none;
}

.ire__body--timeline {
  align-items: stretch;
}

.ire__sections--timeline {
  padding-left: 0.1rem;

  .ire__tl-row {
    display: flex;
    align-items: stretch;
    gap: 0.65rem;
  }

  .ire__tl-rail {
    position: relative;
    display: flex;
    justify-content: center;
    width: 22px;
    flex-shrink: 0;

    &::after {
      content: '';
      position: absolute;
      top: 22px;
      bottom: -0.65rem;
      left: 50%;
      width: 2px;
      background: $border;
      transform: translateX(-50%);
    }
  }

  .ire__tl-row:last-child .ire__tl-rail::after {
    display: none;
  }

  .ire__tl-content {
    display: block;
    flex: 1;
    min-width: 0;
  }

  .ire__section-head,
  .ire__group > .ire__section-head {
    cursor: pointer;
    user-select: none;
    border-radius: 4px;

    &:focus-visible {
      outline: 2px solid $accent;
      outline-offset: 2px;
    }
  }
}

/* ── Timeline preview panel ────────────────────────────────────────
 * The Wellbore Preview isn't tied to any one lifecycle step, so Timeline view pins it here
 * instead of leaving it in the scrolling step list — `position: sticky` against `.ire__body`
 * (its scrolling ancestor), same pattern the Sections rail already uses, so it stays in view no
 * matter which step is open or how far the steps are scrolled. It's a normal flex sibling of the
 * steps column, never `position: fixed` — it must never sit as a floating overlay on top of the
 * steps content, only ever occupy its own reserved width alongside it.
 *
 * Hiding it animates that reserved `width` down to 0 (rather than removing it from the DOM, or
 * just fading it in place) specifically so the steps column visibly reclaims the freed space as
 * it collapses, instead of leaving a blank gap where the panel used to be. The inner
 * `.ire__tl-preview-anim` wrapper holds its own width fixed and fades/slides out on a faster,
 * independent transition, so the content is already gone by the time the outer collapse would
 * otherwise start clipping it mid-shape.
 */
.ire__tl-preview-sidebar {
  // Grows with the page (the diagram, its figures and labels scale with it), between the old fixed 320px and 27rem.
  --ire-preview-w: clamp(20rem, 27vw, 27rem);

  flex-shrink: 0;
  // A column, so the card inside can be told to fill exactly what the page leaves it (a percentage height would
  // have nothing definite to resolve against here, which is what let the card run past the fold and get clipped).
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  position: sticky;
  top: 0;
  align-self: flex-start;
  width: var(--ire-preview-w);
  // As tall as the page leaves it, whatever the card inside is: the card is told to fill this, so a height taken
  // from the card's own content would be circular (and used to let the card run past the fold and get clipped).
  height: 100%;
  overflow: hidden;
  transition: width tokens.$duration-base tokens.$ease-out;

  &.ire__tl-preview-sidebar--hidden {
    width: 0;
  }
}

.ire__tl-preview-anim {
  flex: 1;
  min-height: 0;
  width: var(--ire-preview-w, 20rem);
  overflow-y: auto;
  overflow-x: hidden;
  transition:
    opacity tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out,
    transform tokens.$duration-fast tokens.$ease-out;
}

.ire__tl-preview-sidebar--hidden .ire__tl-preview-anim {
  opacity: 0;
  transform: translateX(16px);
}

@media (max-width: 1100px) {
  // Only the resting (open) width shrinks here — the hidden-state override above always collapses
  // to 0 regardless of breakpoint, so it isn't repeated in this media query.
  .ire__tl-preview-sidebar {
    --ire-preview-w: 260px;
  }
}

.ire__tl-dot {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  width: 22px;
  height: 22px;
  margin-top: 0.1rem;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: tokens.$surface;
  border: 2px solid $border;
  flex-shrink: 0;

  mat-icon {
    width: 13px;
    height: 13px;
    font-size: 13px;
    color: tokens.$surface;
  }
}

.ire__tl-dot--done {
  background: var(--wp-success-fill);
  border-color: var(--wp-success-fill);
}

.ire__tl-dot--active {
  border-color: $accent;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px tokens.alpha($accent, 16%);
}

// The cobalt ring marks the step still to do. Timeline view opens every step, so a completed one is
// also "active" — without this it took the active ring and halo over its green fill and read as
// half-done. Done wins: green fill, green ring, green halo.
.ire__tl-dot--done.ire__tl-dot--active {
  border-color: var(--wp-success-fill);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px tokens.alpha(var(--wp-success-fill), 22%);
}

.ire__tl-status {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: $muted;
  padding: 0.1rem 0.6rem;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: tokens.$surface-alt;
  border: 1px solid $border;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.ire__tl-status--done {
  color: var(--wp-success);
  background: var(--wp-success-soft);
  border-color: var(--wp-success-line);
}

.ire__subsection-title {
  margin: 0 0 0.4rem;
  font-size: 0.86rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: $navy;
}

/* ── Per-section inline edit ──────────────────────────────────────
 * Each section flips independently into an editable state (its own pencil button, its own
 * Save/Cancel row) — any number of sections can be mid-edit at once, since each one owns its
 * own form/edit-state instance (see SectionEditState in wwell-ir-section-edit.util.ts) rather
 * than sharing one page-level "editing" flag. */
.ire__section-head {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  margin: 0 0 0.5rem;

  .ire__section-title,
  .ire__subsection-title {
    margin: 0;
  }
}

.ire__edit-btn {
  width: 28px;
  height: 28px;
  padding: 0;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  color: $muted;

  &:hover {
    color: $accent;
  }

  mat-icon {
    width: 16px;
    height: 16px;
    font-size: 16px;
  }
}

.ire__edit-btn--danger:hover {
  color: var(--wp-danger-ink);
}

/* Groups a section-head's edit + reset buttons so `.ire__section-head`'s `justify-content:
 * space-between` sees exactly two children (title, actions) instead of spreading the buttons
 * apart from each other across the row. */
.ire__section-actions {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.1rem;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.ire__edit-actions {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: flex-end;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  margin-top: 0.65rem;
}

/* A "+" button's one-row add form: its own card above the table, matching the table wrapper's border and radius. */
.ire__add-block {
  margin-bottom: 0.65rem;
  padding: 0.85rem 0.9rem 0.7rem;
  border: 1px solid $border;
  border-radius: 8px;
}

/* ── View-mode read display ───────────────────────────────────────
 * `[data-tone]` (bound from KvRow.tone — e.g. a nonzero H2S reading) flags a field whose value
 * is actually a problem, not just data. Values are plain text — no pill or box around them; a
 * multi-select's picks are listed one per line by `app-wwell-ir-kv-value`. */
.ire__view-grid {
  margin: 0;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(160px, 1fr));
  gap: 0.65rem 1.25rem;
}

.ire__view-field {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.15rem;
  min-width: 0;

  dt {
    font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
    font-weight: 600;
    color: $muted;
  }

  dd {
    margin: 0;
    font-size: 0.86rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    color: $navy;
    // A remarks field's saved value can carry real line breaks (e.g. Development/Testing,
    // Sampling) — without this, `\n` renders as a plain space and a 3-line remark collapses
    // back into one run-on line.
    white-space: pre-line;
  }

  &[data-tone='warning'] dd {
    color: $warning;
  }

  &[data-tone='danger'] dd {
    color: var(--wp-danger-ink);
  }

  &[data-tone='success'] dd {
    color: var(--wp-success);
  }
}

.ire__view-table-wrap {
  overflow-x: auto;
  border: 1px solid $border;
  border-radius: 8px;
}

.ire__view-table {
  width: 100%;
  border-collapse: collapse;
  font-size: 0.82rem;
  white-space: nowrap;

  th {
    background: tokens.$surface-alt;
    color: $muted;
    font-weight: 600;
    font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.04em;
    text-align: left;
    padding: 0.4rem 0.6rem;
    border-bottom: 1px solid $border;
  }

  td {
    padding: 0.4rem 0.6rem;
    color: $navy;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--wp-line-soft);
  }

  tr:last-child td {
    border-bottom: none;
  }
}

.ire__view-table-empty {
  text-align: center;
  color: $muted;
  font-style: italic;
}

.ire__view-table-actions-col {
  width: 1%;
}

.ire__row-actions {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.15rem;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.ire__row-action-btn {
  width: 26px;
  height: 26px;
  padding: 0;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  color: $muted;

  &:hover {
    color: $accent;
  }

  mat-icon {
    width: 15px;
    height: 15px;
    font-size: 15px;
  }
}

.ire__row-action-btn--danger:hover {
  color: var(--wp-danger-ink);
}

.ire__remarks,
.ire__empty-text {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 0.86rem;
  color: $muted;
  font-style: italic;
}

/* ── Attachments (7.1 Location Map / 7.2 Initiation Request) ─────────────────── */
/* The slot's own icon, inline in its subsection heading — this is the one visual cue that tells
 * the two slots apart at a glance, since their empty states are otherwise identical. */
.ire__attachment-slot-icon {
  width: 16px;
  height: 16px;
  font-size: 16px;
  margin-right: 0.35rem;
  vertical-align: -3px;
  color: $accent;
}

.ire__attachment-required {
  margin-left: 0.2rem;
  color: var(--wp-danger-ink);
  font-weight: 600;
}

.ire__attachment-error {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.4rem;
  margin: 0 0 0.75rem;
  padding: 0.5rem 0.7rem;
  border: 1px solid var(--wp-danger-line);
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: var(--wp-danger-soft);
  color: var(--wp-danger-ink);
  font-size: 0.82rem;
  font-weight: 600;

  mat-icon {
    width: 16px;
    height: 16px;
    font-size: 16px;
  }
}

/* Empty slot: hint copy plus one explicit Upload button. The card itself is deliberately NOT the
 * click target — with a real button present, a whole-card hit area just makes stray clicks open a
 * file dialog the user didn't ask for. */
.ire__attachment-drop {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  gap: 0.25rem;
  padding: 1.1rem 1rem;
  border: 1.5px dashed $border;
  border-radius: 10px;
  background: tokens.$surface-alt;
  text-align: center;
  transition:
    border-color 150ms ease,
    background 150ms ease;

  &:focus-within {
    border-color: $accent;
  }

  > mat-icon {
    width: 22px;
    height: 22px;
    font-size: 22px;
    color: $accent;
  }
}

/* Icon-only upload control — a round accent button, sized to match the row actions on an occupied
 * slot so the two states read as the same control set rather than two different designs. */
.ire__attachment-upload-icon {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  width: 38px;
  height: 38px;
  margin-top: 0.6rem;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: tokens.$cta;
  color: tokens.$on-cta;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition:
    background 150ms ease,
    opacity 150ms ease;

  &:hover {
    background: tokens.$cta-hover;
  }

  /* Keyboard users reach the label through its hidden input, so the ring has to be drawn here. */
  &:focus-within {
    outline: 2px solid $accent;
    outline-offset: 2px;
  }

  &--busy {
    cursor: progress;
    opacity: 0.65;
  }

  mat-icon {
    width: 20px;
    height: 20px;
    font-size: 20px;
  }
}

/* Skinny by design — this sits under a compact card, so a full-height Material bar would read as a
 * separate element rather than as feedback belonging to the slot above it. */
.ire__attachment-progress {
  margin-top: 0.5rem;
  height: 3px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.ire__attachment-drop-label {
  font-size: 0.82rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: $navy;
}

.ire__attachment-drop-hint {
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
  color: $muted;
}

/* Occupied slot: file identity on the left, actions on the right. */
.ire__attachment-file {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.55rem;
  padding: 0.55rem 0.65rem;
  border: 1px solid $border;
  border-radius: 10px;
  background: tokens.$surface-alt;

  /* Held for an unsaved New IR — dashed, matching the empty slot's border, so "not committed yet"
   * is visible at a glance rather than only in the caption text. */
  &--pending {
    border-style: dashed;
    border-color: $accent;
  }
}

.ire__attachment-file-icon {
  flex: none;
  width: 20px;
  height: 20px;
  font-size: 20px;
  color: $accent;
}

.ire__attachment-file-meta {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  min-width: 0;
  /* Takes the slack so the action buttons stay pinned right regardless of name length. */
  flex: 1 1 auto;
}

.ire__attachment-file-name {
  font-size: 0.84rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: $navy;
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.ire__attachment-file-size {
  font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
  color: $muted;
}

.ire__attachment-action {
  flex: none;
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  width: 30px;
  height: 30px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  color: $muted;
  cursor: pointer;

  &:hover {
    color: $accent;
    background: var(--wp-surface-hover);
  }

  &--danger:hover {
    color: var(--wp-danger-ink);
  }

  /* The one action that actually commits the file, so it carries accent weight from the start
   * rather than only on hover like the neutral view/replace icons beside it. */
  &--upload {
    color: $accent;

    &:hover:not(:disabled) {
      background: var(--wp-surface-hover);
    }
  }

  mat-icon {
    width: 17px;
    height: 17px;
    font-size: 17px;
  }
}


/* ── Casing size warnings — same treatment as the old wizard's ──────────────── */
.ire__casing-warnings {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  margin: 0.75rem 0 0;
  padding: 0;
  list-style: none;
}

.ire__casing-warning {
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  gap: 0.55rem;
  padding: 0.6rem 0.85rem;
  background: var(--wp-warning-soft);
  border: 1px solid tokens.alpha($warning, 25%);
  border-radius: 10px;
  font-size: 0.84rem;
  line-height: 1.4;
  color: $warning;

  mat-icon {
    flex-shrink: 0;
    width: 18px;
    height: 18px;
    font-size: 18px;
    margin-top: 0.1rem;
  }
}

/* ── Footer: fixed chrome, never scrolls ─────────────────────────────────── */
.ire__footer {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  display: flex;
  justify-content: flex-end;
  gap: 0.6rem;
  padding: 0.5rem 0.8rem;
  background: tokens.$surface;
  border: 1px solid $border;
  border-radius: 10px;
  box-shadow: 0 -4px 12px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.05);
}

// Keeps the Save button's own width stable whether it's showing the save icon or the
// in-flight spinner, so the footer never reflows/jumps the instant a save starts.
.ire__save-btn .mdc-button__label {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.4rem;
}

.ire__save-spinner {
  display: inline-flex;
}

// Sits opposite Save/Submit for Review — `margin-right: auto` claims the footer's leading edge
// against its `justify-content: flex-end`, so a destructive action is never adjacent to Save and
// can't be misclicked in its place.
.ire__delete-btn {
  margin-right: auto;

  .mdc-button__label {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 0.4rem;
  }
}

.ire .mat-mdc-form-field-required-marker {
  color: var(--wp-danger);
}

// Material dims disabled fields to 38% alpha, which is near-invisible on the field's grey fill.
// Wellbore Details locks fields that carry the linked well's data (BI Number, Target Aquifer…)
// and users need to read them, so they get the view-mode look: navy value, muted label, semibold.
.ire__section--wellbore .mat-mdc-form-field {
  .mat-mdc-input-element,
  .mat-mdc-select-value {
    font-weight: 600;
  }

  &.mat-form-field-disabled {
    .mat-mdc-input-element,
    .mat-mdc-select-value,
    .mat-mdc-form-field-text-suffix {
      color: $navy;
      -webkit-text-fill-color: $navy;
    }

    .mat-mdc-floating-label {
      color: $muted;
    }
  }
}

/* ── Tighter field density, scoped to this component only ───────────────────
 * The shared dynamic-form building blocks (dyf__field, dys__grid, rpg__row) are reused by other
 * consumer forms (wwell-request-form, wwell-wlda-request-form) with their own spacing needs, so
 * their own stylesheets are left untouched — these ::ng-deep overrides only apply where `.ire`
 * wraps them, keeping the "skinny" treatment local to the IR editor. */
.ire ::ng-deep {
  .dys {
    margin-bottom: 0.65rem;
  }

  .dys__grid {
    gap: 0.5rem 0.85rem;
  }

  .rpg {
    gap: 0.45rem;
  }

  .rpg__row {
    padding: 0.5rem 0.65rem;
    border-radius: 9px;
  }

  .rpg__row-fields {
    gap: 0.4rem 0.7rem;
  }

  .rpg__row-index {
    width: 1.3rem;
    height: 1.3rem;
    margin-top: 0.2rem;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .ire__body {
    flex-direction: column;
    overflow-y: visible;
  }

  .ire__rail {
    position: static;
    width: 100%;
    max-height: none;
  }

  .ire__rail-children {
    margin-left: 1.2rem;
  }

  // `.ire__body` just switched to a column flex — everything stacks full-width, so a width
  // collapse (the desktop hide/show mechanism above) can't reclaim any space here. `order: -1`
  // keeps it above the steps list, same placement it always had; the hidden state below collapses
  // `max-height` instead, since that's the axis that actually frees up room in a stacked layout.
  .ire__tl-preview-sidebar {
    position: static;
    width: 100%;
    height: auto;
    max-height: none;
    overflow: hidden;
    order: -1;
    transition: max-height tokens.$duration-base tokens.$ease-out;

    &.ire__tl-preview-sidebar--hidden {
      width: 100%;
      max-height: 0;
    }
  }

  .ire__tl-preview-anim {
    width: 100%;
    max-height: none;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 640px) {
  .ire {
    padding: 0.6rem;
  }

  .ire__footer {
    flex-wrap: wrap;

    button {
      flex: 1;
    }
  }
}
```

```ts
import {

import { mapIrFormValueToSubmission } from '../wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-form.mapper';
import { buildWwellIrFormSchema } from '../wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-form.schema';
import {

  WwellIrRawCasingRow,
  WwellIrRawFormationTop,

} from '../wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-form.types';

import {

import {

  WWELL_IR_ATTACHMENT_MAX_BYTES,
  WWELL_IR_ATTACHMENT_SLOTS,
  irAttachmentFor,
} from '../wwell-ir-attachments.config';
import { WwellIrService } from '../wwell-ir.service';
import { WWELL_IR_STATUS_META, WWELL_IR_SUBMITTABLE_STATUSES, hasKnownIrStatus } from '../wwell-ir.config';
import {

  buildHydrogeologyRows,
  buildProgramDevRows,
```
