```ts
} from './wwell-ir-detail-rows.util';
import { WWELL_IR_PREVIEW_DIAGRAM_LAYOUT, WWELL_IR_SIDEBAR_DIAGRAM_LAYOUT } from './wwell-ir-preview-diagram-layout';
import {
  findLinkedWell,
  formatLinkedWellCoordinates,
  linkedWellHasCoordinates,
  linkedWellHasElevation,
  linkedWellHasSupportingBusiness,
  linkedWellHasTargetAquifer,
  linkedWellHasTargetDepth,
} from '../wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-linked-well.util';
import { InlineRowEditState, SectionEditState, buildAndPatchArray, buildAndPatchGroup } from './wwell-ir-section-edit.util';
import { SectionRailController } from './wwell-ir-section-rail.util';
import { WWELL_IR_SECTION_RAIL, WwellIrRailGroup, wwellIrRailLeaves } from './wwell-ir-section-rail.config';
import { WwellIrTimelineStepStatus, wwellIrTimelineStepStatus } from './wwell-ir-timeline.util';

export type WwellIrEditorViewMode = 'sections' | 'timeline';

const SELECT_PANEL_CLASS = 'ire-select-panel';

function findSection(schema: ReturnType<typeof buildWwellIrFormSchema>, stepKey: string, sectionKey: string): WwellFormSectionConfig {
  const section = schema.find(step => step.key === stepKey)?.sections.find(s => s.key === sectionKey);
  if (!section) throw new Error(`Missing IR form section ${stepKey}.${sectionKey}`);
  return { ...section, title: null };
}

/** A repeatable section's row fields as one flat form — what the section's "+" button opens. `requiredKeys`
 *  are the fields Save identifies a row by (see the payload mapper's row keys), so a row can't be added without them. */
function singleRowSection(section: WwellFormSectionConfig, requiredKeys: readonly string[]): WwellFormSectionConfig {
  const rowFields = section.repeatable?.rowFields ?? [];
  return {
    key: `${section.key}NewRow`,
    title: null,
    fields: rowFields.map(field =>
      requiredKeys.includes(field.key) ? { ...field, validators: { ...field.validators, required: true } } : field,
    ),
  };
}

@Component({
  selector: 'app-wwell-ir-editor',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [
    NgTemplateOutlet,
    MatButtonModule,
    MatIconModule,
    MatProgressBarModule,
    MatProgressSpinnerModule,
    MatTooltipModule,
    DynamicFieldComponent,
    DynamicSectionComponent,
    WwellIrWellborePanelComponent,
    WwellIrKvValueComponent,
    WwellCollapseAnimateDirective,
  ],
  templateUrl: './wwell-ir-editor.component.html',
  styleUrl: './wwell-ir-editor.component.scss',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  encapsulation: ViewEncapsulation.None,
})
export class WwellIrEditorComponent implements AfterViewInit {
  readonly ir = input.required<IntegratedRequirement>();

  private readonly fb = inject(FormBuilder);
  protected readonly store = inject(WwellIrStore);
  private readonly destroyRef = inject(DestroyRef);
  private readonly confirmDialog = inject(ActionConfirmDialogService);
  private readonly notify = inject(NotificationService);
  private readonly wwellIrService = inject(WwellIrService);
  private readonly dialog = inject(MatDialog);

  protected readonly statusMeta = WWELL_IR_STATUS_META;
  protected readonly hasKnownStatus = hasKnownIrStatus;
  protected readonly isSaving = this.store.creatingIr;
  protected readonly isDeleting = this.store.deletingIr;
  protected readonly isSubmittingForReview = this.store.updatingIrStatus;
  protected readonly anyFooterActionPending = computed(
    () => this.isSaving() || this.isDeleting() || this.isSubmittingForReview(),
  );
  protected readonly hasSavedIrSeq = computed(() => this.ir().irSeq !== null && this.ir().irSeq !== undefined);
  protected readonly selectPanelClass = SELECT_PANEL_CLASS;
  protected readonly canSubmitForReview = computed(
    () => WWELL_IR_SUBMITTABLE_STATUSES.includes(this.ir().status) && this.ir().sapWfId == null,
  );

  protected refreshApprovalStatus(): void {
    const ir = this.ir();
    if (ir.irSeq == null || ir.sapWfId == null) return;
    this.store.refreshIrApprovalStatus(ir.irSeq, ir.sapWfId);
  }

  protected readonly railGroups = WWELL_IR_SECTION_RAIL;
  private readonly railLeaves = wwellIrRailLeaves(WWELL_IR_SECTION_RAIL);
  protected readonly rail = new SectionRailController(this.destroyRef);

  readonly viewMode = model<WwellIrEditorViewMode>('timeline');
  private readonly expandedTimelineSteps = signal<ReadonlySet<string>>(new Set(this.railGroups.map(group => group.key)));

  protected setViewMode(mode: WwellIrEditorViewMode): void {
    this.viewMode.set(mode);
    if (mode === 'timeline' && this.expandedTimelineSteps().size === 0) {
      this.expandedTimelineSteps.set(new Set(this.railGroups.map(group => group.key)));
    }
  }

  protected timelineStepStatus(groupKey: string): WwellIrTimelineStepStatus {
    return wwellIrTimelineStepStatus(this.ir(), groupKey);
  }

  protected isTimelineStepExpanded(groupKey: string): boolean {
    return this.viewMode() !== 'timeline' || this.expandedTimelineSteps().has(groupKey);
  }

  protected readonly allTimelineStepsExpanded = computed(() => this.expandedTimelineSteps().size >= this.railGroups.length);

  protected toggleAllTimelineSteps(): void {
    this.expandedTimelineSteps.set(
      this.allTimelineStepsExpanded() ? new Set() : new Set(this.railGroups.map(group => group.key)),
    );
  }

  protected readonly previewSidebarOpen = signal(true);

  protected togglePreviewSidebar(): void {
    this.previewSidebarOpen.update(open => !open);
  }

  protected toggleTimelineStep(groupKey: string): void {
    if (this.viewMode() !== 'timeline') return;
    this.expandedTimelineSteps.update(current => {
      const next = new Set(current);
      if (next.has(groupKey)) {
        next.delete(groupKey);
      } else {
        next.add(groupKey);
      }
      return next;
    });
  }

  private readonly schema = buildWwellIrFormSchema(this.store.linkedRequestWellOptions, this.store.geologistOptions);
  protected readonly referenceMainSection = findSection(this.schema, 'reference', 'main');
  protected readonly programSection = findSection(this.schema, 'programDetails', 'main');
  protected readonly geologistSection = findSection(this.schema, 'contacts', 'geologist');
  protected readonly formationTopsSection = findSection(this.schema, 'subsurface', 'formationTops');
  protected readonly topsRemarksSection = findSection(this.schema, 'subsurface', 'remarks');
  protected readonly casingSection = findSection(this.schema, 'subsurface', 'casing');
  protected readonly hydrogeologySection = findSection(this.schema, 'hydrogeology', 'main');
  protected readonly offsetWellsSection = findSection(this.schema, 'contacts', 'offsetWells');
  protected readonly offsetRemarksSection = findSection(this.schema, 'contacts', 'remarks');
  protected readonly formationTopAddSection = singleRowSection(this.formationTopsSection, ['name', 'depthFt']);
  protected readonly casingAddSection = singleRowSection(this.casingSection, ['type', 'sizeIn', 'depthFtBgl']);
  protected readonly offsetWellAddSection = singleRowSection(this.offsetWellsSection, ['epANum', 'aquifer']);

  protected readonly requestReferenceRows = computed(() =>
    buildRequestReferenceRows(this.ir(), this.store.linkedRequestWellOptions()),
  );
  protected readonly headerTitle = computed(() => {
    const live = this.requestReferenceEdit.liveValue();
    return (live ? live.wellName : this.ir().requestReference.wellName) || 'Untitled Well';
  });
  protected readonly programRows = computed(() => [...buildProgramMainRows(this.ir()), ...buildProgramDevRows(this.ir())]);
  protected readonly hydrogeologyRows = computed(() => buildHydrogeologyRows(this.ir()));
  protected readonly geologistContactRows = computed(() => buildGeologistContactRows(this.ir()));

  protected readonly wellboreDiagramData: Signal<WellboreDiagramData> = computed(() =>
    buildWellboreDiagramDataFromIntegratedRequirement(
      this.ir(),
      this.casingEdit.liveValue(),
      this.formationTopsEdit.liveValue(),
      this.programMainLiveValue(),
      this.hydrogeologyEdit.liveValue(),
    ),
  );
  private readonly programMainLiveValue = computed<WwellIrRawProgramMain | null>(() => {
    const live = this.programEdit.liveValue();
    if (!live) return null;
    return {
      completion: live.completion,
      openHoleSizeIn: live.openHoleSizeIn,
      acidization: live.acidization,
      transmission: live.transmission,
    };
  });
  protected readonly hasWellboreData: Signal<boolean> = computed(() =>
    hasWellboreDiagramDataForIntegratedRequirement(
      this.ir(),
      this.casingEdit.liveValue(),
      this.formationTopsEdit.liveValue(),
      this.programMainLiveValue(),
    ),
  );
  protected readonly hasTargetDepth: Signal<boolean> = computed(() => (this.ir().requestReference.targetDepthFt ?? 0) > 0);
  protected readonly previewDiagramLayout = WWELL_IR_PREVIEW_DIAGRAM_LAYOUT;
  protected readonly sidebarDiagramLayout = WWELL_IR_SIDEBAR_DIAGRAM_LAYOUT;
  protected readonly casingSizeWarnings: Signal<readonly CasingSizeWarning[]> = computed(() =>
    findInvertedCasingSizeRows(this.ir().casing.map(casingRowPatch)),
  );

  protected readonly requestReferenceEdit = new SectionEditState<FormGroup, WwellIrRawReference>(() => {
    const group = buildAndPatchGroup(
      this.referenceMainSection,
      this.fb,
      mapIntegratedRequirementToFormPatch(this.ir()).reference.main,
    );
    const linkedRequestId = group.get('linkedRequestId')?.value as string | null;
    console.log('[IR:link] requestReferenceEdit — section opened', { irId: this.ir().id, linkedRequestId });
    if (linkedRequestId) this.applyLinkedRequestWell(group, linkedRequestId);
    return group;
  });
  protected readonly programEdit = new SectionEditState<FormGroup, WwellIrRawProgramDetails>(() => {
    const patch = mapIntegratedRequirementToFormPatch(this.ir()).programDetails;
    return buildAndPatchGroup(this.programSection, this.fb, { ...patch.main, ...patch.developmentTesting });
  });
  protected readonly geologistContactEdit = new SectionEditState(() =>
    buildAndPatchGroup(this.geologistSection, this.fb, mapIntegratedRequirementToFormPatch(this.ir()).contacts.geologist),
  );
  protected readonly hydrogeologyEdit = new SectionEditState<FormGroup, WwellIrRawHydrology>(() =>
    buildAndPatchGroup(this.hydrogeologySection, this.fb, mapIntegratedRequirementToFormPatch(this.ir()).hydrogeology.main),
  );
  protected readonly formationTopsEdit = new SectionEditState<FormArray, WwellIrRawFormationTop[]>(() =>
    buildAndPatchArray(this.formationTopsSection, this.fb, this.ir().formationTops, formationTopRowPatch),
  );
  protected readonly topsRemarksEdit = new SectionEditState<FormGroup, WwellIrRawTopsRemarks>(() =>
    buildAndPatchGroup(this.topsRemarksSection, this.fb, { topsRemarks: this.ir().topsRemarks ?? '' }),
  );
  protected readonly casingEdit = new SectionEditState<FormArray, WwellIrRawCasingRow[]>(() =>
    buildAndPatchArray(this.casingSection, this.fb, this.ir().casing, casingRowPatch),
  );
  protected readonly offsetWellsEdit = new SectionEditState<
    FormGroup,
    { offsetWells: WwellIrRawOffsetWell[]; remarks: WwellIrRawRemarks }
  >(() => {
    const current = this.ir();
    return this.fb.group({
      offsetWells: buildAndPatchArray(this.offsetWellsSection, this.fb, current.offsetWells, offsetWellRowPatch),
      remarks: buildAndPatchGroup(this.offsetRemarksSection, this.fb, { offsetWellsRemarks: current.offsetWellsRemarks ?? '' }),
    });
  });

  /** The "+" buttons' one-row forms. Their control is a standalone group — a row only reaches the section's live
   *  FormArray (the one Save diffs) once "Add" commits it — so opening one never flips the section into its edit form. */
  protected readonly formationTopAdd = new SectionEditState<FormGroup>(
    () => buildSectionGroup(this.formationTopAddSection, this.fb) as FormGroup,
  );
  protected readonly casingAdd = new SectionEditState<FormGroup>(
    () => buildSectionGroup(this.casingAddSection, this.fb) as FormGroup,
  );
  protected readonly offsetWellAdd = new SectionEditState<FormGroup>(
    () => buildSectionGroup(this.offsetWellAddSection, this.fb) as FormGroup,
  );

  protected readonly formationTopsRows = computed(() => this.formationTopsEdit.liveValue() ?? this.ir().formationTops);
  protected readonly casingRows = computed(() => this.casingEdit.liveValue() ?? this.ir().casing);
  protected readonly offsetWellsRows = computed(() => this.offsetWellsEdit.liveValue()?.offsetWells ?? this.ir().offsetWells);

  protected readonly formationTopsInlineEdit = new InlineRowEditState();
  protected readonly casingInlineEdit = new InlineRowEditState();
  protected readonly offsetWellsInlineEdit = new InlineRowEditState();

  protected rowFieldControl(row: FormGroup, key: string): FormControl {
    return row.get(key) as FormControl;
  }

  private fieldConfigFor(section: WwellFormSectionConfig, key: string): WwellFieldConfig {
    const field = section.repeatable?.rowFields.find(candidate => candidate.key === key);
    if (!field) throw new Error(`Unknown repeatable field "${key}" on section "${section.key}"`);
    return field;
  }

  protected formationTopsFieldConfig(key: string): WwellFieldConfig {
    return this.fieldConfigFor(this.formationTopsSection, key);
  }

  protected casingFieldConfig(key: string): WwellFieldConfig {
    return this.fieldConfigFor(this.casingSection, key);
  }

  protected offsetWellsFieldConfig(key: string): WwellFieldConfig {
    return this.fieldConfigFor(this.offsetWellsSection, key);
  }

  protected formationTopsRowGroup(index: number): FormGroup | null {
    return (this.formationTopsEdit.control()?.at(index) as FormGroup) ?? null;
  }

  protected casingRowGroup(index: number): FormGroup | null {
    return (this.casingEdit.control()?.at(index) as FormGroup) ?? null;
  }

  protected offsetWellsRowGroup(index: number): FormGroup | null {
    const ctrl = this.offsetWellsEdit.control();
    return ctrl ? (this.offsetWellsArrayControl(ctrl).at(index) as FormGroup) : null;
  }

  protected editFormationTopRowInline(index: number): void {
    this.formationTopsEdit.open({ silent: true });
    const row = this.formationTopsRowGroup(index);
    if (row) this.formationTopsInlineEdit.open(index, row);
  }

  protected saveFormationTopRowInline(): void {
    this.formationTopsInlineEdit.close();
    this.notify.info('Formation top updated — click Save to apply.');
  }

  protected cancelFormationTopRowInline(): void {
    const index = this.formationTopsInlineEdit.index();
    this.formationTopsInlineEdit.cancel(index !== null ? this.formationTopsRowGroup(index) : null);
  }

  protected editCasingRowInline(index: number): void {
    this.casingEdit.open({ silent: true });
    const row = this.casingRowGroup(index);
    if (row) this.casingInlineEdit.open(index, row);
  }

  protected saveCasingRowInline(): void {
    this.casingInlineEdit.close();
    this.notify.info('Casing row updated — click Save to apply.');
  }

  protected cancelCasingRowInline(): void {
    const index = this.casingInlineEdit.index();
    this.casingInlineEdit.cancel(index !== null ? this.casingRowGroup(index) : null);
  }

  protected editOffsetWellRowInline(index: number): void {
    this.offsetWellsEdit.open({ silent: true });
    const row = this.offsetWellsRowGroup(index);
    if (row) this.offsetWellsInlineEdit.open(index, row);
  }

  protected saveOffsetWellRowInline(): void {
    this.offsetWellsInlineEdit.close();
    this.notify.info('Offset well updated — click Save to apply.');
  }

  protected cancelOffsetWellRowInline(): void {
    const index = this.offsetWellsInlineEdit.index();
    this.offsetWellsInlineEdit.cancel(index !== null ? this.offsetWellsRowGroup(index) : null);
  }

  protected addFormationTopRow(): void {
    this.commitNewRow<WwellIrRawFormationTop>(this.formationTopAdd, this.formationTopsSection, {
      existingKeys: this.formationTopsRows().map(row => topKey(row)),
      keyOf: topKey,
      liveArray: () => {
        this.formationTopsEdit.open({ silent: true });
        return this.formationTopsEdit.control();
      },
      duplicate: key => `Formation ${key} already has a horizon — edit that row instead.`,
      added: 'Formation top added — click Save to apply.',
    });
  }

  protected addCasingRow(): void {
    this.commitNewRow<WwellIrRawCasingRow>(this.casingAdd, this.casingSection, {
      existingKeys: this.casingRows().map(row => casingKey(row)),
      keyOf: casingKey,
      liveArray: () => {
        this.casingEdit.open({ silent: true });
        return this.casingEdit.control();
      },
      duplicate: key => `Casing ${key} already exists — edit that row instead.`,
      added: 'Casing row added — click Save to apply.',
    });
  }

  protected addOffsetWellRow(): void {
    this.commitNewRow<WwellIrRawOffsetWell>(this.offsetWellAdd, this.offsetWellsSection, {
      existingKeys: this.offsetWellsRows().map(row => waterKey(row.epANum ?? 0)),
      keyOf: row => waterKey(row.epANum ?? 0),
      liveArray: () => {
        this.offsetWellsEdit.open({ silent: true });
        const ctrl = this.offsetWellsEdit.control();
        return ctrl ? this.offsetWellsArrayControl(ctrl) : null;
      },
      duplicate: key => `Area well ${key} already exists — edit that row instead.`,
      added: 'Offset well added — click Save to apply.',
    });
  }

  /** Commits a "+" form's row into the section's live FormArray — the same array inline edits and deletes write to,
   *  so Save diffs it like any other change. Save matches rows on their natural key, so a row whose key is already
   *  taken is refused: it would silently overwrite that row instead of being added. */
  private commitNewRow<TRow>(
    add: SectionEditState<FormGroup>,
    section: WwellFormSectionConfig,
    commit: {
      readonly existingKeys: readonly string[];
      readonly keyOf: (row: TRow) => string;
      readonly liveArray: () => FormArray | null;
      readonly duplicate: (key: string) => string;
      readonly added: string;
    },
  ): void {
    const form = add.control();
    if (!form || form.invalid) return;

    const row = form.getRawValue() as TRow;
    const key = commit.keyOf(row);
    if (commit.existingKeys.includes(key)) {
      this.notify.warn(commit.duplicate(key));
      return;
    }

    const array = commit.liveArray();
    if (!array) return;
    addRepeatableRow(array, section.repeatable?.rowFields ?? [], this.fb);
    array.at(array.length - 1).patchValue(row as object);
    add.close();
    this.notify.info(commit.added);
  }

  protected readonly attachmentSlots = WWELL_IR_ATTACHMENT_SLOTS;
  protected readonly acceptedAttachmentTypes = WWELL_IR_ATTACHMENT_ACCEPT;

  protected attachmentFor(slot: IrAttachmentSlot): IrAttachment | null {
    return irAttachmentFor(this.ir().attachments, slot);
  }

  protected isAttachmentBusy(slot: IrAttachmentSlot): boolean {
    return this.store.busyAttachmentSlot() === slot;
  }

  protected attachmentUploadPercent(): number | null {
    return this.store.attachmentUploadPercent();
  }

  protected canAttach(): boolean {
    return this.ir().irSeq != null;
  }

  protected pendingAttachmentFor(slot: IrAttachmentSlot): File | null {
    return this.store.pendingAttachments()[slot] ?? null;
  }

  protected hasAttachment(slot: IrAttachmentSlot): boolean {
    return this.attachmentFor(slot) !== null || this.pendingAttachmentFor(slot) !== null;
  }

  protected viewAttachment(slot: IrAttachmentSlot): void {
    const config = WWELL_IR_ATTACHMENT_SLOTS.find(entry => entry.slot === slot);
    const label = config?.label ?? 'Attachment';

    const staged = this.pendingAttachmentFor(slot);
    if (staged) {
      this.openViewer(staged, label);
      return;
    }

    const attachment = this.attachmentFor(slot);
    const irSeq = this.ir().irSeq;
    if (!attachment || irSeq == null) return;

    this.wwellIrService
      .getIrAttachmentFile(irSeq, slot, attachment.fileName)
      .pipe(takeUntilDestroyed(this.destroyRef))
      .subscribe({
        next: file => this.openViewer(file, label),
        error: () => this.notify.error(`Failed to open ${attachment.fileName}.`),
      });
  }

  private openViewer(file: File, label: string): void {
    this.dialog.open(WwellIrAttachmentViewerComponent, {
      data: { file, label },
      panelClass: 'ir-viewer-panel',
      width: '92vw',
      maxWidth: '1500px',
      height: '92vh',
      autoFocus: false,
    });
  }

  protected uploadStagedAttachment(slot: IrAttachmentSlot): void {
    const irSeq = this.ir().irSeq;
    if (irSeq == null) {
      this.notify.info('Save the integrated requirement first — this uploads automatically on save.');
      return;
    }
    this.store.uploadStagedAttachment(irSeq, slot);
  }

  protected attachmentSizeLabel(bytes: number): string {
    if (bytes < 1024) return `${bytes} B`;
    if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return `${(bytes / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`;
    return `${(bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1)} MB`;
  }

  protected attachmentIcon(contentType: string): string {
    return contentType === 'application/pdf' ? 'picture_as_pdf' : 'image';
  }

  protected onAttachmentPicked(slot: IrAttachmentSlot, event: Event): void {
    const input = event.target as HTMLInputElement | null;
    const file = input?.files?.[0];
    if (input) input.value = '';
    if (!file) return;

    if (file.size > WWELL_IR_ATTACHMENT_MAX_BYTES) {
      this.notify.error('Attachment exceeds the 25 MB limit.');
      return;
    }

    this.store.setPendingAttachment(slot, file);
  }

  protected removeAttachment(slot: IrAttachmentSlot): void {
    if (this.pendingAttachmentFor(slot)) {
      this.store.removePendingAttachment(slot);
      return;
    }
    const irSeq = this.ir().irSeq;
    if (irSeq == null) return;
    this.store.deleteIrAttachment(irSeq, slot);
  }

  @ViewChildren('sectionEl', { read: ElementRef }) private readonly sectionEls?: QueryList<ElementRef<HTMLElement>>;
  @ViewChild('body', { read: ElementRef }) private readonly bodyEl?: ElementRef<HTMLElement>;

  private previousIrId: string | null = null;

  private readonly hasUnsavedReferenceEdits = computed(() => {
    const ctrl = this.requestReferenceEdit.control();
    return ctrl !== null && this.requestReferenceEdit.liveValue() !== null && ctrl.dirty;
  });

  public readonly hasUnsavedChanges = computed(
    () =>
      this.hasUnsavedReferenceEdits() ||
      this.programEdit.isEditing() ||
      this.geologistContactEdit.isEditing() ||
      this.hydrogeologyEdit.isEditing() ||
      this.formationTopsEdit.isEditing() ||
      this.topsRemarksEdit.isEditing() ||
      this.casingEdit.isEditing() ||
      this.offsetWellsEdit.isEditing(),
  );

  private saveSettledResolvers: ((succeeded: boolean) => void)[] = [];
  private wasSavingForNavigation = false;

  constructor() {
    effect(onCleanup => {
      const ctrl = this.requestReferenceEdit.control();
      if (!ctrl) return;
      const linkedRequestIdControl = ctrl.get('linkedRequestId');
      const subscription = linkedRequestIdControl?.valueChanges.subscribe((linkedRequestId: string) => {
        console.log('[IR:link] "Link to Request" select — valueChanges fired', { linkedRequestId });
        if (linkedRequestId) this.applyLinkedRequestWell(ctrl, linkedRequestId);
      });
      onCleanup(() => subscription?.unsubscribe());
    });

    effect(() => {
      const id = this.ir().id;
      if (this.previousIrId !== null && id !== this.previousIrId) this.closeAllSections();
      this.previousIrId = id;
    });

    effect(() => {
      if (this.ir().irSeq != null) return;
      untracked(() => this.requestReferenceEdit.open());
    });

    effect(() => {
      const saving = this.store.creatingIr();
      const settled = this.wasSavingForNavigation && !saving;
      this.wasSavingForNavigation = saving;
      if (!settled || this.saveSettledResolvers.length === 0) return;

      const succeeded = !this.store.createIrError();
      const resolvers = this.saveSettledResolvers;
      this.saveSettledResolvers = [];
      resolvers.forEach(resolve => resolve(succeeded));
    });
  }

  public discardUnsavedChanges(): void {
    this.closeAllSections();
  }

  public saveUnsavedChangesAndWait(): Promise<boolean> {
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      this.saveSettledResolvers.push(resolve);
      if (!this.store.creatingIr()) this.saveAll();
    });
  }

  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    if (this.bodyEl && this.sectionEls) this.rail.attach(this.bodyEl, this.sectionEls);
  }

  protected leafIndex(leafKey: string): number {
    return this.railLeaves.findIndex(leaf => leaf.key === leafKey);
  }

  protected scrollToLeaf(leafKey: string): void {
    this.rail.scrollToIndex(this.leafIndex(leafKey));
  }

  protected scrollToGroupStart(group: WwellIrRailGroup): void {
    const key = group.leaf?.key ?? group.children?.[0]?.key;
    if (key) this.scrollToLeaf(key);
  }

  protected isLeafActive(leafKey: string): boolean {
    return this.rail.activeIndex() === this.leafIndex(leafKey);
  }

  protected subSeaFt(depthFt: number | null): number | null {
    const elevation = this.ir().requestReference.surfaceElevationFtAsl;
    return elevation === null || depthFt === null ? null : elevation - depthFt;
  }

  private applyLinkedRequestWell(referenceGroup: FormGroup, linkedRequestId: string): void {
    console.log('[IR:link] applyLinkedRequestWell — start', { linkedRequestId, eligibleWellsLoaded: this.store.linkedRequestWells().length });
    const well = findLinkedWell(linkedRequestId, this.store.linkedRequestWells());
    if (!well) {
      console.log('[IR:link] applyLinkedRequestWell — no matching eligible well, aborting patch', { linkedRequestId });
      return;
    }

    const hasElevation = linkedWellHasElevation(well);
    const hasCoordinates = linkedWellHasCoordinates(well);
    const hasTargetDepth = linkedWellHasTargetDepth(well);
    const hasTargetAquifer = linkedWellHasTargetAquifer(well);
    const hasSupportingBusiness = linkedWellHasSupportingBusiness(well);

    const patch: Record<string, string | number> = { wellName: well.wellName };
    if (hasElevation) patch['surfaceElevationFtAsl'] = well.surfaceElevation as number;
    patch['coordinates'] = hasCoordinates ? formatLinkedWellCoordinates(well) : '';
    if (hasTargetDepth) patch['targetDepthFt'] = well.targetDepth as number;
    patch['targetAquifer'] = hasTargetAquifer ? (well.targetAquifer as string) : '';
    patch['supportingBusiness'] = hasSupportingBusiness ? (well.supportings as string) : '';
    console.log('[IR:link] applyLinkedRequestWell — patching form group', {
      patch,
      hasElevation,
      hasCoordinates,
      hasTargetDepth,
      hasTargetAquifer,
      hasSupportingBusiness,
    });
    referenceGroup.patchValue(patch);

    referenceGroup.get('wellName')?.disable();
    this.setLinkedFieldLock(referenceGroup, 'surfaceElevationFtAsl', hasElevation);
    console.log('[IR:link] applyLinkedRequestWell — done', { rawValue: referenceGroup.getRawValue() });
  }

  private setLinkedFieldLock(group: FormGroup, key: string, locked: boolean): void {
    const control = group.get(key);
    if (!control) return;
    console.log('[IR:link] setLinkedFieldLock', { key, locked });
    if (locked) control.disable();
    else control.enable();
  }

  protected offsetWellsArrayControl(ctrl: FormGroup): FormArray {
    return ctrl.get('offsetWells') as FormArray;
  }

  protected offsetWellsRemarksControl(ctrl: FormGroup): FormGroup {
    return ctrl.get('remarks') as FormGroup;
  }

  protected deleteCasingRow(index: number): void {
    const row = this.ir().casing[index];
    this.confirmThenDelete('Delete casing row?', [
      { label: 'Type', value: row.type },
      { label: 'Size', value: row.sizeIn },
      { label: 'Depth', value: `${row.depthFtBgl} ft bgl` },
    ], () => {
      this.casingEdit.open({ silent: true });
      this.casingEdit.control()?.removeAt(index);
      this.notify.info('Casing row removed — click Save to apply.');
    });
  }

  protected deleteFormationTopRow(index: number): void {
    const row = this.ir().formationTops[index];
    this.confirmThenDelete('Delete formation top?', [
      { label: 'Formation', value: row.name },
      { label: 'Depth', value: `${row.depthFt} ft bgl` },
    ], () => {
      this.formationTopsEdit.open({ silent: true });
      this.formationTopsEdit.control()?.removeAt(index);
      this.notify.info('Formation top removed — click Save to apply.');
    });
  }

  protected deleteOffsetWellRow(index: number): void {
    const row = this.ir().offsetWells[index];
    this.confirmThenDelete('Delete offset well?', [
      { label: 'EP A Num', value: String(row.epANum) },
      { label: 'Aquifer', value: row.aquifer || '—' },
    ], () => {
      this.offsetWellsEdit.open({ silent: true });
      const ctrl = this.offsetWellsEdit.control();
      if (ctrl) this.offsetWellsArrayControl(ctrl).removeAt(index);
      this.notify.info('Offset well removed — click Save to apply.');
    });
  }

  protected resetProgramSection(): void {
    this.confirmThenAct({ title: 'Reset Program Details?', confirmLabel: 'Reset' }, () =>
      this.resetFlatSection(this.programEdit, this.programSection, 'Program Details'),
    );
  }

  protected resetGeologistSection(): void {
    this.confirmThenAct({ title: 'Reset Geologist Contact?', confirmLabel: 'Reset' }, () =>
      this.resetFlatSection(this.geologistContactEdit, this.geologistSection, 'Geologist Contact'),
    );
  }

  private resetFlatSection(edit: SectionEditState<FormGroup, unknown>, section: WwellFormSectionConfig, label: string): void {
    edit.open();
    const ctrl = edit.control();
    if (!ctrl) return;
    const defaults: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    (section.fields ?? []).forEach(field => {
      defaults[field.key] = defaultValueFor(field);
    });
    ctrl.patchValue(defaults);
    this.notify.info(`${label} cleared — click Save to apply.`);
  }

  private confirmThenAct(
    config: { title: string; rows?: { label: string; value: string }[]; confirmLabel: string },
    onConfirmed: () => void,
  ): void {
    this.confirmDialog
      .open({ title: config.title, rows: config.rows ?? [], confirmLabel: config.confirmLabel, severity: 'danger' })
      .pipe(takeUntilDestroyed(this.destroyRef))
      .subscribe(confirmed => {
        if (confirmed) onConfirmed();
      });
  }

  private confirmThenDelete(title: string, rows: { label: string; value: string }[], onConfirmed: () => void): void {
    this.confirmThenAct({ title, rows, confirmLabel: 'Delete' }, onConfirmed);
  }

  protected deleteIr(): void {
    const irSeq = this.ir().irSeq;
    if (irSeq === null || irSeq === undefined) return;
    this.confirmThenAct(
      {
        title: 'Delete this Integrated Requirement?',
        rows: [
          { label: 'IR', value: this.ir().code },
          { label: 'Well', value: this.ir().requestReference.wellName || 'Untitled Well' },
        ],
        confirmLabel: 'Delete',
      },
      () => this.store.deleteIr(irSeq),
    );
  }

  private currentRawFormValue(): WwellIrFormRawValue {
    return mapIntegratedRequirementToRawFormValue(this.ir());
  }

  private saveMergedRaw(raw: WwellIrFormRawValue): void {
    const current = this.ir();
    const status: Extract<IntegratedRequirementStatus, 'draft' | 'in-progress'> =
      current.status === 'draft' || current.status === 'in-progress' ? current.status : 'in-progress';
    const { payload } = mapIrFormValueToSubmission(raw, status);
    const irBulkPayload = buildWwellIrBulkSavePayload(raw, {
      irSeq: current.irSeq ?? null,
      existing: current,
      geologistEmpNumByName: this.store.geologistEmpNumByName(),
    });
    this.store.createIr(payload, irBulkPayload);
  }

  protected saveAll(): void {
    // A record whose stored remarks still carry legacy HTML is saved even with nothing edited — the form already
    // holds the cleaned text, and saving is what writes it back.
    if (this.ir().irSeq !== null && !this.hasUnsavedChanges() && !this.ir().hasLegacyMarkup) {
      this.notify.info('No changes to save.');
      return;
    }

    let raw = this.currentRawFormValue();

    const referenceCtrl = this.requestReferenceEdit.control();
    if (referenceCtrl) raw = { ...raw, reference: { main: referenceCtrl.getRawValue() } };

    const programCtrl = this.programEdit.control();
    if (programCtrl) {
      const flat = programCtrl.getRawValue() as WwellIrRawProgramDetails;
      raw = {
        ...raw,
        programDetails: {
          main: {
            completion: flat.completion,
            openHoleSizeIn: flat.openHoleSizeIn,
            acidization: flat.acidization,
            transmission: flat.transmission,
          },
          developmentTesting: {
            logging: flat.logging,
            mudLogging: flat.mudLogging,
            sampling: flat.sampling,
            flowTest: flat.flowTest,
            remarks: flat.remarks,
          },
        },
      };
    }

    const geologistCtrl = this.geologistContactEdit.control();
    if (geologistCtrl) raw = { ...raw, contacts: { ...raw.contacts, geologist: geologistCtrl.getRawValue() } };

    const hydrogeologyCtrl = this.hydrogeologyEdit.control();
    if (hydrogeologyCtrl) raw = { ...raw, hydrogeology: { main: hydrogeologyCtrl.getRawValue() } };

    const formationTopsCtrl = this.formationTopsEdit.control();
    if (formationTopsCtrl) raw = { ...raw, subsurface: { ...raw.subsurface, formationTops: formationTopsCtrl.getRawValue() } };

    const topsRemarksCtrl = this.topsRemarksEdit.control();
    if (topsRemarksCtrl) raw = { ...raw, subsurface: { ...raw.subsurface, remarks: topsRemarksCtrl.getRawValue() } };

    const casingCtrl = this.casingEdit.control();
    if (casingCtrl) raw = { ...raw, subsurface: { ...raw.subsurface, casing: casingCtrl.getRawValue() } };

    const offsetWellsCtrl = this.offsetWellsEdit.control();
    if (offsetWellsCtrl) {
      raw = {
        ...raw,
        contacts: {
          ...raw.contacts,
          offsetWells: this.offsetWellsArrayControl(offsetWellsCtrl).getRawValue(),
          remarks: this.offsetWellsRemarksControl(offsetWellsCtrl).getRawValue(),
        },
      };
    }

    this.saveMergedRaw(raw);
    this.closeAllSections();
  }

  private closeAllSections(): void {
    this.requestReferenceEdit.close();
    this.programEdit.close();
    this.geologistContactEdit.close();
    this.hydrogeologyEdit.close();
    this.formationTopsEdit.close();
    this.topsRemarksEdit.close();
    this.casingEdit.close();
    this.offsetWellsEdit.close();
    this.formationTopAdd.close();
    this.casingAdd.close();
    this.offsetWellAdd.close();
  }

  protected submitForReview(): void {
    this.store.submitIrForReview(this.ir());
  }
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-detail-response.mapper.ts

```ts
import {
  IntegratedRequirement,
  IntegratedRequirementTestType,
  IntegratedRequirementWellDesignFlags,
} from '@models/wwell-planning/integrated-requirement.model';
import { isLegacyIrHtmlText, stripLegacyIrHtml } from './wwell-ir-legacy-html.util';
import {
  IrCasingRow,
  IrHeaderRow,
  IrHydrogeologyRow,
  IrLinkedWellRow,
  IrTopsRow,
  IrWaterRow,
  IrWellDesignRow,
  WwellIrDetailTableBlock,
  WwellIrTableName,
} from './wwell-ir-payload.model';

function normalizeRowKeys<TRow>(row: Record<string, unknown>): TRow {
  const normalized: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  for (const key of Object.keys(row)) {
    normalized[key.toLowerCase()] = row[key];
  }
  return normalized as TRow;
}

function rowsFor<TRow>(blocks: readonly WwellIrDetailTableBlock[], table: WwellIrTableName): readonly TRow[] {
  const block = blocks.find(candidate => candidate.table.toLowerCase() === table);
  return (block?.data ?? []).map(row => normalizeRowKeys<TRow>(row));
}

function resolveGeologistName(
  empNum: number | null | undefined,
  geologistNameByEmpNum: Readonly<Record<number, string>>,
  fallback: string | null,
): string | null {
  if (empNum == null) return fallback;
  return geologistNameByEmpNum[empNum] ?? fallback;
}

function parseH2sPpm(h2s: string | null | undefined): number | null {
  if (!h2s) return null;
  const value = parseFloat(h2s);
  return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null;
}

function normalizeFlag(value: unknown): 'Y' | 'N' {
  return value === 'Y' ? 'Y' : 'N';
}

function normalizeRemarks(value: unknown): string | null {
  return typeof value === 'string' && value ? value : null;
}

function mapWellDesignFlags(row: IrWellDesignRow | undefined): IntegratedRequirementWellDesignFlags | null {
  if (!row) return null;
  return {
    ohFlg: normalizeFlag(row.oh_flg),
    ohRemarks: normalizeRemarks(row.oh_remarks),
    gpFlg: normalizeFlag(row.gp_flg),
    gpRemarks: normalizeRemarks(row.gp_remarks),
    perforatedFlg: normalizeFlag(row.perforated_flg),
    perforatedRemarks: normalizeRemarks(row.perforated_remarks),
    lsFlg: normalizeFlag(row.ls_flg),
    lsRemarks: normalizeRemarks(row.ls_remarks),
  };
}

function resolveLegacyText(raw: string | null | undefined, fallback: string): string {
  return raw != null ? stripLegacyIrHtml(raw) : fallback;
}

export function mergeIrDetailBlocksIntoIntegratedRequirement(
  existing: IntegratedRequirement,
  blocks: readonly WwellIrDetailTableBlock[],
  geologistNameByEmpNum: Readonly<Record<number, string>> = {},
): IntegratedRequirement {
  const header = rowsFor<IrHeaderRow>(blocks, 'exad_gwd_ir_header')[0] as IrHeaderRow | undefined;
  const casingRows = rowsFor<IrCasingRow>(blocks, 'exad_gwd_ir_casing');
  const topsRows = rowsFor<IrTopsRow>(blocks, 'exad_gwd_ir_tops');
  const hydroRow = rowsFor<IrHydrogeologyRow>(blocks, 'exad_gwd_ir_hydrogeology')[0] as IrHydrogeologyRow | undefined;
  const waterRows = rowsFor<IrWaterRow>(blocks, 'exad_gwd_ir_water');
  const linkedWell = rowsFor<IrLinkedWellRow>(blocks, 'exad_rcd_prewap')[0] as IrLinkedWellRow | undefined;
  const wellDesignRow = rowsFor<IrWellDesignRow>(blocks, 'exad_gwd_well_design')[0] as IrWellDesignRow | undefined;
  const completionText = resolveLegacyText(header?.completion ?? header?.cmpl_type, existing.programDetails.completion);
  const wellDesignRowBelongsToThisIr = !!wellDesignRow && !!completionText;
  const hasLegacyMarkup = [
    header?.completion ?? header?.cmpl_type,
    header?.acidization,
    header?.dt_rmks,
    header?.logging,
    header?.mud_rmks,
    header?.sampling,
    header?.flow_rmks,
    header?.dev_pump_test,
  ].some(isLegacyIrHtmlText);

  return {
    ...existing,
    hasLegacyMarkup,
    wellDesignSeq: wellDesignRowBelongsToThisIr ? wellDesignRow.wd_seq : existing.wellDesignSeq,
    sapWfId: header?.sap_wf_id ?? existing.sapWfId,
    requestReference: {
      ...existing.requestReference,
      linkedRequestId: header?.prewap_seq != null ? String(header.prewap_seq) : existing.requestReference.linkedRequestId,
      letterNumber: header?.ltr_no ?? existing.requestReference.letterNumber,
      wellName: linkedWell ? `${linkedWell.w_prim_hid ?? ''}_${linkedWell.w_num ?? ''}` : existing.requestReference.wellName,
      surfaceElevationFtAsl: linkedWell?.sv_surf_elevf ?? existing.requestReference.surfaceElevationFtAsl,
      coordinates:
        linkedWell?.surfacen != null && linkedWell?.surfacee != null
          ? `${linkedWell.surfacen}°N, ${linkedWell.surfacee}°E`
          : existing.requestReference.coordinates,
      targetDepthFt: linkedWell?.target_dpth ?? existing.requestReference.targetDepthFt,
      targetAquifer: linkedWell?.target_form ?? existing.requestReference.targetAquifer,
      supportingBusiness: linkedWell?.expl_wb_name ?? existing.requestReference.supportingBusiness,
    },
    formationTops: topsRows.map(row => ({
      name: row.st_long_cd,
      depthFt: row.plan_tvd_dpth,
      subSeaFtAsl: row.plan_ss_dpth ?? null,
    })),
    topsRemarks: header?.tops_remarks ?? existing.topsRemarks,
    contacts: {
      geologistPrimary:
        resolveGeologistName(header?.cont_geologist, geologistNameByEmpNum, existing.contacts.geologistPrimary) ??
        existing.contacts.geologistPrimary,
      geologistSecondary: resolveGeologistName(header?.sub_cont_geologist, geologistNameByEmpNum, existing.contacts.geologistSecondary),
    },
    offsetWells: waterRows.map(row => ({
      epANum: row.ep_a_num,
      distanceKm: row.distanct ?? null,
      location: row.direction ?? null,
      aquifer: row.aquifer ?? null,
      totalDepthFt: row.td ?? null,
      testDurationMin: row.test_duration ?? null,
      rateGpm: row.rate ?? null,
      rpm: row.rpm ?? null,
      swlFt: row.swl ?? null,
      pwlFt: row.final_pwl ?? null,
      drawdownFt: row.drawdown ?? null,
      specificCapacity: row.spec_capacity ?? null,
      tdsPpm: row.field_tds ?? null,
      h2sPpm: row.h2s ?? null,
    })),
    casing: casingRows.map(row => ({
      type: row.csg_type,
      sizeIn: row.csg_size,
      depthFtBgl: row.csg_depth,
      tolFtBgl: null,
      remarks: row.csg_remarks ?? null,
    })),
    programDetails: {
      ...existing.programDetails,
      completion: completionText,
      acidization: resolveLegacyText(header?.acidization, existing.programDetails.acidization),
      transmission: resolveLegacyText(header?.dt_rmks, existing.programDetails.transmission),
      developmentTesting: {
        logging: resolveLegacyText(header?.logging, existing.programDetails.developmentTesting.logging),
        mudLogging: resolveLegacyText(header?.mud_rmks, existing.programDetails.developmentTesting.mudLogging),
        sampling: resolveLegacyText(header?.sampling, existing.programDetails.developmentTesting.sampling),
        flowTest: resolveLegacyText(header?.flow_rmks, existing.programDetails.developmentTesting.flowTest),
        remarks: resolveLegacyText(header?.dev_pump_test, existing.programDetails.developmentTesting.remarks),
      },
      wellDesignFlags: wellDesignRowBelongsToThisIr ? mapWellDesignFlags(wellDesignRow) : existing.programDetails.wellDesignFlags,
    },
    hydrogeology: hydroRow
      ? {
          aquifer: hydroRow.st_long_cd,
          estimatedTdsPpm: hydroRow.est_water_quality,
          h2sPpm: parseH2sPpm(hydroRow.h2s),
          estimatedPumpDepthFt: wellDesignRow?.pump_lvl ?? existing.hydrogeology.estimatedPumpDepthFt,
        }
      : existing.hydrogeology,
    testProgram: {
      ...existing.testProgram,
      testType: (header?.test_type as IntegratedRequirementTestType | null | undefined) ?? existing.testProgram.testType,
      estimatedRateGpm: hydroRow?.est_productivity ?? existing.testProgram.estimatedRateGpm,
      estimatedWaterLevelFt: hydroRow?.est_sta_water_lvl ?? existing.testProgram.estimatedWaterLevelFt,
      siwhpPsi: hydroRow?.siwhp ?? existing.testProgram.siwhpPsi,
    },
  };
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-legacy-html.util.ts

```ts
/**
 * Several `exad_gwd_ir_header` free-text remarks columns — `completion`, `acidization`, `logging`,
 * `mud_rmks`, `sampling`, `flow_rmks` — carry raw markup on older records instead of plain text:
 * a "Save As HTML" dump straight out of a legacy Windows RTF/rich-text control, e.g.
 * `<HTML><BODY><P ALIGN="left"><FONT FACE="Arial" SIZE="11" COLOR="#000000">7" diameter, 0.012"
 * slots</FONT></P></BODY></HTML>`, sometimes several such `<HTML>...</HTML>` documents
 * concatenated back-to-back in one column value (each `<P>` was originally its own remark line).
 * Other records hold bare fragments from a browser rich-text editor instead — no `<HTML>` wrapper,
 * e.g. `<p>The screen should be jetted…</p><p><br></p>`, possibly followed by plain option lines
 * (`Open Hole`, `Screened`). Both shapes are handled the same way.
 * Left as-is, every place that shows one of these fields — the KV read cards
 * (wwell-ir-detail-rows.util.ts), the editor's combo/select inputs, the printable Letter view —
 * renders the raw tags instead of the remark text.
 *
 * `mergeIrDetailBlocksIntoIntegratedRequirement` (wwell-ir-detail-response.mapper.ts) is the one
 * place backend column values become `IntegratedRequirement` field values, for both the initial
 * detail fetch on selecting a record and the post-save refetch — running every legacy-prone field
 * through `stripLegacyIrHtml` there means every downstream reader (including the editor's own
 * raw-form mapper, which a re-save resends unchanged for any section the user didn't open) only
 * ever sees plain text, so a save after that point persists plain text back to the backend even
 * though the column itself started out as markup.
 */
/** A real markup tag — an `<HTML>` dump's or a rich-text fragment's. A whitelist of tag names rather
 *  than "anything in angle brackets", so plain text with a stray `<` (`PWL < SWL`, `<5000 ppm`) is
 *  never mistaken for markup and rewritten. */
const LEGACY_MARKUP_TAG_PATTERN =
  /<\/?(?:html|body|p|br|div|span|font|b|i|u|s|strong|em|ul|ol|li|h[1-6]|a|sub|sup|hr|pre|blockquote|table|tr|td|th)(?:[\s/][^>]*)?>/i;

/** Where a legacy value's own line breaks were: a `<br>`, or the end of a block element. */
const LINE_BREAK_TAG_PATTERN = /<br\s*\/?>|<\/(?:p|div|li|h[1-6]|tr)>/gi;

/** True when `value` carries legacy markup — an `<HTML>...` dump or a bare `<p>…</p>` fragment —
 *  rather than plain text. A brand-new record's plain, user-typed remarks (or a blank value)
 *  always fail this check. */
export function isLegacyIrHtmlText(value: string | null | undefined): boolean {
  return !!value && LEGACY_MARKUP_TAG_PATTERN.test(value);
}

/** Collapses runs of horizontal whitespace within each line (left by adjacent `<FONT>` run
 *  boundaries) while keeping real paragraph breaks (`\n`, inserted for `<P>`/`<BR>`/separate
 *  `<HTML>` documents below) intact, and drops any resulting blank line. */
function collapseWhitespacePreservingLines(text: string): string {
  return text
    .split('\n')
    .map(line => line.replace(/[^\S\n]+/g, ' ').trim())
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join('\n');
}

/**
 * Strips a legacy `exad_gwd_ir_header` rich-text remarks value down to the plain text it actually
 * displays. Values that AREN'T legacy markup (a plain string, blank, or already-cleaned text from
 * a prior parse) pass through unchanged, so this is safe to call unconditionally on every
 * legacy-prone field rather than only on ones already known to carry HTML.
 *
 * Uses `DOMParser` rather than a hand-rolled tag-stripping regex so that whitespace between
 * adjacent `<FONT>` runs is preserved (or not) exactly as a browser would actually render that
 * markup — the legacy source sometimes omits the space between two runs, and the "exact
 * information" is whatever text the field actually displayed, gaps included.
 */
export function stripLegacyIrHtml(value: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!value) return '';
  if (!isLegacyIrHtmlText(value)) return value.trim();

  const parser = new DOMParser();
  const paragraphs = value
    // Multiple concatenated `<HTML>...</HTML>` documents in one column value are separate
    // remark lines saved back-to-back — split them apart before parsing, since DOMParser only
    // ever reads the first document out of a string containing several.
    .split(/(?=<html[\s>])/i)
    .map(document => document.replace(LINE_BREAK_TAG_PATTERN, '\n'))
    .map(document => parser.parseFromString(document, 'text/html').body.textContent ?? '')
    .map(collapseWhitespacePreservingLines)
    .filter(Boolean);

  return paragraphs.join('\n');
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-payload.mapper.ts

```ts
import {
  IntegratedRequirement,
  IntegratedRequirementCasingRow,
  IntegratedRequirementFormationTop,
  IntegratedRequirementHydrogeology,
  IntegratedRequirementOffsetWell,
  IntegratedRequirementProgramDetails,
  IntegratedRequirementTestProgram,
} from '@models/wwell-planning/integrated-requirement.model';
import {
  WwellIrFormRawValue,
  WwellIrRawCasingRow,
  WwellIrRawFormationTop,
  WwellIrRawHydrology,
  WwellIrRawOffsetWell,
  WwellIrRawProgramMain,
} from './wwell-ir-form.types';
import {
  IrCasingRow,
  IrHeaderRow,
  IrHydrogeologyRow,
  IrRowAction,
  IrTopsRow,
  IrWaterRow,
  IrWellDesignRow,
  WwellIrBulkSavePayload,
} from './wwell-ir-payload.model';
import { deriveCompletionElementFlags } from './wwell-ir-wellbore-diagram.mapper';

/**
 * Carries the previously-assigned ir_seq (if any) so a repeat save UPDATEs instead of
 * re-INSERTing, plus (when known) the record as it was last loaded/saved — used to diff each
 * repeatable row's OWN action instead of inheriting one blanket action for the whole payload.
 * `existing` is intentionally distinguished from "omitted": omitting it (or passing `null`) falls
 * back to the old whole-payload action (every row shares `resolveRowAction(irSeq)`) — the only
 * safe choice when the caller has no snapshot to diff against, since guessing would otherwise
 * risk re-labeling an already-persisted row as a fresh INSERT (duplicate) or a still-current row
 * as DELETE (data loss).
 */
export interface WwellIrSaveContext {
  readonly irSeq: number | null;
  readonly existing?: Pick<
    IntegratedRequirement,
    'casing' | 'formationTops' | 'hydrogeology' | 'testProgram' | 'offsetWells' | 'wellDesignSeq' | 'programDetails'
  > | null;
  /** Live FULL_NAME → EMPNUM map (GET /gwd-ops-members, see WwellIrStore's
   *  `geologistEmpNumByName`) used to resolve cont_geologist/sub_cont_geologist below. Only omitted
   *  by tests that don't exercise geologist resolution — every real save call site supplies it. */
  readonly geologistEmpNumByName?: Readonly<Record<string, number>>;
}

const NEW_IR_CONTEXT: WwellIrSaveContext = { irSeq: null, existing: null };

function resolveRowAction(irSeq: number | null): IrRowAction {
  return irSeq === null ? 'INSERT' : 'UPDATE';
}

const isBlankCasingRow = (row: WwellIrRawCasingRow): boolean =>
  !row.type && !row.sizeIn && !row.remarks && row.depthFtBgl == null;

const isBlankFormationTop = (row: WwellIrRawFormationTop): boolean =>
  !row.name && row.depthFt == null && row.subSeaFtAsl == null;

/** Natural keys the backend's UPDATE/DELETE branches match repeatable rows by (see
 *  EXAD_AGWA_IUD_PKG.save_casing/save_tops in 05-agwa-iud-package.sql) — a row whose key isn't
 *  already present in `existing` is genuinely new and must be sent as INSERT, not UPDATE, or the
 *  backend's `WHERE <key match>` finds nothing and silently drops it. */
export const casingKey = (row: { readonly type: string; readonly sizeIn: string }): string => `${row.type} ${row.sizeIn}`;
export const topKey = (row: { readonly name: string }): string => row.name;

/** Only the columns save_casing's/save_tops' UPDATE branch actually writes (type/size are the
 *  match key itself, not a writable column) — comparing anything else could flag a row "changed"
 *  when nothing the backend would touch actually differs. */
const casingRowUnchanged = (existingRow: IntegratedRequirementCasingRow, row: WwellIrRawCasingRow): boolean =>
  existingRow.depthFtBgl === (row.depthFtBgl ?? 0) && (existingRow.remarks ?? null) === (row.remarks || null);

const topRowUnchanged = (existingRow: IntegratedRequirementFormationTop, row: WwellIrRawFormationTop): boolean =>
  existingRow.depthFt === (row.depthFt ?? 0) && (existingRow.subSeaFtAsl ?? null) === (row.subSeaFtAsl ?? null);

function buildCasingRows(
  raw: readonly WwellIrRawCasingRow[],
  irSeq: number | null,
  existing: readonly IntegratedRequirementCasingRow[] | null | undefined,
): IrCasingRow[] {
  const currentRows = raw.filter(row => !isBlankCasingRow(row));

  if (existing == null) {
    const action = resolveRowAction(irSeq);
    return currentRows.map(row => ({
      action,
      ir_seq: irSeq,
      csg_size: row.sizeIn,
      csg_type: row.type,
      csg_depth: row.depthFtBgl ?? 0,
      csg_remarks: row.remarks || null,
    }));
  }

  const existingByKey = new Map(existing.map(row => [casingKey(row), row]));
  const keptKeys = new Set<string>();

  // A row this save didn't actually change is sent as '' (no-op), not a redundant UPDATE — see
  // IrRowAction's doc comment: the backend's per-row action switch then skips the DML entirely
  // instead of re-writing a row with the exact values it already has on every single save.
  const upserts: IrCasingRow[] = currentRows.map(row => {
    const key = casingKey(row);
    const existingRow = existingByKey.get(key);
    if (existingRow) keptKeys.add(key);
    const action: IrRowAction = existingRow ? (casingRowUnchanged(existingRow, row) ? '' : 'UPDATE') : 'INSERT';
    return {
      action,
      ir_seq: existingRow ? irSeq : null,
      csg_size: row.sizeIn,
      csg_type: row.type,
      csg_depth: row.depthFtBgl ?? 0,
      csg_remarks: row.remarks || null,
    };
  });

  const deletes: IrCasingRow[] = existing
    .filter(row => !keptKeys.has(casingKey(row)))
    .map(row => ({
      action: 'DELETE',
      ir_seq: irSeq,
      csg_size: row.sizeIn,
      csg_type: row.type,
      csg_depth: row.depthFtBgl,
      csg_remarks: row.remarks,
    }));

  return [...upserts, ...deletes];
}

function buildTopsRows(
  raw: readonly WwellIrRawFormationTop[],
  irSeq: number | null,
  existing: readonly IntegratedRequirementFormationTop[] | null | undefined,
): IrTopsRow[] {
  const currentRows = raw.filter(row => !isBlankFormationTop(row));

  if (existing == null) {
    const action = resolveRowAction(irSeq);
    return currentRows.map(row => ({
      action,
      ir_seq: irSeq,
      st_long_cd: row.name,
      plan_tvd_dpth: row.depthFt ?? 0,
      plan_ss_dpth: row.subSeaFtAsl,
    }));
  }

  const existingByKey = new Map(existing.map(row => [topKey(row), row]));
  const keptKeys = new Set<string>();

  const upserts: IrTopsRow[] = currentRows.map(row => {
    const key = topKey(row);
    const existingRow = existingByKey.get(key);
    if (existingRow) keptKeys.add(key);
    const action: IrRowAction = existingRow ? (topRowUnchanged(existingRow, row) ? '' : 'UPDATE') : 'INSERT';
    return {
      action,
      ir_seq: existingRow ? irSeq : null,
      st_long_cd: row.name,
      plan_tvd_dpth: row.depthFt ?? 0,
      plan_ss_dpth: row.subSeaFtAsl,
    };
  });

  const deletes: IrTopsRow[] = existing
    .filter(row => !keptKeys.has(topKey(row)))
    .map(row => ({
      action: 'DELETE',
      ir_seq: irSeq,
      st_long_cd: row.name,
      plan_tvd_dpth: row.depthFt,
      plan_ss_dpth: row.subSeaFtAsl ?? null,
    }));

  return [...upserts, ...deletes];
}

/** True when nothing save_hydrogeology's UPDATE branch would actually write has changed —
 *  est_sta_water_lvl/est_productivity/siwhp come from the form's (and domain model's) testProgram
 *  section, not hydrogeology, so both are needed to tell "unchanged" from "changed". testType is
 *  included because it alone drives the `flowing` column (see buildHydrogeologyRows below) even
 *  though testType itself is persisted on the header row, not here. */
const hydrogeologyRowUnchanged = (
  existingHydro: IntegratedRequirementHydrogeology,
  existingTestProgram: IntegratedRequirementTestProgram,
  raw: WwellIrRawHydrology,
): boolean =>
  existingTestProgram.estimatedWaterLevelFt === raw.estimatedWaterLevelFt &&
  existingHydro.estimatedTdsPpm === raw.estimatedTdsPpm &&
  existingTestProgram.estimatedRateGpm === raw.estimatedRateGpm &&
  existingHydro.h2sPpm === raw.h2sPpm &&
  (existingTestProgram.siwhpPsi ?? null) === raw.siwhpPsi &&
  existingTestProgram.testType === raw.testType;

/** 'flow-test' → 'Y', 'pump-test' → 'N' — exad_gwd_ir_hydrogeology.flowing mirrors the same
 *  Test Type choice the header's own test_type column stores, denormalized onto this row too. */
const flowingFlag = (testType: string): 'Y' | 'N' => (testType === 'flow-test' ? 'Y' : 'N');

/** Only ever 0 or 1 row (the form has a single hydrogeology section, not a repeatable list), keyed
 *  by aquifer (st_long_cd) the same way the backend matches it — so switching to a different
 *  aquifer must DELETE the old row, not silently orphan it under a key nothing will ever match again. */
function buildHydrogeologyRows(
  raw: WwellIrRawHydrology,
  irSeq: number | null,
  existing: Pick<IntegratedRequirement, 'hydrogeology' | 'testProgram'> | null | undefined,
): IrHydrogeologyRow[] {
  const currentAquifer = raw.aquifer || null;

  if (existing == null) {
    if (!currentAquifer) return [];
    const action = resolveRowAction(irSeq);
    return [
      {
        action,
        ir_seq: irSeq,
        st_long_cd: currentAquifer,
        est_sta_water_lvl: raw.estimatedWaterLevelFt,
        est_water_quality: raw.estimatedTdsPpm,
        est_productivity: raw.estimatedRateGpm,
        flowing: flowingFlag(raw.testType),
        siwhp: raw.siwhpPsi,
        h2s: raw.h2sPpm != null ? `${raw.h2sPpm} ppm` : null,
      },
    ];
  }

  const existingAquifer = existing.hydrogeology.aquifer || null;
  const rows: IrHydrogeologyRow[] = [];

  if (existingAquifer && existingAquifer !== currentAquifer) {
    rows.push({
      action: 'DELETE',
      ir_seq: irSeq,
      st_long_cd: existingAquifer,
      est_sta_water_lvl: null,
      est_water_quality: null,
      est_productivity: null,
      flowing: null,
      siwhp: null,
      h2s: null,
    });
  }

  if (currentAquifer) {
    const isExisting = existingAquifer === currentAquifer;
    const action: IrRowAction = isExisting
      ? hydrogeologyRowUnchanged(existing.hydrogeology, existing.testProgram, raw)
        ? ''
        : 'UPDATE'
      : 'INSERT';
    rows.push({
      action,
      ir_seq: isExisting ? irSeq : null,
      st_long_cd: currentAquifer,
      est_sta_water_lvl: raw.estimatedWaterLevelFt,
      est_water_quality: raw.estimatedTdsPpm,
      est_productivity: raw.estimatedRateGpm,
      flowing: flowingFlag(raw.testType),
      siwhp: raw.siwhpPsi,
      h2s: raw.h2sPpm != null ? `${raw.h2sPpm} ppm` : null,
    });
  }

  return rows;
}

const isBlankOffsetWell = (row: WwellIrRawOffsetWell): boolean => row.epANum == null && !row.aquifer;

/** Natural key exad_gwd_ir_water is matched on — (ir_seq, ep_a_num) is its primary key. */
export const waterKey = (epANum: number): string => String(epANum);

/** Only the columns save_water's UPDATE branch writes — ep_a_num is the match key itself. */
const waterRowUnchanged = (existingRow: IntegratedRequirementOffsetWell, row: WwellIrRawOffsetWell): boolean =>
  (existingRow.distanceKm ?? null) === row.distanceKm &&
  (existingRow.location ?? null) === (row.location || null) &&
  (existingRow.aquifer ?? null) === (row.aquifer || null) &&
  (existingRow.totalDepthFt ?? null) === row.totalDepthFt &&
  (existingRow.testDurationMin ?? null) === row.testDurationMin &&
  (existingRow.rateGpm ?? null) === row.rateGpm &&
  (existingRow.rpm ?? null) === row.rpm &&
  (existingRow.swlFt ?? null) === row.swlFt &&
  (existingRow.pwlFt ?? null) === row.pwlFt &&
  (existingRow.drawdownFt ?? null) === row.drawdownFt &&
  (existingRow.specificCapacity ?? null) === row.specificCapacity &&
  (existingRow.tdsPpm ?? null) === row.tdsPpm &&
  (existingRow.h2sPpm ?? null) === row.h2sPpm;

/** `temp` is the one exad_gwd_ir_water column the form has no field for — always sent null. */
const toWaterRow = (row: WwellIrRawOffsetWell, action: IrRowAction, irSeq: number | null): IrWaterRow => ({
  action,
  ir_seq: irSeq,
  ep_a_num: row.epANum ?? 0,
  distanct: row.distanceKm,
  direction: row.location || null,
  aquifer: row.aquifer,
  td: row.totalDepthFt,
  rate: row.rateGpm,
  rpm: row.rpm,
  swl: row.swlFt,
  final_pwl: row.pwlFt,
  drawdown: row.drawdownFt,
  spec_capacity: row.specificCapacity,
  temp: null,
  field_tds: row.tdsPpm,
  test_duration: row.testDurationMin,
  h2s: row.h2sPpm,
});

function buildWaterRows(
  raw: readonly WwellIrRawOffsetWell[],
  irSeq: number | null,
  existing: readonly IntegratedRequirementOffsetWell[] | null | undefined,
): IrWaterRow[] {
  const currentRows = raw.filter(row => !isBlankOffsetWell(row));

  if (existing == null) {
    const action = resolveRowAction(irSeq);
    return currentRows.map(row => toWaterRow(row, action, irSeq));
  }

  const existingByKey = new Map(existing.map(row => [waterKey(row.epANum), row]));
  const keptKeys = new Set<string>();

  const upserts: IrWaterRow[] = currentRows.map(row => {
    const key = waterKey(row.epANum ?? 0);
    const existingRow = existingByKey.get(key);
    if (existingRow) keptKeys.add(key);
    const action: IrRowAction = existingRow ? (waterRowUnchanged(existingRow, row) ? '' : 'UPDATE') : 'INSERT';
    return toWaterRow(row, action, existingRow ? irSeq : null);
  });

  const deletes: IrWaterRow[] = existing
    .filter(row => !keptKeys.has(waterKey(row.epANum)))
    .map(row => toWaterRow(offsetWellToRaw(row), 'DELETE', irSeq));

  return [...upserts, ...deletes];
}

/** Domain → raw shape, so a DELETE row is built by the same toWaterRow() as every other action. */
const offsetWellToRaw = (row: IntegratedRequirementOffsetWell): WwellIrRawOffsetWell => ({
  epANum: row.epANum,
  distanceKm: row.distanceKm ?? null,
  location: row.location ?? '',
  aquifer: row.aquifer ?? '',
  totalDepthFt: row.totalDepthFt ?? null,
  testDurationMin: row.testDurationMin ?? null,
  rateGpm: row.rateGpm ?? null,
  rpm: row.rpm ?? null,
  swlFt: row.swlFt ?? null,
  pwlFt: row.pwlFt ?? null,
  drawdownFt: row.drawdownFt ?? null,
  specificCapacity: row.specificCapacity ?? null,
  tdsPpm: row.tdsPpm ?? null,
  h2sPpm: row.h2sPpm ?? null,
});

/** Resolves a geologist's display name (FULL_NAME, as picked from the Geologist Contact dropdown)
 *  to the real EMPNUM exad_gwd_ir_header.cont_geologist/sub_cont_geologist expects, via the live
 *  map built from GET /gwd-ops-members — see WwellIrSaveContext.geologistEmpNumByName. */
function resolveGeologistId(name: string, empNumByName: Readonly<Record<string, number>> | undefined): number | null {
  return name && empNumByName ? (empNumByName[name] ?? null) : null;
}

/**
 * Estimated Pump Depth (pump_lvl) plus the eight completion-element columns (derived from
 * Completion/Open Hole Size via deriveCompletionElementFlags — see IrWellDesignRow's own doc
 * comment) are mapped onto exad_gwd_well_design. Unlike every other block above, this table is
 * keyed by wd_seq (not ir_seq): with no wd_seq yet known (no detail fetch has resolved one), a row
 * is only worth INSERTing once there's an actual value to save (a pump depth, or a real Completion
 * selection) and a linked request to hang it off of — otherwise there is nothing to persist yet.
 *
 * DATA-LOSS CAVEAT: update_ir_data_prc's exad_gwd_well_design MERGE (docs/ir/update_ir_data_prc.sql)
 * is a blanket `WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET <every column the proc's JSON_TABLE declares>` off
 * this same JSON row — a column this app's payload omits (design_rpt, status, design_phase,
 * w_prim_hid, w_num, sta_water_lvl, tds, completion_type, notes, publish_dt/flg/by, curr_flg) comes
 * back NULL from JSON_TABLE and gets written as NULL, not left alone. If a wd_seq this UPDATE
 * targets already carries real data from that other Well Design workflow, this silently wipes it.
 * That risk already existed for pump_lvl-only saves before this change; sending Completion more
 * often (it's now the primary driver, not just an occasional pump-depth edit) makes it fire more
 * often too. Not fixed here — would need either the backend proc to COALESCE(t.col, h.col) instead
 * of overwriting, or this app reading back and re-sending every other column untouched.
 */
function buildWellDesignRow(
  programMain: WwellIrRawProgramMain,
  pumpDepthFt: number | null,
  prewapSeq: number | null,
  existingWellDesignSeq: number | null | undefined,
  existingPumpDepthFt: number | null | undefined,
  existingProgramDetails: Pick<IntegratedRequirementProgramDetails, 'completion' | 'openHoleSizeIn'> | null | undefined,
): IrWellDesignRow | null {
  const wdSeq = existingWellDesignSeq ?? null;
  const flags = deriveCompletionElementFlags(programMain.completion, programMain.openHoleSizeIn || null);
  // IntegratedRequirementWellDesignFlags is camelCase (domain-model shape, shared with the
  // wellbore-diagram mapper); the wire payload below needs update_ir_data_prc's own snake_case
  // column names (see docs/ir/update_ir_data_prc.sql) — translate rather than rename one side.
  const flagColumns = {
    oh_flg: flags.ohFlg,
    oh_remarks: flags.ohRemarks,
    gp_flg: flags.gpFlg,
    gp_remarks: flags.gpRemarks,
    perforated_flg: flags.perforatedFlg,
    perforated_remarks: flags.perforatedRemarks,
    ls_flg: flags.lsFlg,
    ls_remarks: flags.lsRemarks,
  };

  if (wdSeq != null) {
    const unchanged =
      (existingPumpDepthFt ?? null) === pumpDepthFt &&
      (existingProgramDetails?.completion ?? '') === programMain.completion &&
      (existingProgramDetails?.openHoleSizeIn ?? null) === (programMain.openHoleSizeIn || null);
    const action: IrRowAction = unchanged ? '' : 'UPDATE';
    return { action, wd_seq: wdSeq, prewap_seq: prewapSeq, pump_lvl: pumpDepthFt, ...flagColumns };
  }

  if ((pumpDepthFt == null && !programMain.completion) || prewapSeq == null) return null;
  return { action: 'INSERT', wd_seq: null, prewap_seq: prewapSeq, pump_lvl: pumpDepthFt, ...flagColumns };
}

/** Bridges the wizard's raw form value to the exad_gwd_ir_* bulk-save wire format — see wwell-ir-payload.model.ts. */
export function buildWwellIrBulkSavePayload(
  raw: WwellIrFormRawValue,
  context: WwellIrSaveContext = NEW_IR_CONTEXT,
): WwellIrBulkSavePayload {
  const { irSeq, existing } = context;
  const headerAction = resolveRowAction(irSeq);
  const prewapSeq = raw.reference.main.linkedRequestId ? Number(raw.reference.main.linkedRequestId) : null;

  const header: IrHeaderRow = {
    action: headerAction,
    ir_seq: irSeq,
    prewap_seq: prewapSeq,
    ltr_no: raw.reference.main.letterNumber || null,
    screen: '',
    sampling: raw.programDetails.developmentTesting.sampling || null,
    logging: raw.programDetails.developmentTesting.logging || null,
    tops_remarks: raw.subsurface.remarks.topsRemarks || null,
    publish_dt: null,
    publish_flg: 'N', // flips once the approval workflow publishes this record, not at authoring time
    curr_flg: 'Y',
    publish_by: null,
    cont_geologist: resolveGeologistId(raw.contacts.geologist.geologistPrimary, context.geologistEmpNumByName),
    sub_cont_geologist: resolveGeologistId(raw.contacts.geologist.geologistSecondary, context.geologistEmpNumByName),
    water_remarks: null,
    gen_remarks: null,
    completion: raw.programDetails.main.completion || null,
    cmpl_type: null,
    acidization: raw.programDetails.main.acidization || null,
    trns_type: null,
    dt_rmks: raw.programDetails.main.transmission || null,
    mud_rmks: raw.programDetails.developmentTesting.mudLogging || null,
    test_type: raw.hydrogeology.main.testType || null,
    flow_rmks: raw.programDetails.developmentTesting.flowTest || null,
    dev_pump_test: raw.programDetails.developmentTesting.remarks || null,
  };

  const casing = buildCasingRows(raw.subsurface.casing, irSeq, existing?.casing);
  const tops = buildTopsRows(raw.subsurface.formationTops, irSeq, existing?.formationTops);
  const hydrogeology = buildHydrogeologyRows(raw.hydrogeology.main, irSeq, existing);
  const water = buildWaterRows(raw.contacts.offsetWells, irSeq, existing?.offsetWells);
  const wellDesign = buildWellDesignRow(
    raw.programDetails.main,
    raw.hydrogeology.main.estimatedPumpDepthFt,
    prewapSeq,
    existing?.wellDesignSeq,
    existing?.hydrogeology.estimatedPumpDepthFt,
    existing?.programDetails,
  );

  return [
    { table: 'exad_gwd_ir_header', data: [header] },
    { table: 'exad_gwd_ir_casing', data: casing.length ? casing : null },
    { table: 'exad_gwd_ir_hydrogeology', data: hydrogeology.length ? hydrogeology : null },
    { table: 'exad_gwd_ir_tops', data: tops.length ? tops : null },
    { table: 'exad_gwd_ir_water', data: water.length ? water : null },
    { table: 'exad_gwd_well_design', data: wellDesign ? [wellDesign] : null },
  ];
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir.component.html

```html
<section class="wwell-ir">
  <div class="wwell-ir__master">
    <header class="wwell-ir__header">
      <div class="wwell-ir__heading">
        <h1 class="wwell-ir__title">Integrated Requirements</h1>
        <span class="wwell-ir__count">{{ rows().length }}</span>
      </div>

      <div class="wwell-ir__actions">
        <button
          type="button"
          mat-flat-button
          color="primary"
          class="wwell-ir__new-btn"
          [disabled]="store.creatingIr()"
          [attr.aria-busy]="store.creatingIr()"
          (click)="createNewIr()"
        >
          <span class="wwell-ir__new-inner">
            <span class="wwell-ir__new-icon" aria-hidden="true">
              @if (store.creatingIr()) {
                <span class="wwell-ir__new-spinner"></span>
              } @else {
                <mat-icon>add</mat-icon>
              }
            </span>
            {{ store.creatingIr() ? 'Creating&hellip;' : 'New IR' }}
          </span>
        </button>
      </div>
    </header>

    <div class="wwell-ir__filter-bar">
      <button
        type="button"
        class="wwell-ir__lifecycle-toggle"
        [class.wwell-ir__lifecycle-toggle--inactive]="lifecycleFilter() === 'inactive'"
        [attr.aria-label]="lifecycleFilter() === 'active' ? 'Showing active records — click for inactive' : 'Showing inactive records — click for active'"
        [attr.title]="lifecycleFilter() === 'active' ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'"
        (click)="toggleLifecycleFilter()"
      >
        <mat-icon>{{ lifecycleFilter() === 'active' ? 'toggle_on' : 'toggle_off' }}</mat-icon>
      </button>

      @if (lifecycleFilter() === 'active') {
        <div class="wwell-ir__status-chips" role="group" aria-label="Status filter" appWwellCollapseAnimate>
          @for (status of statusChips; track status) {
            <button
              type="button"
              class="wwell-ir__status-chip"
              [class.wwell-ir__status-chip--active]="statusFilter() === status"
              (click)="setStatusFilter(status)"
            >
              {{ statusChipLabel(status) }} &middot; {{ statusChipCount(status) }}
            </button>
          }
        </div>
      }
    </div>

    <label class="wwell-ir__search">
      <mat-icon class="wwell-ir__search-icon">search</mat-icon>
      <input
        type="search"
        placeholder="Search code, well&hellip;"
        aria-label="Search by code, well, request or client"
        title="Search by code, well, request or client"
        [ngModel]="searchTerm()"
        (ngModelChange)="onSearchChange($event)"
      />
    </label>

    @if (isLoading()) {
      <ul class="wwell-ir__list wwell-ir__list--skeleton" aria-hidden="true">
        @for (n of skeletonRows; track n) {
          <li class="wwell-ir__row wwell-ir__skeleton-row">
            <div class="wwell-ir__row-top">
              <span class="wwell-ir__skeleton-bar wwell-ir__skeleton-bar--code"></span>
              <span class="wwell-ir__skeleton-bar wwell-ir__skeleton-bar--badge"></span>
            </div>
            <span class="wwell-ir__skeleton-bar wwell-ir__skeleton-bar--name"></span>
            <span class="wwell-ir__skeleton-bar wwell-ir__skeleton-bar--meta"></span>
          </li>
        }
      </ul>
    } @else if (loadError()) {
      <div class="wwell-ir__status wwell-ir__status--error">{{ loadError() }}</div>
    } @else if (rows().length === 0) {
      <div class="wwell-ir__status">No integrated requirements found.</div>
    } @else {
      <cdk-virtual-scroll-viewport
        [itemSize]="rowItemSize"
        class="wwell-ir__viewport"
        #listHost
        role="list"
        aria-label="Integrated requirements"
      >
        <div
          *cdkVirtualFor="let ir of rows(); trackBy: trackByIrRow"
          class="wwell-ir__row-item"
          [style.height.px]="rowItemSize"
          role="listitem"
        >
          <button
            type="button"
            class="wwell-ir__row"
            [class.wwell-ir__row--active]="ir.irSeq === selectedIrSeq()"
            [class.wwell-ir__row--pending]="ir.irSeq === pendingId()"
            [style.borderLeftColor]="chainBorderColor(ir.chain)"
            [disabled]="rowIrSeq(ir) === null"
            (click)="selectIrRow(ir)"
          >
            <div class="wwell-ir__row-top">
              <span class="wwell-ir__row-code">{{ ir.code }}</span>
              @if (hasKnownStatus(ir)) {
                <span
                  class="wwell-ir__badge"
                  [style.background]="statusMeta[ir.status].bg"
                  [style.color]="statusMeta[ir.status].color"
                >
                  <span class="wwell-ir__badge-dot" [style.background]="statusMeta[ir.status].dot"></span>
                  {{ ir.sapWfStatusText ?? statusMeta[ir.status].label }}
                </span>
              }
            </div>
            @if (ir.requestReference.wellName) {
              <p class="wwell-ir__row-name">{{ ir.requestReference.wellName }}</p>
            }
            <p class="wwell-ir__row-meta">{{ ir.requestReference.client }} &middot; Updated {{ ir.updatedDate }}</p>
          </button>

          @if (rowIrSeq(ir); as irSeq) {
            <button
              type="button"
              mat-icon-button
              class="wwell-ir__view-btn"
              aria-label="View integrated requirement"
              title="View"
              (click)="$event.stopPropagation(); viewIr(irSeq)"
            >
              <mat-icon>visibility</mat-icon>
            </button>
          }
        </div>
      </cdk-virtual-scroll-viewport>
    }
  </div>

  <div class="wwell-ir__detail">
    @if (isSelecting()) {
      <!-- 'edit' is the default/sticky detail mode (see WwellIrDetailMode's own doc comment), so
           most selections land on the editor's rail+section+table layout, not the read-only
           stepper — the shimmer below matches whichever one is about to render. -->
      @if (detailMode() === 'view') {
        <app-wwell-detail-loading />
      } @else {
        <app-wwell-ir-editor-loading [viewMode]="editorViewMode()" />
      }
    } @else if (selectedIr(); as ir) {
      <div class="wwell-ir__detail-panel" [style.--chain-color]="chainBorderColor(ir.chain)" #detailHost>
        @if (detailMode() === 'view') {
          <div class="wwell-ir__view-layout">
            <app-wwell-ir-view
              class="wwell-ir__view-main"
              [ir]="ir"
              [linkedRequestWellOptions]="store.linkedRequestWellOptions()"
              (editRequested)="setDetailMode('edit')"
            />
            <aside class="wwell-ir__view-sidebar">
              <div class="wwell-ir__approval-card">
                <header class="wwell-ir__approval-head">
                  <span class="wwell-ir__approval-icon">
                    <mat-icon>verified</mat-icon>
                  </span>
                  <div class="wwell-ir__approval-head-text">
                    <h2 class="wwell-ir__approval-title">Approval Workflow</h2>
                    @if (store.irApprovalTimelineLoading()) {
                      <p class="wwell-ir__approval-progress">Loading status…</p>
                    } @else if (ir.sapWfId === null) {
                      <p class="wwell-ir__approval-progress">Not yet submitted for review.</p>
                    } @else {
                      <p class="wwell-ir__approval-progress">{{ approvedStepCount(ir.chain) }} of {{ ir.chain.length }} approved</p>
                    }
                  </div>
                  @if (ir.sapWfId !== null) {
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      mat-icon-button
                      class="wwell-ir__approval-refresh-btn"
                      aria-label="Refresh approval status"
                      title="Refresh approval status"
                      [disabled]="store.irApprovalTimelineLoading()"
                      (click)="refreshApprovalStatus(ir)"
                    >
                      <mat-icon>refresh</mat-icon>
                    </button>
                  }
                </header>
                @if (ir.sapWfId !== null) {
                  <app-wwell-approval-timeline [steps]="ir.chain" [orientation]="'vertical'" />
                }
              </div>
            </aside>
          </div>
        } @else {
          <app-wwell-ir-editor [ir]="ir" [(viewMode)]="editorViewMode" />
        }
      </div>
    } @else {
      <div class="wwell-ir__empty">
        <mat-icon>description</mat-icon>
        <p>Select an integrated requirement to view its details.</p>
      </div>
    }
  </div>
</section>
```
