## [A] src/app/layout/_shell-theme.scss

```scss
// Shell theme — the tokens behind the top bar and left nav ("Monolith": flat, hairline, pure monochrome).
//
// Global on purpose, like `_wwell-theme.scss`: component-scoped SCSS can't target `:root`. Light/dark
// follow the same `data-wp-theme` attribute `WwellThemeService` already writes on <html>; the same
// service writes `data-shell="mono" | "legacy"` for the Appearance popover's Legacy switch.
//
// Legacy is the old navy shell. Its colours stay hard-coded in the `*-legacy` partials next to each
// component, so this file only hands it the one thing the page layout shares: the bar height.
// Every text tier below stays ≥ 4.5:1 on the bar and rail surface it sits on.

:root {
  --shell-bar-h: 56px;

  // Light: "Paper"
  --shell-back: #ededed;
  --shell-bar: #ffffff;
  --shell-rail: #fafafa;
  --shell-hair: #e4e4e4;
  --shell-hair-strong: #cfcfcf;
  --shell-text: #0a0a0a;
  --shell-muted: #6b6b6b;
  --shell-label: #6b6b6b;
  --shell-hover: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04);
  --shell-press: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.07);
  --shell-active-bg: #0a0a0a; // the inverted block behind the current page
  --shell-active-text: #ffffff;
  --shell-avatar-bg: #0a0a0a;
  --shell-avatar-text: #ffffff;
  --shell-signal: #d92d20; // red only where it means something: notification badge, lock chip
  --shell-on-signal: #ffffff;
  --shell-lock-bg: rgba(220, 38, 38, 0.08);
  --shell-lock-bd: rgba(220, 38, 38, 0.3);
  --shell-lock-fg: #c62828;
}

:root[data-wp-theme='dark'] {
  --shell-back: #050505;
  --shell-bar: #0a0a0a;
  --shell-rail: #0a0a0a;
  --shell-hair: #262626;
  --shell-hair-strong: #383838;
  --shell-text: #f4f4f4;
  --shell-muted: #949494;
  --shell-label: #808080;
  --shell-hover: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  --shell-press: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.09);
  --shell-active-bg: #f4f4f4;
  --shell-active-text: #0a0a0a;
  --shell-avatar-bg: #f4f4f4;
  --shell-avatar-text: #0a0a0a;
  --shell-lock-bg: rgba(220, 38, 38, 0.15);
  --shell-lock-bd: rgba(220, 38, 38, 0.3);
  --shell-lock-fg: #f87171;
}

// Today's bar was 62px; the mono bar is 56px. Everything that hangs off the bar reads this variable.
:root[data-shell='legacy'] {
  --shell-bar-h: 62px;
}

// The page layout's own backdrop follows the mono shell. Legacy keeps the navy `--bg-*` tokens from
// styles.scss untouched. (Every page paints its own surface over this; it only shows in the gaps.)
:root[data-shell='mono'] {
  --bg-app: var(--shell-back);
  --bg-content: var(--shell-back);
}
```

## [A] src/app/layout/header/_top-navbar-legacy.scss

```scss
// ── Layout constants ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
$nav-h:        62px;
$ease:         cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);
// Light-on-dark palette for the dark navy bar
$nav-text:     rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.92);
$nav-muted:    rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.55);
$nav-border:   rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.10);
$nav-hover-bg: rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.14);

// The navy top bar this app shipped with, kept as it was and scoped to `data-shell='legacy'` (set on
// <html> by WwellThemeService). The redesigned bar lives in _top-navbar-mono.scss. `:host` is in the
// component stylesheet, since both shells share it.

// ── Shell ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
:host-context([data-shell='legacy']) .top-navbar {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  height: $nav-h;
  padding: 0 20px 0 0;
  background: var(--bg-topbar);
  border-bottom: 1px solid $nav-border;
  box-shadow: 0 2px 20px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4);
  position: relative;
  z-index: 200;

  // Subtle grid texture
  &::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    background-image:
      linear-gradient(rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.035) 1px, transparent 1px),
      linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.035) 1px, transparent 1px);
    background-size: 32px 32px;
    pointer-events: none;
  }

  // Bottom accent line
  &::after {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    bottom: 0; left: 0; right: 0;
    height: 1px;
    background: linear-gradient(
      to right,
      transparent,
      rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.55) 30%,
      rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.55) 70%,
      transparent
    );
    pointer-events: none;
  }

  // ── Brand ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  &__brand {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    gap: 10px;
    padding: 0 14px;
    height: 100%;
    border-right: 1px solid $nav-border;
    min-width: 224px;
    transition: min-width 0.26s $ease;
  }

  // ── Hamburger ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  &__hamburger {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 4px;
    width: 36px;
    height: 36px;
    padding: 6px;
    background: transparent;
    border: none;
    cursor: pointer;
    border-radius: 8px;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    transition: background 0.15s;

    span {
      display: block;
      height: 2px;
      background: $nav-muted;
      border-radius: 2px;
      transition: background 0.2s, transform 0.25s;

      &:nth-child(1) { width: 20px; }
      &:nth-child(2) { width: 14px; }
      &:nth-child(3) { width: 17px; }
    }

    &:hover {
      background: $nav-hover-bg;
      span { background: #38bdf8; }
    }

    &--active span { background: #38bdf8; }
  }

  // ── Logo ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  &__logo {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 8px;
    flex: 1;
  }

  &__logo-icon {
    width: 34px;
    height: 34px;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    filter: drop-shadow(0 0 10px rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.65));
  }

  &__logo-text {
    font-family: 'DM Sans', system-ui, sans-serif;
    font-size: 19px;
    font-weight: 800;
    letter-spacing: 2.5px;
    color: #38bdf8;
    text-shadow: 0 0 18px rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.5);
    text-transform: uppercase;
  }

  // ── Center title ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  &__title {
    flex: 1;
    display: flex;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
    padding: 0 24px;

    h1 {
      margin: 0;
      font-family: 'DM Sans', system-ui, sans-serif;
      font-size: 15px;
      font-weight: 600;
      letter-spacing: 1.8px;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      color: $nav-text;
      white-space: nowrap;
    }
  }

  // ── Right: user area ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  &__user {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 14px;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    margin-left: auto;
  }

  &__notifications {
    position: relative;
  }

  &__icon-btn {
    width: 40px;
    height: 40px;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
    border: 1px solid $nav-border;
    border-radius: 10px;
    cursor: pointer;
    color: $nav-muted;
    transition: background 0.2s $ease, border-color 0.2s $ease, color 0.2s $ease, box-shadow 0.2s $ease;
    position: relative;

    svg { width: 18px; height: 18px; }

    &:hover {
      background: $nav-hover-bg;
      border-color: rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.4);
      color: #38bdf8;
      box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.2);
    }
  }

  &__divider {
    width: 1px;
    height: 26px;
    background: $nav-border;
  }

  // ── User info (name text) ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
  &__user-info {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: flex-end;
  }

  &__user-name {
    font-size: 13.5px;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: $nav-text;
    letter-spacing: 0.2px;
    line-height: 1.2;
  }

  &__last-login {
    font-size: 10px;
    color: $nav-text;
    font-weight: 700;
    letter-spacing: 0.15px;
    line-height: 1.2;
  }

  &__roles {
    display: flex;
    gap: 4px;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    justify-content: flex-end;
    margin-top: 3px;
  }

  &__role-chip {
    font-size: 9.5px;
    font-weight: 700;
    letter-spacing: 0.5px;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: #38bdf8;
    background: rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.10);
    border: 1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.28);
    border-radius: 4px;
    padding: 1px 6px;
    line-height: 1.6;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }

  // ── Menu ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  &__menu {
    position: relative;
  }

  &__menu-trigger {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 10px;
    padding: 4px 8px 4px 14px;
    background:
      linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.10), rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04)),
      rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.07);
    border: 1px solid $nav-border;
    border-radius: 999px;
    color: $nav-text;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition:
      background 0.2s $ease,
      border-color 0.2s $ease,
      box-shadow 0.2s $ease,
      transform 0.24s $ease;

    &:hover {
      background: $nav-hover-bg;
      border-color: rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.4);
      box-shadow: 0 0 14px rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.16);
      transform: translateY(-1px);
    }

    &:focus-visible {
      outline: 2px solid #38bdf8;
      outline-offset: 2px;
    }

    &--open {
      background:
        linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.22), rgba(3, 105, 161, 0.12)),
        rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
      border-color: rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.45);
      box-shadow: 0 12px 28px rgba(2, 132, 199, 0.24);

      .top-navbar__chevron {
        transform: rotate(180deg);
        color: #7dd3fc;
      }
    }
  }

  &__avatar {
    width: 36px;
    height: 36px;
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, #0ea5e9 0%, #0369a1 100%);
    border: 2px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.55);
    border-radius: 50%;
    font-size: 13px;
    font-weight: 800;
    color: #fff;
    letter-spacing: 0.5px;
    box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.45);
  }

  &__chevron {
    width: 15px;
    height: 15px;
    color: $nav-muted;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    transition: transform 0.25s $ease;
  }
}

// ── Dropdown popup ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
:host-context([data-shell='legacy']) {
  .top-navbar__menu-popup {
    width: 17rem;
    border-radius: 14px;
    border: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.08);
    background: #ffffff;
    box-shadow:
      0 20px 48px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.12),
      0 4px 16px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.06);
    position: absolute;
    top: calc(100% + 10px);
    right: 0;
    z-index: 300;
    overflow: hidden;
    transform-origin: top right;
    animation: top-navbar-menu-enter 200ms cubic-bezier(0.18, 0.89, 0.32, 1.28);
  }

  .top-navbar__profile-card {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
  }

  // ── Hero row: avatar + name ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
  .top-navbar__profile-hero {
    display: flex;
    align-items: flex-start;
    gap: 0.75rem;
    padding: 1rem;
    border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.06);
  }

  .top-navbar__profile-avatar {
    width: 2.4rem;
    height: 2.4rem;
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    border-radius: 50%;
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, #0ea5e9 0%, #0369a1 100%);
    border: 2px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.45);
    color: #fff;
    font-size: 0.95rem;
    font-weight: 800;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    box-shadow: 0 0 14px rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.4);
  }

  .top-navbar__profile-details {
    flex: 1;
    min-width: 0;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 0.3rem;
  }

  .top-navbar__profile-name {
    color: #0f172a;
    font-size: 0.9rem;
    font-weight: 800;
    white-space: nowrap;
    overflow: hidden;
    text-overflow: ellipsis;
    line-height: 1.3;
  }

  .top-navbar__profile-subtitle {
    display: flex;
    align-items: flex-start;
    gap: 0.35rem;
    font-size: 0.68rem;
    color: #64748b;
    font-weight: 500;
    white-space: normal;
    line-height: 1.45;

    svg {
      width: 11px;
      height: 11px;
      flex-shrink: 0;
      margin-top: 1px;
      color: #0ea5e9;
    }
  }

  // ── Body: roles ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  .top-navbar__profile-body {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 0.65rem;
    padding: 0.85rem 1rem;
    border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.06);
  }

  .top-navbar__profile-roles {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 5px;
  }

  .top-navbar__profile-role-chip {
    font-size: 9.5px;
    font-weight: 700;
    letter-spacing: 0.4px;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: #38bdf8;
    background: rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.10);
    border: 1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.22);
    border-radius: 4px;
    padding: 2px 7px;
    line-height: 1.6;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }

  // ── Logout ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  .top-navbar__logout-btn {
    width: 100%;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 0.55rem;
    padding: 0.8rem 1rem;
    border: none;
    background: transparent;
    color: #ef4444;
    font-size: 0.82rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: background 0.15s ease;
    text-align: left;

    svg {
      width: 15px;
      height: 15px;
      flex-shrink: 0;
    }

    &:hover {
      background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.06);
    }
  }
}

@keyframes top-navbar-menu-enter {
  from {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(-8px) scale(0.96);
  }
  to {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: translateY(0) scale(1);
  }
}

@keyframes top-navbar-pulse {
  0% {
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 0 rgba(134, 239, 172, 0.5);
  }
  70% {
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 8px rgba(134, 239, 172, 0);
  }
  100% {
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 0 rgba(134, 239, 172, 0);
  }
}

// ── Mat-badge override ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
:host-context([data-shell='legacy']) ::ng-deep .mat-badge-content {
  font-size: 9px;
  font-weight: 800;
  min-width: 17px;
  height: 17px;
  line-height: 17px;
  border-radius: 6px;
  letter-spacing: 0.3px;
}

// ── Responsive ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
@media (max-width: 900px) {
  :host-context([data-shell='legacy']) .top-navbar {
    &__title      { display: none; }
    &__user       { gap: 10px; }
    &__user-info  { display: none; }
    &__menu-trigger { padding-left: 6px; }
  }

  :host-context([data-shell='legacy']) .top-navbar__menu-popup {
    min-width: 17.5rem;
    right: -0.25rem;
  }

}
```

## [A] src/app/layout/header/_top-navbar-mono.scss

```scss
// The redesigned top bar ("Monolith"): flat, hairline, monochrome. Scoped to `data-shell='mono'`, which
// WwellThemeService keeps on <html>. Colours come from layout/_shell-theme.scss, so it follows
// light/dark; the user dropdown borrows the planning `--wp-*` surface tokens, which flip with the theme.

$font: 'Geist Variable', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, 'Helvetica Neue', Arial, sans-serif;
$mono: 'Geist Mono Variable', ui-monospace, 'SFMono-Regular', Menlo, Consolas, monospace;
$ease: cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.9, 0.25, 1);
$radius: 4px;

:host-context([data-shell='mono']) {
  // ── Shell ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  .top-navbar {
    position: relative;
    z-index: 200;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    box-sizing: border-box; // the hairline sits inside --shell-bar-h, so the bar is exactly that tall
    height: var(--shell-bar-h);
    padding: 0 14px 0 0;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--shell-hair);
    background: var(--shell-bar);
    color: var(--shell-text);
    font-family: $font;

    // Buttons don't inherit the family by default, and the user trigger and logout button hold text.
    button {
      font-family: inherit;
    }

    button:focus-visible {
      outline: 2px solid var(--shell-text);
      outline-offset: 2px;
    }
  }

  // ── Brand: its width matches the rail below, so the hairline runs straight down ──
  .top-navbar__brand {
    display: flex;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 8px;
    width: 244px;
    height: 100%;
    padding: 0 12px 0 10px;
    border-right: 1px solid var(--shell-hair);
  }

  .top-navbar__hamburger {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 4px;
    width: 36px;
    height: 36px;
    padding: 0 8px;
    border: 0;
    border-radius: $radius;
    background: transparent;
    color: var(--shell-muted);
    cursor: pointer;
    transition:
      background-color 0.2s,
      color 0.2s;

    span {
      display: block;
      height: 2px;
      border-radius: 2px;
      background: currentColor;

      &:nth-child(1) { width: 20px; }
      &:nth-child(2) { width: 14px; }
      &:nth-child(3) { width: 17px; }
    }

    &:hover {
      background: var(--shell-hover);
      color: var(--shell-text);
    }
  }

  .top-navbar__logo {
    display: flex;
    flex: 1;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 8px;
  }

  .top-navbar__logo-icon {
    width: 26px;
    height: 26px;
    flex-shrink: 0;
  }

  .top-navbar__logo-text {
    font: 700 16px/1 $font;
    letter-spacing: 0.3em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--shell-text);
  }

  // ── Title ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  .top-navbar__title {
    display: flex;
    flex: 1;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: flex-start;
    min-width: 0;
    padding: 0 24px;

    h1 {
      margin: 0;
      overflow: hidden;
      font: 500 12px/1 $mono;
      letter-spacing: 0.14em;
      text-overflow: ellipsis;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      white-space: nowrap;
      color: var(--shell-muted);
    }
  }

  // ── Right cluster ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  .top-navbar__user {
    display: flex;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 10px;
    margin-left: auto;
  }

  .top-navbar__notifications {
    position: relative;
  }

  .top-navbar__icon-btn {
    position: relative;
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    width: 34px;
    height: 34px;
    padding: 0;
    border: 0;
    border-radius: $radius;
    background: transparent;
    color: var(--shell-muted);
    cursor: pointer;
    transition:
      background-color 0.2s,
      color 0.2s,
      transform 0.12s;

    svg {
      width: 18px;
      height: 18px;
    }

    &:hover {
      background: var(--shell-press);
      color: var(--shell-text);
    }

    &:active {
      transform: scale(0.95);
    }
  }

  .top-navbar__divider {
    width: 1px;
    height: 26px;
    background: var(--shell-hair);
  }

  // ── User trigger ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  .top-navbar__menu {
    position: relative;
  }

  .top-navbar__menu-trigger {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 10px;
    padding: 3px 8px 3px 14px;
    border: 1px solid var(--shell-hair);
    border-radius: $radius;
    background: transparent;
    color: var(--shell-text);
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: border-color 0.2s;

    &:hover,
    &--open {
      border-color: var(--shell-hair-strong);
    }

    &--open .top-navbar__chevron {
      transform: rotate(180deg);
    }
  }

  .top-navbar__user-info {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: flex-end;
    line-height: 1.25;
  }

  .top-navbar__user-name {
    font-size: 13px;
    font-weight: 600;
    color: var(--shell-text);
  }

  .top-navbar__last-login {
    font: 500 11px/1.25 $mono;
    color: var(--shell-muted);
  }

  .top-navbar__avatar {
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    width: 30px;
    height: 30px;
    border-radius: $radius;
    background: var(--shell-avatar-bg);
    color: var(--shell-avatar-text);
    font-size: 12px;
    font-weight: 800;
  }

  .top-navbar__chevron {
    flex-shrink: 0;
    width: 15px;
    height: 15px;
    color: var(--shell-muted);
    transition: transform 0.25s $ease;
  }

  // ── Dropdown: a raised planning surface, so it flips with the theme ─────────
  .top-navbar__menu-popup {
    position: absolute;
    top: calc(100% + 8px);
    right: 0;
    z-index: 300;
    width: 17rem;
    overflow: hidden;
    border: 1px solid var(--wp-line-strong);
    border-radius: 8px;
    background: var(--wp-surface-raised);
    box-shadow: var(--wp-shadow-3);
    color: var(--wp-text);
    transform-origin: top right;
    animation: top-navbar-menu-enter 180ms $ease;
  }

  .top-navbar__profile-card {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
  }

  .top-navbar__profile-hero {
    display: flex;
    align-items: flex-start;
    gap: 0.75rem;
    padding: 1rem;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--wp-line);
  }

  .top-navbar__profile-avatar {
    display: grid;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    place-items: center;
    width: 2.4rem;
    height: 2.4rem;
    border-radius: $radius;
    background: var(--shell-avatar-bg);
    color: var(--shell-avatar-text);
    font-size: 0.95rem;
    font-weight: 800;
  }

  .top-navbar__profile-details {
    display: flex;
    flex: 1;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 0.3rem;
    min-width: 0;
  }

  .top-navbar__profile-name {
    overflow: hidden;
    font-size: 0.9rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    line-height: 1.3;
    text-overflow: ellipsis;
    white-space: nowrap;
    color: var(--wp-ink);
  }

  .top-navbar__profile-subtitle {
    display: flex;
    align-items: flex-start;
    gap: 0.35rem;
    font: 500 11px/1.45 $mono;
    color: var(--wp-muted);

    svg {
      flex-shrink: 0;
      width: 11px;
      height: 11px;
      margin-top: 2px;
      color: var(--wp-subtle);
    }
  }

  .top-navbar__profile-body {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 0.65rem;
    padding: 0.85rem 1rem;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--wp-line);
  }

  .top-navbar__profile-roles {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 5px;
  }

  .top-navbar__profile-role-chip {
    padding: 2px 7px;
    border: 1px solid var(--wp-line-strong);
    border-radius: $radius;
    background: var(--wp-surface-sunken);
    font: 600 11px/1.6 $mono;
    letter-spacing: 0.04em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    white-space: nowrap;
    color: var(--wp-muted);
  }

  .top-navbar__logout-btn {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 0.55rem;
    width: 100%;
    padding: 0.8rem 1rem;
    border: 0;
    background: transparent;
    color: var(--wp-danger-ink);
    font-size: 0.82rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    text-align: left;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: background-color 0.15s;

    svg {
      flex-shrink: 0;
      width: 15px;
      height: 15px;
    }

    &:hover {
      background: var(--wp-danger-soft);
    }
  }

  // ── Material badge on the bell: the one place red shows up ──────────────────
  ::ng-deep .mat-badge-content {
    min-width: 18px;
    height: 18px;
    padding: 0 4px;
    border-radius: 6px;
    background: var(--shell-signal);
    color: var(--shell-on-signal);
    font-family: $font;
    font-size: 11px;
    font-weight: 800;
    letter-spacing: 0;
    line-height: 18px;
  }
}

// ── Responsive ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
@media (max-width: 900px) {
  :host-context([data-shell='mono']) {
    .top-navbar__title,
    .top-navbar__user-info {
      display: none;
    }

    .top-navbar__menu-trigger {
      padding-left: 6px;
    }

    .top-navbar__menu-popup {
      right: -0.25rem;
      min-width: 17.5rem;
    }
  }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  :host-context([data-shell='mono']) {
    .top-navbar__menu-popup {
      animation: none;
    }

    .top-navbar__hamburger,
    .top-navbar__icon-btn,
    .top-navbar__menu-trigger,
    .top-navbar__chevron {
      transition: none;
    }
  }
}
```

## [M] src/app/layout/header/top-navbar.component.html

```html
<header class="top-navbar">

  <!-- Left: hamburger + logo -->
  <div class="top-navbar__brand">
    <button
      class="top-navbar__hamburger"
      (click)="toggleSidenav.emit()"
      [attr.aria-label]="sidenavCollapsed() ? 'Expand sidebar' : 'Collapse sidebar'"
      [class.top-navbar__hamburger--active]="!sidenavCollapsed()"
    >
      <span></span><span></span><span></span>
    </button>

    <div class="top-navbar__logo">
      <div class="top-navbar__logo-icon">
        <svg viewBox="0 0 32 32" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
          <ellipse cx="16" cy="20" rx="9" ry="11" fill="url(#dropGrad)"/>
          <ellipse cx="16" cy="20" rx="5"  ry="7"  fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.25)"/>
          <path d="M16 4 C16 4 22 12 22 18" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.6)" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round"/>
          <defs>
            <linearGradient id="dropGrad" x1="16" y1="9" x2="16" y2="31" gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
              <stop offset="0%"   stop-color="#38bdf8"/>
              <stop offset="100%" stop-color="#0369a1"/>
            </linearGradient>
          </defs>
        </svg>
      </div>
      <span class="top-navbar__logo-text">{{ logoText }}</span>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Center: title -->
  <div class="top-navbar__title">
    <h1>{{ appTitle }}</h1>
  </div>

  <!-- Right: notifications + user -->
  <div class="top-navbar__user">

    <div class="top-navbar__notifications">
      <button
        class="top-navbar__icon-btn"
        aria-label="Notifications"
        [matBadge]="3"
        matBadgeColor="warn"
        [matBadgeOverlap]="true"
      >
        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2">
          <path d="M18 8A6 6 0 006 8c0 7-3 9-3 9h18s-3-2-3-9"/>
          <path d="M13.73 21a2 2 0 01-3.46 0"/>
        </svg>
      </button>
    </div>

    <app-wwell-theme-toggle />

    <div class="top-navbar__divider"></div>

    <div class="top-navbar__menu">
      <button
        type="button"
        class="top-navbar__menu-trigger"
        (click)="toggleUserMenu($event)"
        [class.top-navbar__menu-trigger--open]="menuOpen()"
        aria-haspopup="menu"
        [attr.aria-expanded]="menuOpen()"
        aria-label="Open user menu"
      >
        <div class="top-navbar__user-info">
          <span class="top-navbar__user-name">{{ auth.displayUsername() }}</span>
          @if (lastLoginDisplay()) {
            <span class="top-navbar__last-login">{{ lastLoginDisplay() }}</span>
          }
        </div>

        <div class="top-navbar__avatar" aria-hidden="true">{{ userInitial() }}</div>

        <svg class="top-navbar__chevron" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2">
          <path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6" />
        </svg>
      </button>

      @if (menuOpen()) {
        <div
          class="top-navbar__menu-popup"
          role="menu"
          tabindex="0"
          (click)="keepMenuOpen($event)"
          (keydown)="keepMenuOpen($event)"
        >
          <div class="top-navbar__profile-card">

            <div class="top-navbar__profile-hero">
              <div class="top-navbar__profile-avatar" aria-hidden="true">{{ userInitial() }}</div>
              <div class="top-navbar__profile-details">
                <strong class="top-navbar__profile-name">{{ auth.displayUsername() }}</strong>
                @if (lastLoginDisplay()) {
                  <span class="top-navbar__profile-subtitle">
                    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" aria-hidden="true">
                      <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><polyline points="12 6 12 12 16 14"/>
                    </svg>
                    {{ lastLoginDisplay() }}
                  </span>
                }
              </div>
            </div>

            @if (userRoleLabels().length) {
              <div class="top-navbar__profile-body">
                <div class="top-navbar__profile-roles">
                  @for (label of userRoleLabels(); track label) {
                    <span class="top-navbar__profile-role-chip">{{ label }}</span>
                  }
                </div>
              </div>
            }

            <button
              type="button"
              class="top-navbar__logout-btn"
              (click)="logout($event)"
            >
              <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" aria-hidden="true">
                <path d="M9 21H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V5a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h4"/>
                <polyline points="16 17 21 12 16 7"/>
                <line x1="21" y1="12" x2="9" y2="12"/>
              </svg>
              Sign out
            </button>

          </div>
        </div>
      }
    </div>
  </div>

</header>
```

```scss
// Two shells share this component's markup. The redesigned one is the default; the navy one is opt-in
// via the Appearance popover. Each partial is scoped to its own `data-shell` value on <html>
// (written by WwellThemeService), so neither can leak into the other.
@use 'top-navbar-mono';
@use 'top-navbar-legacy';

:host {
  display: block;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}
```

## [M] src/app/layout/header/top-navbar.component.spec.ts

```ts
import { ComponentFixture, TestBed } from '@angular/core/testing';
import { NoopAnimationsModule } from '@angular/platform-browser/animations';

import { TopNavbarComponent } from './top-navbar.component';
import { AuthStore } from 'src/app/features/auth/store/auth.store';
import { RbacStore } from '@store/rbac/rbac.store';

describe('TopNavbarComponent', () => {
  let component: TopNavbarComponent;
  let fixture: ComponentFixture<TopNavbarComponent>;
  const authStoreMock = {
    user: jest.fn(() => ({ groups: ['GWD_admin'] })),
    userEmail: jest.fn(() => 'jane.doe@aramco.com'),
    lastLogin: jest.fn(() => null),
    displayUsername: jest.fn(() => 'jane.doe'),
    logout: jest.fn(),
  };
  const rbacStoreMock = {
    roleDefinitions: jest.fn(() => [
      { name: 'GWD_admin', label: 'Admin', routes: [], canUpdate: [] },
    ]),
    isLoaded: jest.fn(() => true),
    userPermissions: jest.fn(() => ({ home: ['read'] })),
  };
  beforeEach(async () => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();

    await TestBed.configureTestingModule({
      imports: [TopNavbarComponent, NoopAnimationsModule],
      providers: [
        { provide: AuthStore, useValue: authStoreMock },
        { provide: RbacStore, useValue: rbacStoreMock },
      ],
    })
    .compileComponents();

    fixture = TestBed.createComponent(TopNavbarComponent);
    component = fixture.componentInstance;
    fixture.detectChanges();
  });

  it('should create', () => {
    expect(component).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('should render the logged in username', () => {
    const username = fixture.nativeElement.querySelector('.top-navbar__user-name');
    expect(username?.textContent?.trim()).toBe('jane.doe');
  });

  it('should call logout from the dropdown menu', () => {
    const trigger: HTMLButtonElement = fixture.nativeElement.querySelector('.top-navbar__menu-trigger');
    trigger.click();
    fixture.detectChanges();

    const logoutButton: HTMLButtonElement | null = fixture.nativeElement.querySelector('.top-navbar__logout-btn');
    expect(logoutButton).toBeTruthy();
    logoutButton?.click();

    expect(authStoreMock.logout).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('should host the theme + appearance control between the bell and the user menu', () => {
    const bar: HTMLElement = fixture.nativeElement.querySelector('.top-navbar__user');
    const children = Array.from(bar.children).map((el) => el.tagName.toLowerCase() === 'app-wwell-theme-toggle'
      ? 'toggle'
      : el.className);

    expect(children).toEqual([
      'top-navbar__notifications',
      'toggle',
      'top-navbar__divider',
      'top-navbar__menu',
    ]);
  });
});
```

## [M] src/app/layout/header/top-navbar.component.ts

```ts
import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  computed,
  HostListener,
  inject,
  input,
  output,
  signal,
} from '@angular/core';
import { MatBadgeModule } from '@angular/material/badge';
import { AuthStore } from 'src/app/features/auth/store/auth.store';
import { WwellThemeToggleComponent } from 'src/app/features/wwell-planning/shared/wwell-theme-toggle/wwell-theme-toggle.component';
import { RbacStore } from '@store/rbac/rbac.store';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-top-navbar',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [MatBadgeModule, WwellThemeToggleComponent],
  templateUrl: './top-navbar.component.html',
  styleUrl: './top-navbar.component.scss',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class TopNavbarComponent {
  readonly sidenavCollapsed = input<boolean>(false);
  readonly toggleSidenav = output<void>();

  protected readonly auth     = inject(AuthStore);
  protected readonly rbac     = inject(RbacStore);
  protected readonly menuOpen = signal(false);

  protected readonly userRoleLabels = computed(() => {
    // After permissions load: if the backend granted nothing, the JWT groups are stale/revoked.
    if (this.rbac.isLoaded() && Object.keys(this.rbac.userPermissions()).length === 0) {
      return [];
    }
    return (this.auth.user()?.groups as string[] | undefined) ?? [];
  });

  protected readonly lastLoginDisplay = computed(() => {
    const d = this.auth.lastLogin();
    if (!d) return null;
    return d.toLocaleString('en-US', {
      timeZone: 'Asia/Riyadh',
      month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric',
      hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit',
    });
  });

  readonly appTitle = 'Aramco Ground Water Application';
  readonly logoText = 'AGWA';
  protected readonly profileSubtitle = computed(() => this.auth.userEmail() ?? 'Ground Water Team');
  protected readonly roleCountLabel = computed(() => {
    const count = this.userRoleLabels().length;
    if (count === 0) return 'No roles assigned';
    return count === 1 ? '1 role assigned' : `${count} roles assigned`;
  });

  @HostListener('document:click')
  protected closeUserMenu(): void {
    if (this.menuOpen()) {
      this.menuOpen.set(false);
    }
  }

  @HostListener('document:keydown.escape')
  protected closeUserMenuOnEscape(): void {
    this.menuOpen.set(false);
  }

  protected toggleUserMenu(event: MouseEvent): void {
    event.stopPropagation();
    this.menuOpen.update((open) => !open);
  }

  protected keepMenuOpen(event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation();
  }

  protected logout(event?: MouseEvent): void {
    event?.stopPropagation();
    this.menuOpen.set(false);
    this.auth.logout();
  }

  protected userInitial(): string {
    return this.auth.displayUsername().charAt(0).toUpperCase() || 'U';
  }
}
```

## [M] src/app/layout/layout.component.scss

```scss
// The bar's height is a shell token (56px mono / 62px legacy, see _shell-theme.scss).
$topbar-h: var(--shell-bar-h, 62px);

:host {
  display: block;
  height: 100vh;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.dashboard {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  height: 100vh;
  overflow: hidden;
  background: var(--bg-app);         // ← was hardcoded #0d2a47

  &__topbar {
    flex-shrink: 0;
    height: $topbar-h;
  }

  &__body {
    flex: 1;
    display: flex;
    overflow: hidden;
  }

  &__sidenav {
    flex-shrink: 0;
    height: 100%;
    transition: width 0.28s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);
  }

  &__content {
    flex: 1;
    overflow-y: auto;
    background: var(--bg-content);   // ← was hardcoded #0f3460
    position: relative;
    min-width: 0;
    min-height: 0;

    &::before {
      content: '';
      position: absolute;
      top: -60px; left: -60px;
      width: 400px; height: 400px;
      background: radial-gradient(circle, var(--accent-muted) 0%, transparent 70%);
      pointer-events: none;
    }

    &--scroll-locked {
      overflow: hidden;
    }
  }
}

// The sky-blue corner glow belongs to the legacy shell; the mono shell is colourless.
:host-context([data-shell='mono']) .dashboard__content::before {
  display: none;
}
```

## [A] src/app/layout/sidebar/_left-sidenav-legacy.scss

```scss
// ── Layout constants ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
$expanded-w:  244px;
$collapsed-w: 64px;
$ease:        cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);
$transition:  0.26s $ease;

// Light-on-dark palette — sidenav is a dark navy surface
$nav-text:    rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.88);
$nav-muted:   rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.48);
$nav-border:  rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
$nav-hover-bg: rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.10);

// The navy left nav this app shipped with, kept as it was and scoped to `data-shell='legacy'` (set on
// <html> by WwellThemeService). The redesigned nav lives in _left-sidenav-mono.scss. `:host` is in the
// component stylesheet, since both shells share it.

// ── Shell ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
:host-context([data-shell='legacy']) .sidenav {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  width: $expanded-w;
  height: 100%;
  background: var(--bg-sidenav);
  border-right: 1px solid $nav-border;
  overflow: visible;
  transition: width $transition;
  position: relative;
  z-index: 30;

  // Subtle right-edge glow
  &::after {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    top: 0; right: 0;
    width: 1px; height: 100%;
    background: linear-gradient(
      to bottom,
      rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.35),
      rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.08) 40%,
      transparent 70%
    );
    pointer-events: none;
  }

  // ── Collapsed state ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  &--collapsed {
    width: $collapsed-w;

    .sidenav__label,
    .sidenav__footer-text {
      opacity: 0;
      width: 0;
      overflow: hidden;
    }

    .sidenav__btn {
      justify-content: center;
      padding: 0;
    }

    .sidenav__icon { margin-right: 0; }

    .sidenav__section-label { opacity: 0; height: 0; padding: 0; overflow: hidden; }
  }

  // ── Section label ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  &__section-label {
    padding: 20px 18px 6px;
    font-size: 10px;
    font-weight: 700;
    letter-spacing: 1.8px;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: $nav-muted;
    transition: opacity $transition, height $transition, padding $transition;
    white-space: nowrap;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    overflow: hidden;
  }

  // ── List ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  &__list {
    list-style: none;
    margin: 0;
    padding: 14px 0 18px;
    flex: 1;
    overflow-y: auto;
    overflow-x: hidden;

    &::-webkit-scrollbar { width: 0; }
  }

  &__item {
    padding: 4px 12px;
  }

  // ── Nav button ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  &__btn {
    position: relative;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    width: 100%;
    min-height: 58px;
    padding: 8px 16px 8px 10px;
    border: none;
    border-radius: 14px;
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.02);
    color: $nav-muted;
    cursor: pointer;
    font-family: 'DM Sans', system-ui, sans-serif;
    font-size: 16px;
    font-weight: 700;
    letter-spacing: 0.2px;
    gap: 2px;
    transition:
      background $transition,
      color $transition,
      box-shadow $transition,
      transform 0.15s ease;
    overflow: hidden;
    white-space: nowrap;

    &:hover {
      background: rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.14);
      color: $nav-text;
      transform: translateX(2px);
      box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.14);

      .sidenav__icon {
        background: rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.16);

        svg {
          stroke: #38bdf8;
          filter: drop-shadow(0 0 5px rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.4));
        }

        .sidenav__icon-img {
          background-color: #38bdf8;
          filter: drop-shadow(0 0 5px rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.4));
        }
      }
    }

    &--active {
      background: linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.2) 0%, rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.08) 100%);
      color: #dff6ff;
      font-weight: 800;
      box-shadow:
        inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.28),
        0 8px 18px rgba(2, 132, 199, 0.12);

      .sidenav__icon {
        background: rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.22);

        svg {
          stroke: #38bdf8;
          filter: drop-shadow(0 0 8px rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.55));
        }

        .sidenav__icon-img {
          background-color: #38bdf8;
          filter: drop-shadow(0 0 8px rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.55));
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // ── Icon container ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  &__icon {
    width: 40px;
    height: 40px;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    border-radius: 12px;
    margin-right: 13px;
    transition: margin $transition, background $transition;

    svg {
      width: 22px;
      height: 22px;
      stroke: currentColor;
      transition: stroke 0.2s ease, filter 0.2s ease;
    }
  }

  // ── Image-based icon ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  &__icon-img {
    width: 22px;
    height: 22px;
    background-color: currentColor;
    -webkit-mask-size: contain;
    mask-size: contain;
    -webkit-mask-repeat: no-repeat;
    mask-repeat: no-repeat;
    -webkit-mask-position: center;
    mask-position: center;
    transition: background-color 0.2s ease, filter 0.2s ease;
  }

  // ── Label ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  &__label {
    flex: 1;
    font-size: 16px;
    font-weight: 700;
    line-height: 1.2;
    color: inherit;
    transition: opacity $transition, width $transition;
    white-space: normal;
    overflow-wrap: break-word;
  }

  // ── Lock icon ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  &__lock {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    margin-left: auto;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    width: 22px;
    height: 22px;
    border-radius: 6px;
    background: rgba(220, 38, 38, 0.15);
    color: #f87171;
    border: 1px solid rgba(220, 38, 38, 0.28);
    transition: background 0.2s ease, color 0.2s ease, box-shadow 0.2s ease;

    svg {
      width: 13px;
      height: 13px;
      stroke: currentColor;
      stroke-width: 2.2;
      flex-shrink: 0;
    }
  }

  &__btn--locked {
    opacity: 0.72;

    &:hover {
      opacity: 1;

      .sidenav__lock {
        background: rgba(220, 38, 38, 0.26);
        color: #fca5a5;
        box-shadow:
          0 0 0 1px rgba(220, 38, 38, 0.4),
          0 0 10px rgba(220, 38, 38, 0.35);
      }
    }
  }

  // ── Active indicator (left edge) ────────────────────────────────────────
  &__active-bar {
    position: absolute;
    left: 0;
    top: 9px;
    bottom: 9px;
    width: 4px;
    background: #38bdf8;
    border-radius: 0 4px 4px 0;
    box-shadow: 2px 0 14px rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.55);
  }

  // ── Footer ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  &__footer {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 14px;
    padding: 16px 18px 18px;
    border-top: 1px solid $nav-border;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    background: rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.03);
    overflow: visible;
  }

  &__brand {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 12px;
    position: relative;
    overflow: visible;
  }

  &__water-logo {
    position: relative;
    width: 28px;
    height: 34px;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    filter: drop-shadow(0 0 10px rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.6));
    outline: none;
    z-index: 60;

    svg { width: 100%; height: 100%; }

    &:hover,
    &:focus-visible {
      .sidenav__build-popover {
        opacity: 1;
        transform: translate(0, 0) scale(1);
        pointer-events: auto;
      }
    }
  }

  &__footer-text {
    font-size: 12.5px;
    font-weight: 800;
    color: $nav-muted;
    letter-spacing: 1.2px;
    white-space: nowrap;
    transition: opacity $transition, width $transition;
    text-transform: uppercase;
  }

  // ── Build info (terminal-style) ─────────────────────────────────────────
  &__build-card {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 5px;
    min-width: 0;
    padding: 10px 13px;
    border-radius: 10px;
    background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.16);
    box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
    font-family: 'SFMono-Regular', Consolas, Menlo, monospace;
    cursor: default;
  }

  &__build-line {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 6px;
    min-width: 0;
  }

  &__build-icon {
    width: 13px;
    height: 13px;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    stroke: #38bdf8;
    opacity: 0.75;
  }

  &__build-ref {
    min-width: 0;
    overflow: hidden;
    text-overflow: ellipsis;
    white-space: nowrap;
    font-size: 11.5px;
    letter-spacing: 0.2px;
  }

  &__build-branch {
    font-weight: 700;
    color: $nav-text;
  }

  &__build-at {
    color: $nav-muted;
    opacity: 0.5;
    margin: 0 0.5px;
  }

  &__build-hash {
    color: #38bdf8;
    opacity: 0.85;
  }

  &__build-status {
    width: 6px;
    height: 6px;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    margin-left: auto;
    border-radius: 50%;
    background: #38bdf8;
    box-shadow: 0 0 6px rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.7);

    &--prod {
      background: #fb923c;
      box-shadow: 0 0 6px rgba(251, 146, 60, 0.7);
    }
  }

  &__build-sub {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 6px;
    padding-left: 19px;
    font-size: 10px;
    font-style: italic;
    color: $nav-muted;
    letter-spacing: 0.2px;
    white-space: nowrap;
    overflow: hidden;
  }

  &__build-version {
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    color: #dff6ff;
  }

  &__build-dash {
    opacity: 0.4;
    font-style: normal;
  }

  &__build-env {
    font-weight: 700;
    font-style: normal;
    letter-spacing: 0.5px;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.75);

    &--prod {
      color: rgba(251, 146, 60, 0.85);
    }
  }

  &__build-popover {
    position: absolute;
    left: calc(100% + 16px);
    bottom: -2px;
    z-index: 50;
    width: 190px;
    padding: 9px 10px;
    border: 1px solid rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.22);
    border-radius: 11px;
    background:
      linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.98), rgba(2, 6, 23, 0.98)),
      radial-gradient(circle at top left, rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.16), transparent 42%);
    box-shadow:
      0 18px 38px rgba(2, 6, 23, 0.34),
      inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
    color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.92);
    filter: none;
    opacity: 0;
    pointer-events: none;
    transform: translate(-6px, 0) scale(0.97);
    transition: opacity 0.16s ease, transform 0.16s ease;

    &::before {
      content: '';
      position: absolute;
      left: -7px;
      bottom: 12px;
      width: 10px;
      height: 10px;
      background: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.98);
      border-left: 1px solid rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.22);
      border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.22);
      transform: rotate(45deg);
    }
  }

  &__build-popover-title {
    margin-bottom: 7px;
    padding-bottom: 6px;
    border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.18);
    color: #7dd3fc;
    font-family: 'DM Sans', system-ui, sans-serif;
    font-size: 10px;
    font-weight: 900;
    letter-spacing: 0.12em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
  }

  &__build-popover-row {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 52px minmax(0, 1fr);
    gap: 8px;
    align-items: baseline;
    min-width: 0;
    padding: 2px 0;
    font-family: 'SFMono-Regular', Consolas, Menlo, monospace;

    span {
      color: rgba(203, 213, 225, 0.62);
      font-size: 9px;
      font-weight: 700;
      letter-spacing: 0.08em;
      text-transform: uppercase;
    }

    strong {
      min-width: 0;
      overflow: hidden;
      color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.94);
      font-size: 10.5px;
      font-weight: 800;
      line-height: 1.35;
      text-overflow: ellipsis;
      white-space: nowrap;
    }
  }
}
```

## [A] src/app/layout/sidebar/_left-sidenav-mono.scss

```scss
// The redesigned left nav ("Monolith"): flat, hairline, monochrome. Scoped to `data-shell='mono'`, which
// WwellThemeService keeps on <html>. Colours come from layout/_shell-theme.scss, so it follows
// light/dark; the build-info popover borrows the planning `--wp-*` surface tokens.
//
// The current page is an inverted block. `.sidenav__hl` is that block as its own element, so it can
// glide between items (transform only). LeftSidenavComponent measures the active button into
// --hl-x / --hl-y / --hl-w / --hl-h and flips `.sidenav--glide` once it has; until then the active
// button paints the same fill itself, so the current page is never left without a highlight.

$font: 'Geist Variable', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, 'Helvetica Neue', Arial, sans-serif;
$mono: 'Geist Mono Variable', ui-monospace, 'SFMono-Regular', Menlo, Consolas, monospace;
$ease: cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.9, 0.25, 1);
$slide: 0.26s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);
$expanded-w: 244px;
$collapsed-w: 64px;
$radius: 4px;

:host-context([data-shell='mono']) {
  // ── Shell ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  .sidenav {
    position: relative;
    z-index: 30;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    width: $expanded-w;
    height: 100%;
    overflow: visible;
    border-right: 1px solid var(--shell-hair);
    background: var(--shell-rail);
    font-family: $font;
    transition: width $slide;

    button:focus-visible {
      outline: 2px solid var(--shell-text);
      outline-offset: 2px;
    }

    &--collapsed {
      width: $collapsed-w;
    }
  }

  // ── List ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  .sidenav__list {
    position: relative;
    flex: 1;
    margin: 0;
    padding: 10px 0 12px;
    overflow-x: hidden;
    overflow-y: auto;
    list-style: none;

    &::-webkit-scrollbar {
      width: 0;
    }
  }

  // The gliding highlight. No transition until `--glide`, so its first placement doesn't slide in from
  // the corner; width is driven per frame by LeftSidenavComponent's ResizeObserver, so it isn't animated.
  .sidenav__hl {
    position: absolute;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    z-index: 0;
    display: block;
    width: var(--hl-w, 0);
    height: var(--hl-h, 0);
    border-radius: $radius;
    background: var(--shell-active-bg);
    opacity: 0;
    pointer-events: none;
    transform: translate(var(--hl-x, 0), var(--hl-y, 0));
    will-change: transform;
  }

  .sidenav--glide .sidenav__hl {
    opacity: 1;
    transition:
      transform 0.34s $ease,
      opacity 0.2s ease;
  }

  .sidenav__section-label {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 10px;
    padding: 20px 20px 8px;
    overflow: hidden;
    font: 600 11px/1 $mono;
    letter-spacing: 0.12em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    white-space: nowrap;
    color: var(--shell-label);
    transition:
      opacity $slide,
      height $slide,
      padding $slide;

    // A hairline runs out to the edge: the label reads as a section break, not a heading.
    &::after {
      content: '';
      flex: 1;
      height: 1px;
      background: var(--shell-hair);
    }
  }

  .sidenav__item {
    padding: 2px 10px;
  }

  // ── Nav button ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  .sidenav__btn {
    position: relative;
    z-index: 1;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 12px;
    width: 100%;
    min-height: 40px;
    padding: 0 10px;
    overflow: hidden;
    border: 0;
    border-radius: $radius;
    background: transparent;
    color: var(--shell-muted);
    font: 500 14px/1.18 $font;
    text-align: left;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition:
      background-color 0.2s,
      color 0.2s;

    &:hover {
      background: var(--shell-hover);
      color: var(--shell-text);
    }

    &--active,
    &--active:hover {
      background: var(--shell-active-bg);
      color: var(--shell-active-text);
      font-weight: 600;
    }

    &--locked {
      opacity: 0.8;

      &:hover {
        opacity: 1;
      }
    }
  }

  // Once the highlight element is in place it carries the fill, so the button steps aside.
  .sidenav--glide .sidenav__btn--active,
  .sidenav--glide .sidenav__btn--active:hover {
    background: transparent;
  }

  .sidenav__icon {
    display: grid;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    place-items: center;
    width: 22px;
    height: 22px;

    svg {
      width: 20px;
      height: 20px;
      stroke: currentColor;
      stroke-width: 1.6;
    }
  }

  // Image icon (rig): the PNG is a mask, so it takes the text colour like the SVG icons do.
  .sidenav__icon-img {
    width: 20px;
    height: 20px;
    background-color: currentColor;
    -webkit-mask-size: contain;
    mask-size: contain;
    -webkit-mask-repeat: no-repeat;
    mask-repeat: no-repeat;
    -webkit-mask-position: center;
    mask-position: center;
  }

  .sidenav__label {
    flex: 1;
    min-width: 0;
    overflow-wrap: break-word;
    white-space: normal;
  }

  .sidenav__lock {
    display: grid;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    place-items: center;
    width: 22px;
    height: 22px;
    margin-left: auto;
    border: 1px solid var(--shell-lock-bd);
    border-radius: 6px;
    background: var(--shell-lock-bg);
    color: var(--shell-lock-fg);

    svg {
      width: 13px;
      height: 13px;
      stroke: currentColor;
      stroke-width: 2.2;
    }
  }

  // The legacy left-edge bar; the inverted block replaces it.
  .sidenav__active-bar {
    display: none;
  }

  // ── Collapsed ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  .sidenav--collapsed {
    .sidenav__section-label {
      height: 0;
      padding: 0;
      opacity: 0;
    }

    .sidenav__btn {
      justify-content: center;
      padding: 0;
    }

    .sidenav__footer {
      padding-right: 0;
      padding-left: 0;
    }

    .sidenav__brand {
      justify-content: center;
    }
  }

  // ── Footer ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  .sidenav__footer {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    gap: 14px;
    padding: 12px 16px;
    overflow: visible;
    border-top: 1px solid var(--shell-hair);
  }

  .sidenav__brand {
    position: relative;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 10px;
    overflow: visible;
  }

  .sidenav__water-logo {
    position: relative;
    z-index: 60;
    display: flex;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    width: 22px;
    height: 22px;
    outline: none;

    img {
      width: 22px;
      height: 22px;
      object-fit: contain;
    }

    &:hover,
    &:focus-visible {
      .sidenav__build-popover {
        opacity: 1;
        pointer-events: auto;
        transform: translate(0, 0) scale(1);
      }
    }
  }

  .sidenav__footer-text {
    font-size: 12px;
    font-weight: 800;
    letter-spacing: 0.14em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    white-space: nowrap;
    color: var(--shell-muted);
  }

  .sidenav__build-version {
    margin-left: auto;
    font: 500 11px/1 $mono;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    color: var(--shell-muted);
  }

  // ── Build info, shown on hover while the rail is collapsed ──────────────────
  .sidenav__build-popover {
    position: absolute;
    bottom: -2px;
    left: calc(100% + 16px);
    z-index: 50;
    width: 190px;
    padding: 9px 10px;
    border: 1px solid var(--wp-line-strong);
    border-radius: 8px;
    background: var(--wp-surface-raised);
    box-shadow: var(--wp-shadow-3);
    color: var(--wp-text);
    opacity: 0;
    pointer-events: none;
    transform: translate(-6px, 0) scale(0.97);
    transition:
      opacity 0.16s ease,
      transform 0.16s ease;

    &::before {
      content: '';
      position: absolute;
      bottom: 12px;
      left: -6px;
      width: 10px;
      height: 10px;
      border-bottom: 1px solid var(--wp-line-strong);
      border-left: 1px solid var(--wp-line-strong);
      background: var(--wp-surface-raised);
      transform: rotate(45deg);
    }
  }

  .sidenav__build-popover-title {
    margin-bottom: 7px;
    padding-bottom: 6px;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--wp-line);
    font: 700 11px/1 $mono;
    letter-spacing: 0.12em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--wp-muted);
  }

  .sidenav__build-popover-row {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 52px minmax(0, 1fr);
    gap: 8px;
    align-items: baseline;
    min-width: 0;
    padding: 2px 0;
    font-family: $mono;

    span {
      font-size: 11px;
      font-weight: 600;
      letter-spacing: 0.06em;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      color: var(--wp-muted);
    }

    strong {
      min-width: 0;
      overflow: hidden;
      font-size: 11px;
      font-weight: 700;
      line-height: 1.35;
      text-overflow: ellipsis;
      white-space: nowrap;
      color: var(--wp-ink);
    }
  }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  :host-context([data-shell='mono']) {
    .sidenav,
    .sidenav__section-label,
    .sidenav__btn,
    .sidenav__build-popover,
    .sidenav--glide .sidenav__hl {
      transition: none;
    }
  }
}
```

## [M] src/app/layout/sidebar/left-sidenav.component.html

```html
<nav
  class="sidenav"
  [class.sidenav--collapsed]="collapsed()"
  [class.sidenav--glide]="glideReady()"
  aria-label="Main navigation"
>
  <!-- Nav Items -->
  <ul class="sidenav__list" role="list" #list>
    <!-- Gliding highlight behind the active item (mono shell only; measured in the component) -->
    <li class="sidenav__hl" aria-hidden="true"></li>
    @for (group of navGroups; track $index) {
      @if (group.section) {
        <li class="sidenav__section-label">{{ group.section }}</li>
      }
      @for (item of group.items; track item.id) {
        <li class="sidenav__item">
          <button
            class="sidenav__btn"
            [class.sidenav__btn--active]="item.id === activeRoute()"
            [class.sidenav__btn--locked]="isLocked(item)"
            (click)="onItemClick(item)"
            [attr.aria-current]="item.id === activeRoute() ? 'page' : null"
            [title]="collapsed() ? item.label : ''"
          >
            <!-- Icon -->
            <span class="sidenav__icon">
              @if (item.iconImage) {
                <span
                  class="sidenav__icon-img"
                  [style.-webkit-mask-image]="'url(' + item.iconImage + ')'"
                  [style.mask-image]="'url(' + item.iconImage + ')'"
                ></span>
              } @else {
                <svg
                  viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                  fill="none"
                  stroke="currentColor"
                  stroke-width="1.8"
                  stroke-linecap="round"
                  stroke-linejoin="round"
                >
                  @for (pathD of item.icon.split(" M "); track $index) {
                    @if ($index === 0) {
                      <path [attr.d]="pathD" />
                    } @else {
                      <path [attr.d]="'M ' + pathD" />
                    }
                  }
                </svg>
              }
            </span>

            <!-- Label (hidden when collapsed) -->
            @if (!collapsed()) {
              <span class="sidenav__label">{{ item.label }}</span>
            }

            <!-- Lock icon for restricted items -->
            @if (isLocked(item) && !collapsed()) {
              <span
                class="sidenav__lock"
                matTooltip="Access restricted — contact your administrator to request access to this module."
                matTooltipPosition="after"
                matTooltipClass="sidenav-lock-tooltip"
                [matTooltipShowDelay]="180"
                aria-label="Access restricted"
              >
                <svg
                  viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                  fill="none"
                  stroke="currentColor"
                  stroke-width="2"
                  stroke-linecap="round"
                  stroke-linejoin="round"
                >
                  <rect x="3" y="11" width="18" height="11" rx="2" ry="2" />
                  <path d="M7 11V7a5 5 0 0110 0v4" />
                </svg>
              </span>
            }

            <!-- Active indicator bar -->
            @if (item.id === activeRoute()) {
              <span class="sidenav__active-bar"></span>
            }
          </button>
        </li>
      }
    }
  </ul>

  <!-- Bottom: Brand + build info -->
  <div class="sidenav__footer">
    <!-- Brand row: water drop + wordmark -->
    <div class="sidenav__brand">
      <div
        class="sidenav__water-logo"
        tabindex="0"
        aria-label="Build information"
      >
        <img class="size-8" src="assets/images/agwa-logo.svg" alt="AGWA" />
        @if (collapsed()) {
          <div class="sidenav__build-popover" role="tooltip">
            <div class="sidenav__build-popover-title">Build Info</div>
            <div class="sidenav__build-popover-row">
              <span>Branch</span>
              <strong>{{ buildInfo.branch }}</strong>
            </div>
            <div class="sidenav__build-popover-row">
              <span>Commit</span>
              <strong>{{ shortHash }}</strong>
            </div>
            <div class="sidenav__build-popover-row">
              <span>Version</span>
              <strong>v{{ buildInfo.version }}</strong>
            </div>
            <div class="sidenav__build-popover-row">
              <span>Env</span>
              <strong>{{ buildInfo.env }}</strong>
            </div>
          </div>
        }
      </div>
      @if (!collapsed()) {
        <span class="sidenav__footer-text">AGWA</span>
        <span class="sidenav__build-version">v{{ buildInfo.version }}</span>
      }
    </div>

    <!-- Build info card -->
    <!-- @if (!collapsed()) {
      <div class="sidenav__build-card">
        <div class="sidenav__build-line">
          <svg
            class="sidenav__build-icon"
            viewBox="0 0 24 24"
            fill="none"
            stroke="currentColor"
            stroke-width="1.8"
            stroke-linecap="round"
            stroke-linejoin="round"
          >
            <line x1="6" y1="3" x2="6" y2="15" />
            <circle cx="18" cy="6" r="2.5" />
            <circle cx="6" cy="18" r="2.5" />
            <path d="M18 8.5a8.5 8.5 0 0 1-8.5 8.5" />
          </svg>
          <span class="sidenav__build-ref" [title]="buildInfo.branch">
            <span class="sidenav__build-branch">{{ buildInfo.branch }}</span
            ><span class="sidenav__build-at">&#64;</span
            ><span class="sidenav__build-hash">{{ shortHash }}</span>
          </span>
          <span
            class="sidenav__build-status"
            [class.sidenav__build-status--prod]="buildInfo.env === 'PROD'"
            [title]="buildInfo.env"
          ></span>
        </div>
        <div class="sidenav__build-sub">
          <span class="sidenav__build-version">v{{ buildInfo.version }}</span>
          <span class="sidenav__build-dash">&mdash;</span>
          <span
            class="sidenav__build-env"
            [class.sidenav__build-env--prod]="buildInfo.env === 'PROD'"
            >{{ buildInfo.env }}</span
          >
        </div>
      </div>
    } -->
  </div>
</nav>
```

## [M] src/app/layout/sidebar/left-sidenav.component.scss

```scss
// Two shells share this component's markup. The redesigned one is the default; the navy one is opt-in
// via the Appearance popover. Each partial is scoped to its own `data-shell` value on <html>
// (written by WwellThemeService), so neither can leak into the other.
@use 'left-sidenav-mono';
@use 'left-sidenav-legacy';

:host {
  display: block;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

// The gliding highlight is a mono-shell element; legacy paints its active item on the button itself.
.sidenav__hl {
  display: none;
}
```

## [M] src/app/layout/sidebar/left-sidenav.component.spec.ts

```ts
import { ComponentFixture, TestBed } from '@angular/core/testing';

import { LeftSidenavComponent } from './left-sidenav.component';
import { AuthStore } from '../../features/auth/store/auth.store';
import { RbacStore } from '@store/rbac/rbac.store';

describe('LeftSidenavComponent', () => {
  let component: LeftSidenavComponent;
  let fixture: ComponentFixture<LeftSidenavComponent>;
  const authStoreMock = {
    user: jest.fn(() => ({ groups: ['GWD_admin'] })),
  };
  const rbacStoreMock = {
    hasPermission: jest.fn(() => true),
  };

  beforeEach(async () => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();

    await TestBed.configureTestingModule({
      imports: [LeftSidenavComponent],
      providers: [
        { provide: AuthStore, useValue: authStoreMock },
        { provide: RbacStore, useValue: rbacStoreMock },
      ],
    })
    .compileComponents();

    fixture = TestBed.createComponent(LeftSidenavComponent);
    component = fixture.componentInstance;
    fixture.detectChanges();
  });

  it('should create', () => {
    expect(component).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('should keep the nav items in their original order', () => {
    const labels = Array.from<HTMLElement>(fixture.nativeElement.querySelectorAll('.sidenav__label'))
      .map((el) => el.textContent?.trim());

    expect(labels).toEqual([
      'Home',
      'Requests',
      'WLDA Approvals',
      'Land Affairs Approvals',
      'Integrated Requirements',
      'Active Water Well',
      'Report',
      'Presentation',
    ]);
  });

  it('should mark only the active route as the current page', () => {
    fixture.componentRef.setInput('activeRoute', 'requests');
    fixture.detectChanges();

    const active: HTMLElement[] = Array.from(fixture.nativeElement.querySelectorAll('.sidenav__btn--active'));

    expect(active).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(active[0].textContent).toContain('Requests');
    expect(active[0].getAttribute('aria-current')).toBe('page');
  });

  it('should render the highlight element the mono shell glides behind the active item', () => {
    expect(fixture.nativeElement.querySelector('.sidenav__hl')).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('should measure the active item into the highlight variables and switch the glide on', () => {
    jest.spyOn(window, 'requestAnimationFrame').mockImplementation((callback) => {
      callback(0);
      return 0;
    });

    fixture.componentRef.setInput('activeRoute', 'requests');
    fixture.detectChanges();

    const list: HTMLElement = fixture.nativeElement.querySelector('.sidenav__list');
    expect(list.style.getPropertyValue('--hl-h')).toBe('0px'); // jsdom has no layout, so every measure is 0
    expect(fixture.nativeElement.querySelector('.sidenav')?.classList).toContain('sidenav--glide');
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    jest.restoreAllMocks();
  });
});
```

## [M] src/app/layout/sidebar/left-sidenav.component.ts

```ts
import {
  afterNextRender,
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  computed,
  DestroyRef,
  effect,
  ElementRef,
  inject,
  input,
  output,
  signal,
  viewChild,
} from '@angular/core';
import { MatTooltipModule } from '@angular/material/tooltip';
import { RbacStore } from '@store/rbac/rbac.store';
import { ACTIONS } from '@models/rbac/role.constants';
import { BuildInfoService } from '@shared/services/build-info.service';
import { NotificationService } from '@shared/components/notification/notification.service';

const ACCESS_RESTRICTED_MESSAGE =
  'Access Restricted — contact your administrator to request access to this module.';

export interface NavItem {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  icon: string;
  iconImage?: string;
  routeId?: string;
  badge?: string | number;
  active?: boolean;
  section?: string;
}

export interface NavGroup {
  section: string | null;
  items: NavItem[];
}

@Component({
  selector: 'app-left-sidenav',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [MatTooltipModule],
  templateUrl: './left-sidenav.component.html',
  styleUrl: './left-sidenav.component.scss',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class LeftSidenavComponent {
  private readonly rbacStore = inject(RbacStore);
  private readonly buildInfoService = inject(BuildInfoService);
  private readonly notificationService = inject(NotificationService);
  private readonly destroyRef = inject(DestroyRef);
  private readonly list = viewChild<ElementRef<HTMLElement>>('list');

  protected readonly buildInfo = this.buildInfoService.info;
  protected readonly shortHash = this.buildInfoService.shortHash;

  readonly collapsed    = input<boolean>(false);
  readonly activeRoute  = input<string>('home');
  readonly navItemClick = output<string>();

  /** True once the mono shell's highlight has been measured; until then the active button paints its own fill. */
  protected readonly glideReady = signal(false);

  constructor() {
    // Re-measure whenever the current page or the rail's width changes.
    effect(() => {
      this.activeRoute();
      this.collapsed();
      requestAnimationFrame(() => this.placeHighlight());
    });

    // The rail animates its width and labels re-wrap as it does, so follow the list's size frame by frame.
    afterNextRender(() => {
      const list = this.list()?.nativeElement;
      if (!list || typeof ResizeObserver === 'undefined') return;
      const observer = new ResizeObserver(() => this.placeHighlight());
      observer.observe(list);
      this.destroyRef.onDestroy(() => observer.disconnect());
    });
  }

  readonly navItems: NavItem[] = [
    {
      id: 'home',
      label: 'Home',
      icon: 'M3 12L12 3l9 9M5 10v10a1 1 0 001 1h4v-5h4v5h4a1 1 0 001-1V10',
      active: true,
    },

    // PLANNING (open to all roles for now — no RBAC gating yet)
    {
      id: 'requests',
      label: 'Requests',
      section: 'PLANNING',
      icon: 'M14 2H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v16a2 2 0 002 2h12a2 2 0 002-2V8z M14 2v6h6 M16 13H8 M16 17H8 M10 9H8',
    },
    {
      id: 'wlda-approvals',
      label: 'WLDA Approvals',
      section: 'PLANNING',
      icon: 'M22 11.08V12a10 10 0 11-5.93-9.14 M22 4L12 14.01l-3-3',
    },
    {
      id: 'land-affairs-approvals',
      label: 'Land Affairs Approvals',
      section: 'PLANNING',
      icon: 'M22 11.08V12a10 10 0 11-5.93-9.14 M22 4L12 14.01l-3-3',
    },
    {
      id: 'integrated-requirements',
      label: 'Integrated Requirements',
      section: 'PLANNING',
      icon: 'M9 2h6a1 1 0 011 1v2H8V3a1 1 0 011-1z M8 4H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v14a2 2 0 002 2h12a2 2 0 002-2V6a2 2 0 00-2-2h-2 M9 12h6 M9 16h6',
    },

    // OPERATIONS
    {
      id: 'active-wwell',
      label: 'Active Water Well',
      section: 'OPERATIONS',
      routeId: 'active-wwell',
      icon: 'M12 2C8.13 2 5 5.13 5 9c0 5.25 7 13 7 13s7-7.75 7-13c0-3.87-3.13-7-7-7zm0 9.5c-1.38 0-2.5-1.12-2.5-2.5s1.12-2.5 2.5-2.5 2.5 1.12 2.5 2.5-1.12 2.5-2.5 2.5z',
      iconImage: 'assets/images/oil-rig-icon.png',
    },
    {
      id: 'morning-report',
      label: 'Report',
      section: 'OPERATIONS',
      routeId: 'morning-report',
      icon: 'M5 4h14a3 3 0 013 3v10a3 3 0 01-3 3H5a3 3 0 01-3-3V7a3 3 0 013-3zM2 7l10 7 10-7',
    },
    {
      id: 'water-wells-overview',
      label: 'Presentation',
      section: 'OPERATIONS',
      routeId: 'presentations',
      icon: 'M9.75 17L9 20l-1 1h8l-1-1-.75-3M3 13h18M5 17h14a2 2 0 002-2V5a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v10a2 2 0 002 2z',
    },
  ];

  protected readonly navGroups: NavGroup[] = this.navItems.reduce<NavGroup[]>((groups, item) => {
    const section = item.section ?? null;
    const last = groups[groups.length - 1];
    return last && last.section === section
      ? [...groups.slice(0, -1), { section, items: [...last.items, item] }]
      : [...groups, { section, items: [item] }];
  }, []);

  protected readonly lockedRouteIds = computed(() =>
    new Set(
      this.navItems
        .filter(item => item.routeId && !this.rbacStore.hasPermission(item.routeId, ACTIONS.READ))
        .map(item => item.routeId!),
    ),
  );

  protected isLocked(item: NavItem): boolean {
    return !!item.routeId && this.lockedRouteIds().has(item.routeId);
  }

  protected onItemClick(item: NavItem): void {
    if (this.isLocked(item)) {
      this.notificationService.error(ACCESS_RESTRICTED_MESSAGE);
      return;
    }
    this.navItemClick.emit(item.id);
  }

  /** Measures the active button into the CSS variables the mono shell's highlight is drawn from. */
  private placeHighlight(): void {
    const list = this.list()?.nativeElement;
    const active = list?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.sidenav__btn--active');
    if (!list || !active) {
      this.glideReady.set(false);
      return;
    }

    list.style.setProperty('--hl-x', `${active.offsetLeft}px`);
    list.style.setProperty('--hl-y', `${active.offsetTop}px`);
    list.style.setProperty('--hl-w', `${active.offsetWidth}px`);
    list.style.setProperty('--hl-h', `${active.offsetHeight}px`);

    // Enabled a frame after the first placement, so the highlight doesn't slide in from the corner.
    if (!this.glideReady()) {
      requestAnimationFrame(() => this.glideReady.set(true));
    }
  }
}
```
