## [M] src/styles.scss

```scss

@use '@angular/material' as mat;
// wwell-planning: self-hosted Geist / Geist Mono (Latin subsets load on demand via unicode-range)
// and the light/dark `--wp-*` token sets. See wwell-theme.service.ts for how the theme is switched.
@use '@fontsource-variable/geist/wght.css' as geist-font;
@use '@fontsource-variable/geist-mono/wght.css' as geist-mono-font;
@use './app/features/wwell-planning/shared/wwell-theme';
@use './app/features/wwell-planning/shared/wwell-design-tokens' as wp;
// Top bar + left nav tokens (light/dark + the legacy switch). See layout/_shell-theme.scss.
@use './app/layout/shell-theme';

@include mat.core();

html, body { height: 100%; overflow: hidden; }
body { margin: 0; font-family: Roboto, "Helvetica Neue", sans-serif; overflow: hidden; }

// ── Design tokens ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
:root {
  // App shell backgrounds
  --bg-app:       #0b1e38;
  --bg-topbar:    #0d2340;
  --bg-sidenav:   #0d2340;
  --bg-content:   #0f3460;
  --bg-surface:   #ffffff;
  --bg-card:      #f8fafc;

  // Borders
  --border:       rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.2);
  --border-sub:   rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.12);

  // Text
  --text-primary:   #0f172a;
  --text-secondary: #475569;
  --text-muted:     #000;

  // Accent
  --accent-muted:   rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.08);

  // Semantic
  --danger: #dc2626;
}


/* 
   Gradient definitions 
  */

svg defs linearGradient#mainGradient stop:nth-child(1) { stop-color: #5d6264; }
svg defs linearGradient#mainGradient stop:nth-child(2) { stop-color: #fff; }
svg defs linearGradient#mainGradient stop:nth-child(3) { stop-color: #5d6264; }

svg defs linearGradient#complCasingGradient stop { stop-color: #4ca746; }

/* 
   Grid pattern (used by the liner‑screen component)
 */
 
svg defs pattern#gridpattern path {
  stroke: #000;
  stroke-width: 2;
  fill: none;
}

.agwa-theme-bg-color {
  background: #5084F0;
background: linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(80, 132, 240, 1) 44%, rgba(121, 207, 241, 1) 86%);
}

/* ── Well bore design preview dialog — container overrides ───────────────────── */
// Portaled to <body>, so it reads the global `--wp-*` tokens and pins its own colour-scheme.
.wwell-ir-design-preview-dialog-panel .mat-mdc-dialog-surface {
  border-radius: var(--wp-radius-lg) !important;
  overflow: hidden !important;
  padding: 0 !important;
  background-color: var(--wp-surface-raised) !important;
  color: var(--wp-text) !important;
  color-scheme: var(--wp-scheme);
  border: 1px solid var(--wp-line) !important;
  box-shadow: var(--wp-shadow-3) !important;
}

/* ── File preview dialog — container overrides ──────────────────────────────── */
.file-preview-panel .mat-mdc-dialog-surface {
  border-radius: 14px !important;
  overflow: hidden !important;
  padding: 0 !important;
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1) !important;
  box-shadow:
    0 0 0 1px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04),
    0 24px 80px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.75),
    0 8px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55),
    0 2px 8px  rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4) !important;
  background: transparent !important;
}

.hover-card:hover{
  box-shadow: 4px 3px 9px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.65);
-webkit-box-shadow: 4px 3px 9px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.65);
-moz-box-shadow: 4px 3px 9px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.65);
transition: 0.3s ease;
}

.word-wrap{
  word-wrap: break-word;
}

// ── Lock icon tooltip ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
.mat-mdc-tooltip.sidenav-lock-tooltip .mdc-tooltip__surface {
  background: linear-gradient(150deg, #dc2626 0%, #991b1b 100%) !important;
  color: #ffffff !important;
  font-family: 'DM Sans', system-ui, sans-serif !important;
  font-size: 12.5px !important;
  font-weight: 500 !important;
  line-height: 1.6 !important;
  letter-spacing: 0.15px !important;
  padding: 10px 14px !important;
  border-radius: 8px !important;
  max-width: 230px !important;
  white-space: normal !important;
  text-align: left !important;
  border-top: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.18) !important;
  border-left: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.10) !important;
  box-shadow:
    0 0 0 1px rgba(220, 38, 38, 0.35),
    0 6px 24px rgba(153, 27, 27, 0.6),
    0 2px 8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45) !important;
}

// ── IR view: "Download IR report" tooltip ───────────────────────────────────
// CDK overlay tooltips render as a direct child of <body>, outside the wwell-ir-view
// component's own DOM subtree — so the scoped `mat.theme(...)` CSS custom properties
// defined on `.irv` (see wwell-ir-view.component.scss) never reach them by inheritance,
// and the tooltip falls back to unstyled text. Same fix as `.sidenav-lock-tooltip`
// above: style it directly, globally, keyed off `matTooltipClass`.
// Colours come from the `--wp-tooltip-*` tokens: a dark chip in light theme, an inverse (light) chip in
// dark theme, so the tooltip never disappears into the surface behind it.
.mat-mdc-tooltip.irv-download-tooltip .mdc-tooltip__surface {
  background: var(--wp-tooltip-bg) !important;
  color: var(--wp-tooltip-ink) !important;
  font-family: wp.$font-family !important;
  font-size: 12.5px !important;
  font-weight: 500 !important;
  line-height: 1.5 !important;
  letter-spacing: 0.1px !important;
  padding: 8px 12px !important;
  border-radius: 8px !important;
  max-width: 220px !important;
  white-space: normal !important;
  text-align: left !important;
  box-shadow:
    0 0 0 1px var(--wp-tooltip-ring),
    var(--wp-shadow-2) !important;
}

// ── IR attachment row actions: View / Upload / Replace / Delete ──────────────
// Same overlay problem as the two tooltips above — these render outside the editor component, so
// they have to be styled globally off `matTooltipClass` or they fall back to Material's default.
// Sized for one-word labels: nowrap and centred, rather than the wrapped paragraph treatment the
// sentence-length tooltips above need.
// `.wp-tooltip` is the generic one-word tooltip for the module (e.g. the theme toggle).
.mat-mdc-tooltip.ir-attachment-tooltip .mdc-tooltip__surface,
.mat-mdc-tooltip.wp-tooltip .mdc-tooltip__surface {
  background: var(--wp-tooltip-bg) !important;
  color: var(--wp-tooltip-ink) !important;
  font-family: wp.$font-family !important;
  font-size: 11.5px !important;
  font-weight: 600 !important;
  line-height: 1.4 !important;
  letter-spacing: 0.3px !important;
  padding: 6px 10px !important;
  border-radius: 6px !important;
  white-space: nowrap !important;
  text-align: center !important;
  box-shadow:
    0 0 0 1px var(--wp-tooltip-ring),
    var(--wp-shadow-2) !important;
}

// ── IR attachment viewer toolbar ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// Inverted on purpose: the viewer's chrome is near-black (#1b1e26), so the dark surface used above
// would sit almost invisibly on top of it. A light chip is the readable choice over that stage.
.mat-mdc-tooltip.ir-viewer-tooltip .mdc-tooltip__surface {
  background: #f1f5f9 !important;
  color: #0f172a !important;
  font-family: inherit !important;
  font-size: 11.5px !important;
  font-weight: 600 !important;
  line-height: 1.4 !important;
  letter-spacing: 0.3px !important;
  padding: 6px 10px !important;
  border-radius: 6px !important;
  white-space: nowrap !important;
  text-align: center !important;
  box-shadow: 0 6px 18px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45) !important;
}

.wells-value{
  background: #87ceeb;
  border-color: #38bdf8;
  color: #0f172a;
}

// Resizable expand dialog
.resizable-dialog .mat-mdc-dialog-container {
  resize: both;
  overflow: hidden;
  min-width: 480px;
  min-height: 320px;
}
.resizable-dialog .mat-mdc-dialog-surface {
  height: 100%;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  overflow: hidden;
}
```
